  1595	  router.post("/:id/resume", async (req, res, next) => {
  1596	    try {
  1597	      const updates = (req.body?.task_package ?? req.body ?? {}) as Partial<GeminxTaskPackage>;
  1598	      const parentRun = taskEngine.getRun(req.params.id);
  1599	      if (!parentRun) {
  1600	        res.status(404).json({ ok: false, error: "run not found", data: null });
  1601	        return;
  1602	      }
  1603	
  1604	      if (parentRun.protected_action_refused) {
  1605	        res.status(409).json(
  1606	          err(
  1607	            "resume blocked after a protected-action refusal; start a new explicitly authorised run instead of weakening the refused boundary"
  1608	          )
  1609	        );
  1610	        return;
  1611	      }
  1612	
  1613	      if (updates.protected_surfaces === undefined) {
  1614	        res.status(409).json(
  1615	          err(
  1616	            "resume requires an explicit protected_surfaces decision; omission cannot silently downgrade authority"
  1617	          )
  1618	        );
  1619	        return;
  1620	      }
  1621	
  1622	      if (updates.required_executables === undefined) {
  1623	        res.status(409).json(
  1624	          err(
  1625	            "resume requires an explicit required_executables decision; omission cannot silently downgrade runtime capability requirements"
  1626	          )
  1627	        );
  1628	        return;
  1629	      }
  1630	
  1631	      const capabilityCheck = taskCapabilityPreflight(updates);
  1632	      if (!capabilityCheck.valid) {
  1633	        res.status(400).json(err(capabilityCheck.error));
  1634	        return;
  1635	      }
  1636	      if (!capabilityCheck.preflight.ready) {
  1637	        res.status(409).json({
  1638	          ok: false,
  1639	          error: "runtime capability preflight failed before resume execution",
  1640	          data: { runtime_preflight: capabilityCheck.preflight }
  1641	        });
  1642	        return;
  1643	      }
  1644	
  1645	      const result = await taskEngine.resumeTask(req.params.id, updates);
  1646	      res.json(ok({ ...result, runtime_preflight: capabilityCheck.preflight }));
  1647	    } catch (error) {
  1648	      next(error);
  1649	    }
  1650	  });
  1651	
  1652	  router.post("/", async (req, res, next) => {
  1653	    try {
  1654	      const body = (req.body ?? {}) as CreateRunRequestBody;
  1655	      if (body.task_package) {
  1656	        const taskPackage = body.task_package as GeminxTaskPackage;
  1657	        const capabilityCheck = taskCapabilityPreflight(taskPackage);
  1658	        if (!capabilityCheck.valid) {
  1659	          res.status(400).json(err(capabilityCheck.error));
  1660	          return;
  1661	        }
  1662	        if (!capabilityCheck.preflight.ready) {
  1663	          res.status(409).json({
  1664	            ok: false,
  1665	            error: "runtime capability preflight failed before task execution",
  1666	            data: { runtime_preflight: capabilityCheck.preflight }
  1667	          });
  1668	          return;
  1669	        }
  1670	
  1671	        const result = await taskEngine.executeTaskPackage(taskPackage);
  1672	        res.json(ok({ ...result, runtime_preflight: capabilityCheck.preflight }));
  1673	        return;
  1674	      }
  1675	
  1676	      const creatorMode = readOptionalString(body.creator_input_mode);
  1677	      const gameTarget = readOptionalString(body.game_target);
  1678	      if (creatorMode === "kid_creator" && gameTarget === "roblox") {
  1679	        const creatorResult = await executeCreatorRobloxRun(body, req);
  1680	        res.status(creatorResult.status).json(creatorResult.payload);
  1681	        return;
  1682	      }
  1683	
  1684	      const intent = readOptionalString(body.intent);
  1685	      if (intent) {
  1686	        const inputMode = readOptionalString(body.input_mode) ?? "typed";
  1687	        const rawRequestedAgent = readOptionalString(body.agent_mode)?.toLowerCase() ?? "neo";
  1688	        const officeAgent: OfficeAgentId = isOfficeAgentId(rawRequestedAgent)
  1689	          ? rawRequestedAgent
  1690	          : normalizeOfficeAgentId(rawRequestedAgent, "neo");
  1691	        const isSpecialistOfficeAgent = ["brian", "mark", "kerry", "sophie", "ads"].includes(officeAgent);
  1692	        const companyScope = normalizeCompanyScope(
  1693	          readOptionalString((body as unknown as Record<string, unknown>).company_scope),
  1694	          officeAgent
  1695	        );
  1696	        const agentMode: "neo" | "ag" = readOptionalString(body.agent_mode)?.toLowerCase() === "ag" ? "ag" : "neo";
  1697	        // A Sophie conversation must never be accepted as another agent's turn.
  1698	        const requestedOfficeConversation = readOptionalString(body.conversation_id);
  1699	        if (isSpecialistOfficeAgent && requestedOfficeConversation &&
  1700	            !requestedOfficeConversation.startsWith(officeAgent + "-")) {
  1701	          res.status(409).json(err("OFFICE_CONVERSATION_IDENTITY_MISMATCH")); return;
  1702	        }
  1703	        // DS-NOVEL-03: Only a server-verified Founder session can authorise durable companion dispatch.
  1704	        // Never trust client-supplied body.founder_authorized or x-founder-auth header.
  1705	        const isFounderAuthorized = Boolean(req && hasValidFounderSession(req));
  1706	        const clientContext = resolveFounderClientContext(body.client_context, req);
  1707	        const requestMessageId = readOptionalString(body.message_id) ?? readOptionalString(body.client_message_id);
  1708	
  1709	        if (inputMode === "companion" && requestMessageId) {
  1710	          const durableJobId = durableFounderJobId(requestMessageId);
  1711	          const existingJob = backgroundCommsStore.getMessage(durableJobId) ?? backgroundCommsStore.getMessage(requestMessageId);
  1712	          if (existingJob && existingJob.type === "founder_autonomous_job") {
  1713	            const payload = existingJob.payload && typeof existingJob.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(existingJob.payload)
  1714	              ? existingJob.payload as Record<string, unknown>
  1715	              : {};
  1716	            const conversationId = readOptionalString(payload.conversation_id) ?? readOptionalString(body.conversation_id);
  1717	            if (isSpecialistOfficeAgent &&
  1718	                (String(payload.office_agent || "").toLowerCase() !== officeAgent ||
  1719	                 !String(conversationId || "").startsWith(officeAgent + "-"))) {
  1720	              res.status(409).json(err("OFFICE_IDEMPOTENT_JOB_IDENTITY_MISMATCH")); return;
  1721	            }
  1722	            const liveAck = existingJob.reply_payload?.message || existingJob.reply_payload?.summary || founderLiveAck(intent);
  1723	            const isTerminal = ["COMPLETED", "FAILED", "BLOCKED", "CANCELLED"].includes(existingJob.status);
  1724	            const routingReceipt = buildCompanionRoutingReceipt({
  1725	              intent,
  1726	              correlation_id: existingJob.correlation_id,
  1727	              conversation_id: conversationId,
  1728	              message_id: requestMessageId,
  1729	              chosen_route: "companion_background_job",
  1730	              fast_plan_action: null,
  1731	              read_only: existingJob.payload && typeof existingJob.payload === "object" && (existingJob.payload as any).read_only === true,
  1732	              durable_dispatched: true
  1733	            });
  1734	
  1735	            res.status(isTerminal ? 200 : 202).json(ok({
  1736	              route: "companion_background_job",
  1737	              agent_mode: officeAgent,
  1738	              office_agent: officeAgent,
  1739	              company_scope: companyScope,
  1740	              routing_receipt: routingReceipt,
  1741	              summary: isTerminal ? "Previous execution result returned idempotently." : "Existing durable task is running.",
  1742	              message: liveAck,
  1743	              live_ack: liveAck,
  1744	              provider: existingJob.reply_payload?.provider || "geminx-worker",
  1745	              model: existingJob.reply_payload?.model || (existingJob.payload as any)?.model_preference || "deepseek",
  1746	              correlation_id: existingJob.correlation_id,
  1747	              conversation_id: conversationId,
  1748	              active_run_id: existingJob.message_id,
  1749	              background_job_id: existingJob.message_id,
  1750	              background_status: existingJob.status,
  1751	              idempotent_admission: true,
  1752	              reply_payload: existingJob.reply_payload ?? null
  1753	            }));
  1754	            return;
  1755	          }
  1756	
  1757	          const requestedConvoId = readOptionalString(body.conversation_id);
  1758	          const existingConvo = requestedConvoId
  1759	            ? conversationStore.get(requestedConvoId)
  1760	            : conversationStore.findByRunId(requestMessageId);
  1761	          if (existingConvo && (!isSpecialistOfficeAgent ||
  1762	              existingConvo.conversation_id.startsWith(officeAgent + "-"))) {
  1763	            const userTurnIndex = existingConvo.turns.findIndex(
  1764	              (t) => t.role === "user" && (t.message_id === requestMessageId || t.client_message_id === requestMessageId)
  1765	            );
  1766	            if (userTurnIndex !== -1) {
  1767	              const assistantTurn = existingConvo.turns.slice(userTurnIndex + 1).find((t) => t.role === "assistant");
  1768	              if (assistantTurn) {
  1769	                const routingReceipt = buildCompanionRoutingReceipt({
  1770	                  intent,
  1771	                  correlation_id: readOptionalString(body.correlation_id) ?? `idempotent:${requestMessageId}`,
  1772	                  conversation_id: existingConvo.conversation_id,
  1773	                  message_id: requestMessageId,
  1774	                  chosen_route: "companion_idempotent_replay",
  1775	                  fast_plan_action: null,
  1776	                  read_only: true,
  1777	                  durable_dispatched: false
  1778	                });
  1779	                res.json(ok({
  1780	                  route: "companion_idempotent_replay",
  1781	                  agent_mode: officeAgent,
  1782	                  office_agent: officeAgent,
  1783	                  company_scope: companyScope,
  1784	                  routing_receipt: routingReceipt,
  1785	                  summary: "Previous response returned idempotently without repeating execution.",
  1786	                  message: assistantTurn.content,
  1787	                  provider: "geminx-continuity",
  1788	                  model: existingConvo.model_preference || "deepseek",
  1789	                  correlation_id: readOptionalString(body.correlation_id) ?? `idempotent:${requestMessageId}`,

  1791	                  active_run_id: existingConvo.active_run_id,
  1792	                  idempotent_admission: true
  1793	                }));
  1794	                return;
  1795	              }
  1796	            }
  1797	          }
  1798	        }

  1800	        // Cheap-first deterministic lane for exact simple commands. It bypasses the
  1801	        // model, never the canonical ToolRegistry. Compound/ambiguous requests fall through.

  1803	          inputMode === "companion"
  1804	          && officeAgent === "neo"
  1805	          && body.parallel_chat_only !== true
  1806	          && (!Array.isArray(body.attachments) || body.attachments.length === 0)
  1807	            ? classifyFastCompanionIntent(intent)
  1808	            : null;

  1811	          const correlationId = readOptionalString(body.correlation_id) ?? `fast:${randomUUID()}`;

  1816	          else conversationRecord = conversationStore.setModelPreference(conversationRecord, modelPreference);

  1820	            conversationRecord,
  1821	            {
  1822	              role: "user",
  1823	              ...(clientMessageId ? { message_id: clientMessageId, client_message_id: clientMessageId } : {}),
  1824	              content: intent,
  1825	              at: new Date().toISOString()
  1826	            },
  1827	            { domain: "desktop", summary: intent }
  1828	          );
  1829

  1831	
  1832	          conversationStore.appendTurn(
  1833	            conversationRecord,
  1834	            { role: "assistant", content: fast.message, at: new Date().toISOString() },
  1835	            { domain: "desktop", summary: intent }
  1836	          );

  1839	            intent,
  1840	            correlation_id: correlationId,
  1841	            conversation_id: conversationRecord.conversation_id,
  1842	            message_id: clientMessageId ?? null,
  1843	            chosen_route: "companion_fast_intent",
  1844	            fast_plan_action: fast.action,
  1845	            read_only: false,
  1846	            durable_dispatched: false
  1847	          });

  2263	              level: auditResult.shouldTakeover ? "warn" : "info",
  2264	              event: auditResult.shouldTakeover ? "sentinel_takeover_triggered" : "turn_evaluated",
  2265	              details: {

  2309	                  evidence: [`audit_reason=${auditResult.reason ?? "UNKNOWN"}; recorded_tool_calls=${reply.tool_calls?.length ?? 0}`],
  2310	                  recommended_action: "Inspect actual tool receipts for this chat, repair the verified error with the existing governed execution lane, report a mechanical result before closing.",
  2311	                });
  2312	                agIncidentId = incident.incident_id;
  2313	              } catch {
  2314	                // Never convert a repair-queue write failure into fake takeover proof.
  2315	              }
  2316	            }
  2317	
  2318	            if (auditResult.shouldTakeover) {

  2326	                auditResult

  2328	
  2329	              // The interceptor records a diagnostic, not a model execution.
  2330	              // Preserve the model/provider that really answered this turn.
  2331	              finalReply = reply;
  2332

  2419	            );
  2420	          } catch (error) {
  2421	            const convoId = readOptionalString(body.conversation_id);
  2422	            const activeRunId = readOptionalString(body.active_run_id);
  2423	            const providerFailure = error instanceof AstraCompanionError || error instanceof DeepSeekCompanionError;
  2424	            const statusCode = providerFailure ? error.statusCode : 500;
  2425	            // Every office agent uses this same chat entry point. Route genuine
  2426	            // backend failures to the existing AG incident/Comms ledger, never
  2427	            // infer repair success from a model reply or the visible transcript.

  2441	              } catch {
  2442	                // An inaccessible ledger must not replace the real chat error.
  2443	              }
  2444	            }
  2445	            if (error instanceof AstraCompanionError || error instanceof DeepSeekCompanionError) {
  2446	              res.status(error.statusCode).json({
  2447	                ok: false,
  2448	                error: error.publicMessage,
  2449	                data: {
  2450	                  conversation_id: convoId,
  2451	                  active_run_id: activeRunId
  2452	                }
  2453	              });
  2454	              return;
  2455	            }
  2456	
  2457	            res.status(500).json({
  2458	              ok: false,
  2459	              error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
  2460	              data: {
  2461	                conversation_id: convoId,
  2462	                active_run_id: activeRunId
  2463	              }
  2464	            });
  2465	            return;
  2466	          }
  2467	
  2468	          return;
  2469	        }
  2470	      }
  2471	
  2472	      res.status(501).json(err("not implemented"));
  2473	    } catch (error) {
  2474	      next(error);
  2475	    }
  2476	  });
  2477	
  2478	  router.post("/lifecycle-flush", (req, res) => {
  2479	    const body = req.body && typeof req.body === "object" && !Array.isArray(req.body)
  2480	      ? req.body as Record<string, unknown>
  2481	      : {};
  2482	    const rawAgent = String(body.selected_agent ?? body.agent_mode ?? "GeminX").trim();
  2483	    const selectedAgent: "GeminX" | "AG" | "NEO" =
  2484	      rawAgent.toUpperCase() === "AG" ? "AG" : rawAgent.toUpperCase() === "NEO" ? "NEO" : "GeminX";
  2485	    const snapshot = persistLifecycleFlushSnapshot({
  2486	      reason: ["visibilitychange", "pagehide", "beforeunload", "turn_commit", "step_commit"].includes(String(body.reason ?? ""))
  2487	        ? (String(body.reason) as "visibilitychange" | "pagehide" | "beforeunload" | "turn_commit" | "step_commit")
  2488	        : "visibilitychange",
  2489	      conversation_id: readOptionalString(body.conversation_id),
  2490	      running_job_id: readOptionalString(body.running_job_id ?? body.active_run_id),
  2491	      selected_agent: selectedAgent,
  2492	      composer_draft: String(body.composer_draft ?? "").slice(0, 12_000),
  2493	      active_scratchpad: String(body.active_scratchpad ?? "").slice(0, 12_000),
  2494	      client_viewport: body.client_viewport ?? null
  2495	    });
  2496	    res.status(200).json(ok(snapshot));
  2497	  });
  2498	
  2499	  router.get("/lifecycle-flush", (req, res) => {
  2500	    const conversationId = readOptionalString(req.query?.conversation_id);
  2501	    const snapshot = readLatestLifecycleFlushSnapshot(conversationId);
  2502	    res.status(200).json(ok({ snapshot }));
  2503	  });
  2504	
  2505	  return router;
  2506	}

    90	/**
    91	 * A completed native provider transcript is a continuation checkpoint, not
    92	 * permission to replay previously executed tools. Any unresolved tool_call_id,
    93	 * mismatched objective or pending mutation refuses automatic reentry.
    94	 * Covers read-only and receipt-confirmed mixed workflows such as AG browser QA.
    95	 */
    96	export function canResumeNoReplyFromNativeCheckpoint(
    97	  checkpoint: ExecutionTranscriptCheckpoint | null,
    98	  originalObjective: string
    99	): boolean {
   100	  if (!checkpoint || checkpoint.state !== "CLEAN" || checkpoint.pending_tool_id !== null ||
   101	      checkpoint.intent_sha256 !== ConversationStore.intentSha256(originalObjective)) return false;
   102	  const hasObservedTool = checkpoint.messages.some(row => row.role === "tool" && Boolean(row.tool_call_id)) ||
   103	    Boolean(checkpoint.archived_execution_evidence?.length);
   104	  return hasObservedTool && unresolvedNativeToolCallIds(checkpoint.messages).length === 0;
   105	}
   106	
   107	export interface AutonomousRecoveryTruth {
   108	  interruption_detected: boolean;
   109	  same_job_recovered: boolean;
   110	  evidence: string | null;
   111	}
   112	
   113	export function autonomousRecoveryTruth(
   114	  message: Pick<GeminxMessage, "attempt_count" | "activity"> & { payload?: unknown }
   115	): AutonomousRecoveryTruth {
   116	  const currentAttempt = Math.max(1, Number(message.attempt_count ?? 1));
   117	  const expectedChallenge = `resume-attempt:${currentAttempt}`;
   118	  const activity = Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : [];
   119	  const restartEvent = [...activity].reverse().find(item =>
   120	    item.phase === "restart-running-recovery" &&
   121	    item.tool === "REQUEUED_SAME_WORK_ITEM" &&
   122	    item.challenge === expectedChallenge
   123	  );
   124	  const resumeEvent = [...activity].reverse().find(item =>
   125	    item.phase === "resume" && (item.label?.includes("resumed") || item.status === "running")
   126	  );
   127	  const payload = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown> : null;
   128	  const isResumedSameObjective = Boolean(payload?.resume_same_objective || resumeEvent);
   129	
   130	  const event = restartEvent || (isResumedSameObjective ? resumeEvent : null);
   131	
   132	  return {
   133	    interruption_detected: Boolean(event),
   134	    same_job_recovered: Boolean(event),
   135	    evidence: event
   136	      ? [event.event_id, event.at, event.url ?? (resumeEvent ? "founder-resume-endpoint" : "")].filter(Boolean).join("|")
   137	      : null,
   138	  };
   139	}
   140	
   141	export function normalizeAutonomousRecoveryClaims(
   142	  value: string,
   143	  truth: AutonomousRecoveryTruth
   144	): string {
   145	  const expected = truth.same_job_recovered ? "YES" : "NO";
   146	  return String(value ?? "")
   147	    .replace(/(INTERRUPTION_DETECTED\s*:\s*)(?:YES|NO)\b/gi, `$1${expected}`)
   148	    .replace(/(SAME_JOB_RECOVERED\s*:\s*)(?:YES|NO)\b/gi, `$1${expected}`);
   149	}

   153	}

   155	function intervalMs(): number {
   156	  const raw = Number(env("GEMINX_INBOX_WORKER_POLL_MS"));
   157	  return Number.isFinite(raw) && raw >= 1_000 ? Math.floor(raw) : DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS;
   158	}

   160	function heartbeatMs(): number {
   161	  const raw = Number(env("GEMINX_INBOX_HEARTBEAT_MS"));
   162	  return Number.isFinite(raw) && raw >= 2_000 ? Math.floor(raw) : DEFAULT_HEARTBEAT_MS;
   163	}

   165	export function maxConcurrentJobs(rawValue = env("GEMINX_INBOX_MAX_CONCURRENT_JOBS")): number {
   166	  const raw = Number(rawValue);
   167	  return Number.isFinite(raw) && raw >= 1
   168	    ? Math.min(HARD_MAX_CONCURRENT_JOBS, Math.floor(raw))
   169	    : DEFAULT_MAX_CONCURRENT_JOBS;
   170	}
   171	
   172	export function geminxInboxPriorityRank(value: string): number {
   173	  const priority = String(value ?? "").trim().toUpperCase();
   174	  if (priority === "P0") return 0;
   175	  if (priority === "CRITICAL") return 1;
   176	  if (priority === "P1" || priority === "HIGH") return 2;
   177	  return 3;
   178	}
   179	
   180	export function compareGeminxInboxQueue(
   181	  a: Pick<GeminxMessage, "priority" | "attempt_count" | "created_at">,
   182	  b: Pick<GeminxMessage, "priority" | "attempt_count" | "created_at">
   183	): number {
   184	  const priorityDelta = geminxInboxPriorityRank(a.priority) - geminxInboxPriorityRank(b.priority);
   185	  if (priorityDelta !== 0) return priorityDelta;
   186	
   187	  // Recovered retry storms must not starve fresh Founder work at the same priority.
   188	  // A new P0 gets a chance before a P0 that has already failed 15, 50 or 200 times.
   189	  const attemptDelta = Math.max(0, Number(a.attempt_count ?? 0)) - Math.max(0, Number(b.attempt_count ?? 0));
   190	  if (attemptDelta !== 0) return attemptDelta;
   191	
   192	  return a.created_at.localeCompare(b.created_at);
   193	}
   194	
   195	function dueForAttempt(message: GeminxMessage, now = Date.now()): boolean {
   196	  if (!message.next_attempt_at) return true;
   197	  const parsed = Date.parse(message.next_attempt_at);
   198	  return Number.isNaN(parsed) || parsed <= now;
   199	}
   200	
   201	export function ambiguousProviderOutcome(error: unknown): boolean {
   202	  const detail = error instanceof Error
   203	    ? [error.name, error.message, (error as Error & { publicMessage?: unknown }).publicMessage ?? ""].filter(Boolean).join(" ")
   204	    : String(error);
   205	  const statusCode = (error && typeof error === "object" && "statusCode" in error && typeof (error as { statusCode?: unknown }).statusCode === "number")
   206	    ? (error as { statusCode: number }).statusCode
   207	    : undefined;
   208	
   209	  if (/AGX_HARNESS_(?:NOT_CONFIGURED|MCP_NOT_VERIFIED)/i.test(detail)) return false;
   210	  // If an interaction is already in-progress, incomplete, or pending, a 429 received subsequently
   211	  // cannot rule out prior provider acceptance or side effects; it must remain ambiguous.
   212	  const hasPendingInteractionOrSideEffect = /AGX_HARNESS_NOT_COMPLETED|AGX_HARNESS_AMBIGUOUS_PROVIDER_OUTCOME|Google managed Antigravity did not complete|GEMINX_GEMINI_NO_REPLY_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED|GEMINX_MANAGED_INTERACTION_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED|GEMINX_MUTATION_OUTCOME_UNCERTAIN_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED|interaction status:\s*(?:in_progress|incomplete|requires_action|pending)/i.test(detail);
   213	  if (hasPendingInteractionOrSideEffect) return true;
   214	  // A clean pre-execution rate limit without pending interaction status is safe to retry with backoff.
   215	  if (statusCode === 429 || /HTTP 429|rate limit|too many requests|quota exhausted|resource_exhausted/i.test(detail)) {
   216	    return false;
   217	  }
   218	  return /DEEPSEEK_AMBIGUOUS_NETWORK_FAILURE_NO_AUTORETRY|DeepSeek returned HTTP 5\d\d|OpenAI returned HTTP 5\d\d|Astra request failed[\s\S]*(?:timeout|timed out|network|socket|5\d\d)|Google Gemini did not return a usable answer|Google returned HTTP 5\d\d|Antigravity returned HTTP 5\d\d/i.test(detail);
   219	}
   220	
   221	export function transientExecutionError(error: unknown): boolean {
   222	  const detail = error instanceof Error
   223	    ? [error.name, error.message, (error as Error & { cause?: unknown }).cause instanceof Error ? (error as Error & { cause?: Error }).cause?.message : ""].filter(Boolean).join(" ")
   224	    : String(error);
   225	  const statusCode = (error && typeof error === "object" && "statusCode" in error && typeof (error as { statusCode?: unknown }).statusCode === "number")
   226	    ? (error as { statusCode: number }).statusCode
   227	    : undefined;
   228	
   229	  // Local spend, schema, and capacity faults are never provider HTTP 429. Retrying these
   230	  // from the outer inbox sweep would reset the paid companion run.
   231	  if (/DEEPSEEK_(?:TASK_SPEND_BUDGET_REACHED|OBJECTIVE_LIFETIME_BUDGET_REACHED|PROVIDER_REQUEST_BUDGET_REACHED|OBJECTIVE_LEDGER_LOCK_UNAVAILABLE|OBJECTIVE_VELOCITY_FUSE_TRIPPED)|GEMINX_(?:WORKFLOW_SCHEMA_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED|CONTEXT_PRESSURE_PREFLIGHT)/i.test(detail)) return false;
   232	
   233	  // Ambiguous provider outcomes (uncertain mutations, pending/incomplete interactions) must fail closed.
   234	  if (ambiguousProviderOutcome(error)) return false;
   235	
   236	  // Internal model-finalisation HTTP 502 failures and uncompleted harness states must not be treated as generic network errors.
   237	  // Note: Do not globally exclude AntigravityCompanionError by class name; classify using structured status code and outcome evidence.
   238	  if (/MODEL_DID_NOT_PRODUCE_A_USABLE_REPLY|final_synthesis_unavailable|Model reply could not be generated|ASTRA_EMPTY_CHAT_OUTPUT|DEEPSEEK_FLASH_502_PRO_HELD|Unexpected tool-loop exit|AGX_HARNESS_(?:NOT_COMPLETED|NOT_CONFIGURED|MCP_NOT_VERIFIED)|GEMINX_(?:MODEL_NO_REPLY_NATIVE_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED|MANAGED_INTERACTION_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED|PROVIDER_OUTCOME_AMBIGUOUS|NATIVE_TRANSCRIPT_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED|INTERNAL_CONTEXT_PRESSURE|INTERNAL_FINITE_BUDGET|MUTATION_OUTCOME_UNCERTAIN_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED|GEMINI_NO_REPLY_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED)|BLOCKED_TURN_0_UNBOUND_CONNECTOR/i.test(detail)) {
   239	    return false;
   240	  }
   241	
   242	  // Structured HTTP status codes
   243	  if (statusCode === 429 || statusCode === 503 || statusCode === 504) {
   244	    return true;
   245	  }
   246	
   247	  return /timeout|timed out|timedout|etimedout|aborted|failed to fetch|fetch failed|network|econnreset|econnrefused|enotfound|socket|429|502|503|504|temporar|rate limit|overloaded/i.test(detail);
   248	}

   250	function astraCreditExhausted(error: unknown): boolean {
   251	  const detail = error instanceof Error
   252	    ? [error.name, error.message, (error as Error & { publicMessage?: unknown }).publicMessage ?? ""].join(" ")
   253	    : String(error);
   254	  return /ASTRA_PROVIDER_CREDIT_EXHAUSTED|credit_balance_exhausted|insufficient_quota|Astra credits are exhausted/i.test(detail);
   255	}

   257	export function deepSeekCreditExhausted(error: unknown): boolean {
   258	  const detail = error instanceof Error
   259	    ? [error.name, error.message, (error as Error & { publicMessage?: unknown }).publicMessage ?? ""].join(" ")
   260	    : String(error);

   264	/** Eligibility only: a provider refusal is safe to reroute when no tool execution
   265	 * might need reconciliation. The actual same-job switch is persisted below. */
   266	export function planDeepSeekGoogleFailover(input: {
   267	  failure: string;
   268	  googleConfigured: boolean;
   269	  selectedDeepSeek: boolean;
   270	  googleAlreadyAttempted: boolean;
   271	  toolReceipts: number;
   272	  checkpointToolMessages: number;
   273	  unresolvedToolCalls: number;
   274	}): "NOT_ELIGIBLE" | "GOOGLE_UNAVAILABLE" | "RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED" | "REROUTE_GOOGLE" {
   275	  if (!input.selectedDeepSeek || input.googleAlreadyAttempted ||
   276	      !/DeepSeek returned HTTP (?:402|429)|DEEPSEEK_PROVIDER_CREDIT_EXHAUSTED|Insufficient Balance/i.test(input.failure)) {
   277	    return "NOT_ELIGIBLE";
   278	  }
   279	  if (input.toolReceipts > 0 || input.checkpointToolMessages > 0 || input.unresolvedToolCalls > 0) {
   280	    return "RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED";
   281	  }
   282	  return input.googleConfigured ? "REROUTE_GOOGLE" : "GOOGLE_UNAVAILABLE";
   283	}

   299	export function geminxAutonomousRetryLimit(rawValue = env("GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_MAX_RETRY_ATTEMPTS")): number {
   300	  const parsed = Number(rawValue);
   301	  return Number.isFinite(parsed) && parsed >= 1
   302	    ? Math.min(3, Math.floor(parsed))
   303	    : DEFAULT_MAX_AUTONOMOUS_RETRY_ATTEMPTS;
   304	}

   306	export function geminxRetryCeilingReached(
   307	  attemptCount: number,
   308	  rawValue?: string
   309	): boolean {
   310	  return Math.max(0, Number(attemptCount ?? 0)) >= geminxAutonomousRetryLimit(rawValue);
   311	}

   313	type RetryCircuitProgressState = {
   314	  fingerprint: string | null;
   315	  consecutive_same_failure: number;
   316	  turns_without_progress: number;
   317	  total_objective_turns: number;
   318	  failure_occurrences: Record<string, number>;
   319	  strategy_change_count: number;
   320	  provider_transient_failures: number;
   321	  provider_transient_fingerprint: string | null;
   322	  observation_evidence_keys: string[];
   323	  recorded_attempt: number | null;
   324	  observed_at: string;
   325	};
   326	
   327	type RetryFailureControl = {
   328	  fingerprint: string;
   329	  consecutive_same_failure: number;
   330	  cumulative_failure_occurrences: number;
   331	  turns_without_progress: number;
   332	  total_objective_turns: number;
   333	};
   334	
   335	export function geminxObjectiveTurnLimit(rawValue = env("GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_MAX_OBJECTIVE_TURNS")): number {
   336	  const parsed = Number(rawValue);
   337	  if (!Number.isFinite(parsed) || parsed < 1) return DEFAULT_MAX_AUTONOMOUS_OBJECTIVE_TURNS;
   338	  // Historical production values such as 3, 6 or 16 represented the old
   339	  // semantic-turn guillotine. Never let those stale values recreate it.
   340	  return Math.max(
   341	    DEFAULT_MAX_AUTONOMOUS_OBJECTIVE_TURNS,
   342	    Math.min(HARD_MAX_AUTONOMOUS_OBJECTIVE_TURNS, Math.floor(parsed))
   343	  );
   344	}
   345	
   346	export function geminxObjectiveTurnLimitForCommand(
   347	  _command: Pick<GeminxAutonomousTaskCommand, "objective" | "read_only">,
   348	  rawValue = env("GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_MAX_OBJECTIVE_TURNS")
   349	): number {
   350	  // Read-only/status work is not cheaper in *steps* merely because it cannot
   351	  // mutate. It often needs more observations. All objectives share the same
   352	  // emergency run-loop ceiling; normal stopping is governed by verified
   353	  // completion, real boundaries, spend/context limits and no-progress circuits.
   354	  return geminxObjectiveTurnLimit(rawValue);
   355	}
   356	
   357	export function geminxNoProgressTurnLimit(rawValue = env("GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_MAX_TURNS_WITHOUT_PROGRESS")): number {
   358	  const parsed = Number(rawValue);
   359	  if (!Number.isFinite(parsed) || parsed < 1) return DEFAULT_MAX_TURNS_WITHOUT_PROGRESS;
   360	  // Old production values such as 6 were from the pre-NEO-parity circuit.
   361	  // Never let stale env recreate the six-turn guillotine.
   362	  return Math.max(
   363	    DEFAULT_MAX_TURNS_WITHOUT_PROGRESS,
   364	    Math.min(HARD_MAX_TURNS_WITHOUT_PROGRESS, Math.floor(parsed))
   365	  );
   366	}
   367	
   368	export function geminxCumulativeFailureLimit(rawValue = env("GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_MAX_FAILURE_OCCURRENCES")): number {
   369	  const parsed = Number(rawValue);
   370	  if (!Number.isFinite(parsed) || parsed < 1) return DEFAULT_MAX_CUMULATIVE_FAILURE_OCCURRENCES;
   371	  return Math.max(
   372	    DEFAULT_MAX_CUMULATIVE_FAILURE_OCCURRENCES,
   373	    Math.min(HARD_MAX_CUMULATIVE_FAILURE_OCCURRENCES, Math.floor(parsed))
   374	  );
   375	}
   376	
   377	export function geminxStrategyChangeLimit(): number {
   378	  return DEFAULT_MAX_STRATEGY_CHANGES;
   379	}
   380	
   381	function retryCircuitProgressState(message: Pick<GeminxMessage, "payload" | "attempt_count">): RetryCircuitProgressState | null {
   382	  const payload = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
   383	    ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown>
   384	    : {};
   385	  const verified = payload.verified_current_state && typeof payload.verified_current_state === "object" && !Array.isArray(payload.verified_current_state)
   386	    ? payload.verified_current_state as Record<string, unknown>
   387	    : {};
   388	  const state = verified.retry_circuit && typeof verified.retry_circuit === "object" && !Array.isArray(verified.retry_circuit)
   389	    ? verified.retry_circuit as Record<string, unknown>
   390	    : null;
   391	  if (!state) return null;
   392	  const rawOccurrences =
   393	    state.failure_occurrences && typeof state.failure_occurrences === "object" && !Array.isArray(state.failure_occurrences)
   394	      ? state.failure_occurrences as Record<string, unknown>
   395	      : {};
   396	  const failureOccurrences: Record<string, number> = {};
   397	  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(rawOccurrences).slice(-32)) {
   398	    const count = Math.max(0, Number(value ?? 0) || 0);
   399	    if (key.trim() && count > 0) failureOccurrences[key] = count;
   400	  }
   401	  const fingerprint = typeof state.fingerprint === "string" && state.fingerprint.trim()
   402	    ? state.fingerprint.trim()
   403	    : null;
   404	  return {
   405	    fingerprint,
   406	    consecutive_same_failure: Math.max(0, Number(state.consecutive_same_failure ?? 0) || 0),
   407	    turns_without_progress: Math.max(0, Number(state.turns_without_progress ?? 0) || 0),
   408	    total_objective_turns: Math.max(0, Number(state.total_objective_turns ?? 0) || 0),
   409	    failure_occurrences: failureOccurrences,
   410	    strategy_change_count: Math.max(0, Number(state.strategy_change_count ?? 0) || 0),
   411	    provider_transient_failures: Math.max(0, Number(state.provider_transient_failures ?? 0) || 0),
   412	    provider_transient_fingerprint:
   413	      typeof state.provider_transient_fingerprint === "string" && state.provider_transient_fingerprint.trim()
   414	        ? state.provider_transient_fingerprint.trim()
   415	        : null,
   416	    observation_evidence_keys: Array.isArray(state.observation_evidence_keys)
   417	      ? state.observation_evidence_keys.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string" && item.length > 0).slice(-128)
   418	      : [],
   419	    recorded_attempt: Number.isFinite(Number(state.recorded_attempt)) ? Number(state.recorded_attempt) : null,
   420	    observed_at: String(state.observed_at ?? ""),
   421	  };
   422	}
   423	
   424	export function geminxRetryCircuitCount(message: Pick<GeminxMessage, "payload" | "attempt_count">): number {
   425	  const progress = retryCircuitProgressState(message);
   426	  // Legacy messages predate failure-fingerprint accounting. Falling back to the
   427	  // cumulative attempt count preserves the old safety boundary until a repaired
   428	  // deployment explicitly migrates that same work item.
   429	  return progress ? progress.consecutive_same_failure : Math.max(0, Number(message.attempt_count ?? 0));
   430	}

   432	export function recordObjectiveExecutionTurn(message: GeminxMessage): number {
   433	  const payload = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
   434	    ? { ...(message.payload as Record<string, unknown>) }
   435	    : {};
   436	  const verified = payload.verified_current_state && typeof payload.verified_current_state === "object" && !Array.isArray(payload.verified_current_state)
   437	    ? { ...(payload.verified_current_state as Record<string, unknown>) }
   438	    : {};
   439	  const previous = verified.retry_circuit && typeof verified.retry_circuit === "object" && !Array.isArray(verified.retry_circuit)
   440	    ? { ...(verified.retry_circuit as Record<string, unknown>) }
   441	    : {};
   442	  const total = Math.max(0, Number(previous.total_objective_turns ?? 0) || 0) + 1;
   443	  verified.retry_circuit = {
   444	    ...previous,
   445	    fingerprint: typeof previous.fingerprint === "string" ? previous.fingerprint : null,
   446	    consecutive_same_failure: Math.max(0, Number(previous.consecutive_same_failure ?? 0) || 0),
   447	    turns_without_progress: Math.max(0, Number(previous.turns_without_progress ?? 0) || 0),
   448	    total_objective_turns: total,
   449	    failure_occurrences:
   450	      previous.failure_occurrences && typeof previous.failure_occurrences === "object" && !Array.isArray(previous.failure_occurrences)
   451	        ? previous.failure_occurrences
   452	        : {},
   453	    provider_transient_failures: 0,
   454	    provider_transient_fingerprint: null,
   455	    spend_reservation_contention_failures: 0,
   456	    spend_reservation_contention_fingerprint: null,
   457	    observation_evidence_keys: Array.isArray(previous.observation_evidence_keys)
   458	      ? previous.observation_evidence_keys.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string" && item.length > 0).slice(-128)
   459	      : [],
   460	    recorded_attempt: Number.isFinite(Number(previous.recorded_attempt)) ? Number(previous.recorded_attempt) : null,
   461	    observed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   462	  };
   463	  payload.verified_current_state = verified;
   464	  message.payload = payload;
   465	  return total;
   466	}

   468	export function recordRetryCircuitMaterialProgress(message: GeminxMessage): void {
   469	  const payload = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
   470	    ? { ...(message.payload as Record<string, unknown>) }
   471	    : {};
   472	  const verified = payload.verified_current_state && typeof payload.verified_current_state === "object" && !Array.isArray(payload.verified_current_state)
   473	    ? { ...(payload.verified_current_state as Record<string, unknown>) }
   474	    : {};
   475	  const previous = verified.retry_circuit && typeof verified.retry_circuit === "object" && !Array.isArray(verified.retry_circuit)
   476	    ? { ...(verified.retry_circuit as Record<string, unknown>) }
   477	    : {};
   478	  verified.retry_circuit = {
   479	    ...previous,
   480	    fingerprint: null,
   481	    consecutive_same_failure: 0,
   482	    turns_without_progress: 0,
   483	    strategy_change_count: 0,
   484	    strategy_change_required: false,
   485	    total_objective_turns: Math.max(0, Number(previous.total_objective_turns ?? 0) || 0),
   486	    failure_occurrences:
   487	      previous.failure_occurrences && typeof previous.failure_occurrences === "object" && !Array.isArray(previous.failure_occurrences)
   488	        ? previous.failure_occurrences
   489	        : {},
   490	    provider_transient_failures: Math.max(0, Number(previous.provider_transient_failures ?? 0) || 0),
   491	    provider_transient_fingerprint:
   492	      typeof previous.provider_transient_fingerprint === "string" ? previous.provider_transient_fingerprint : null,
   493	    observation_evidence_keys: Array.isArray(previous.observation_evidence_keys)
   494	      ? previous.observation_evidence_keys.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string" && item.length > 0).slice(-128)
   495	      : [],
   496	    recorded_attempt: null,
   497	    observed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   498	  };
   499	  payload.verified_current_state = verified;
   500	  message.payload = payload;
   501	}

   503	export function recordSpendReservationContention(message: GeminxMessage, detail: string): number {
   504	  const payload = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
   505	    ? { ...(message.payload as Record<string, unknown>) }
   506	    : {};
   507	  const verified = payload.verified_current_state && typeof payload.verified_current_state === "object" && !Array.isArray(payload.verified_current_state)
   508	    ? { ...(payload.verified_current_state as Record<string, unknown>) }
   509	    : {};
   510	  const previous = verified.retry_circuit && typeof verified.retry_circuit === "object" && !Array.isArray(verified.retry_circuit)
   511	    ? { ...(verified.retry_circuit as Record<string, unknown>) }
   512	    : {};
   513	  // Reservation dollar amounts are ephemeral concurrency state. They must not
   514	  // let repeated admission contention evade the bounded circuit by producing a
   515	  // different fingerprint every time.
   516	  const fingerprint = createHash("sha256")
   517	    .update("DEEPSEEK_DAILY_SPEND_RESERVATION_BUSY")
   518	    .digest("hex")
   519	    .slice(0, 24);
   520	  const same = String(previous.spend_reservation_contention_fingerprint ?? "") === fingerprint;
   521	  const count = same
   522	    ? Math.max(0, Number(previous.spend_reservation_contention_failures ?? 0) || 0) + 1
   523	    : 1;
   524	  verified.retry_circuit = {
   525	    ...previous,
   526	    spend_reservation_contention_failures: count,
   527	    spend_reservation_contention_fingerprint: fingerprint,
   528	    observed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   529	  };
   530	  payload.verified_current_state = verified;
   531	  message.payload = payload;
   532	  return count;
   533	}
   534	
   535	export function recordProviderTransientFailure(message: GeminxMessage, detail: string): number {
   536	  const payload = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
   537	    ? { ...(message.payload as Record<string, unknown>) }
   538	    : {};
   539	  const verified = payload.verified_current_state && typeof payload.verified_current_state === "object" && !Array.isArray(payload.verified_current_state)
   540	    ? { ...(payload.verified_current_state as Record<string, unknown>) }
   541	    : {};
   542	  const previous = verified.retry_circuit && typeof verified.retry_circuit === "object" && !Array.isArray(verified.retry_circuit)
   543	    ? { ...(verified.retry_circuit as Record<string, unknown>) }
   544	    : {};
   545	  const fingerprint = createHash("sha256")
   546	    .update(normalizeFailureFingerprintText(detail))
   547	    .digest("hex")
   548	    .slice(0, 24);
   549	  const sameProviderFailure = String(previous.provider_transient_fingerprint ?? "") === fingerprint;
   550	  const count = sameProviderFailure
   551	    ? Math.max(0, Number(previous.provider_transient_failures ?? 0) || 0) + 1
   552	    : 1;
   553	  verified.retry_circuit = {
   554	    ...previous,
   555	    fingerprint: typeof previous.fingerprint === "string" ? previous.fingerprint : null,
   556	    consecutive_same_failure: Math.max(0, Number(previous.consecutive_same_failure ?? 0) || 0),
   557	    turns_without_progress: Math.max(0, Number(previous.turns_without_progress ?? 0) || 0),
   558	    total_objective_turns: Math.max(0, Number(previous.total_objective_turns ?? 0) || 0),
   559	    failure_occurrences:
   560	      previous.failure_occurrences && typeof previous.failure_occurrences === "object" && !Array.isArray(previous.failure_occurrences)
   561	        ? previous.failure_occurrences
   562	        : {},
   563	    provider_transient_failures: count,
   564	    provider_transient_fingerprint: fingerprint,
   565	    observation_evidence_keys: Array.isArray(previous.observation_evidence_keys)
   566	      ? previous.observation_evidence_keys.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string" && item.length > 0).slice(-128)
   567	      : [],
   568	    recorded_attempt: Number.isFinite(Number(previous.recorded_attempt)) ? Number(previous.recorded_attempt) : null,
   569	    observed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   570	  };
   571	  payload.verified_current_state = verified;
   572	  message.payload = payload;
   573	  return count;
   574	}
   575	
   576	export function recordRetryCircuitFailure(
   577	  message: GeminxMessage,
   578	  detail: string,
   579	  fingerprintOverride?: string
   580	): RetryFailureControl {
   581	  const payload = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
   582	    ? { ...(message.payload as Record<string, unknown>) }
   583	    : {};
   584	  const verified = payload.verified_current_state && typeof payload.verified_current_state === "object" && !Array.isArray(payload.verified_current_state)
   585	    ? { ...(payload.verified_current_state as Record<string, unknown>) }
   586	    : {};
   587	  const previous = verified.retry_circuit && typeof verified.retry_circuit === "object" && !Array.isArray(verified.retry_circuit)
   588	    ? { ...(verified.retry_circuit as Record<string, unknown>) }
   589	    : {};
   590	  const fingerprint = String(fingerprintOverride ?? "").trim() || createHash("sha256")
   591	    .update(JSON.stringify({
   592	      failure: normalizeFailureFingerprintText(detail),
   593	    }))
   594	    .digest("hex")
   595	    .slice(0, 24);
   596	  const attempt = Math.max(0, Number(message.attempt_count ?? 0));
   597	  const priorOccurrences =
   598	    previous.failure_occurrences && typeof previous.failure_occurrences === "object" && !Array.isArray(previous.failure_occurrences)
   599	      ? { ...(previous.failure_occurrences as Record<string, number>) }
   600	      : {};
   601	  const alreadyRecorded =
   602	    Number(previous.recorded_attempt ?? Number.NaN) === attempt &&
   603	    String(previous.fingerprint ?? "") === fingerprint;
   604	  const sameFailure = String(previous.fingerprint ?? "") === fingerprint;
   605	  const consecutive = alreadyRecorded
   606	    ? Math.max(0, Number(previous.consecutive_same_failure ?? 0) || 0)
   607	    : sameFailure
   608	      ? Math.max(0, Number(previous.consecutive_same_failure ?? 0) || 0) + 1
   609	      : 1;
   610	  const cumulative = alreadyRecorded
   611	    ? Math.max(0, Number(priorOccurrences[fingerprint] ?? 0) || 0)
   612	    : Math.max(0, Number(priorOccurrences[fingerprint] ?? 0) || 0) + 1;
   613	  const turnsWithoutProgress = alreadyRecorded
   614	    ? Math.max(0, Number(previous.turns_without_progress ?? 0) || 0)
   615	    : Math.max(0, Number(previous.turns_without_progress ?? 0) || 0) + 1;
   616	  priorOccurrences[fingerprint] = cumulative;
   617	  const totalTurns = Math.max(0, Number(previous.total_objective_turns ?? 0) || 0);
   618	  verified.retry_circuit = {
   619	    ...previous,
   620	    fingerprint,
   621	    consecutive_same_failure: consecutive,
   622	    turns_without_progress: turnsWithoutProgress,
   623	    total_objective_turns: totalTurns,
   624	    failure_occurrences: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(priorOccurrences).slice(-32)),
   625	    recorded_attempt: attempt,
   626	    observed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   627	  };
   628	  payload.verified_current_state = verified;
   629	  message.payload = payload;
   630	  return {
   631	    fingerprint,
   632	    consecutive_same_failure: consecutive,
   633	    cumulative_failure_occurrences: cumulative,
   634	    turns_without_progress: turnsWithoutProgress,
   635	    total_objective_turns: totalTurns,
   636	  };
   637	}
   638	
   639	export function armAutonomousStrategyChange(
   640	  message: GeminxMessage,
   641	  fingerprint: string,
   642	  occurrenceCount: number
   643	): void {
   644	  const payload = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
   645	    ? { ...(message.payload as Record<string, unknown>) }
   646	    : {};
   647	  const verified = payload.verified_current_state && typeof payload.verified_current_state === "object" && !Array.isArray(payload.verified_current_state)
   648	    ? { ...(payload.verified_current_state as Record<string, unknown>) }
   649	    : {};
   650	  const previous = verified.retry_circuit && typeof verified.retry_circuit === "object" && !Array.isArray(verified.retry_circuit)
   651	    ? { ...(verified.retry_circuit as Record<string, unknown>) }
   652	    : {};
   653	  const occurrences =
   654	    previous.failure_occurrences && typeof previous.failure_occurrences === "object" && !Array.isArray(previous.failure_occurrences)
   655	      ? { ...(previous.failure_occurrences as Record<string, unknown>) }
   656	      : {};
   657	  // OpenClaw-style semantics: retire the repeated route, not the Founder
   658	  // objective. A materially different strategy gets a fresh local stagnation
   659	  // window while the global objective-turn/spend ceilings remain intact.
   660	  occurrences[fingerprint] = 0;
   661	  const strategyChangeCount = Math.max(0, Number(previous.strategy_change_count ?? 0) || 0) + 1;
   662	  verified.retry_circuit = {
   663	    ...previous,
   664	    fingerprint: null,
   665	    consecutive_same_failure: 0,
   666	    turns_without_progress: 0,
   667	    failure_occurrences: occurrences,
   668	    strategy_change_count: strategyChangeCount,
   669	    recorded_attempt: null,
   670	    strategy_change_required: true,
   671	    retired_failure_fingerprint: fingerprint,
   672	    retired_failure_occurrences: occurrenceCount,
   673	    strategy_change_armed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   674	  };
   675	  payload.verified_current_state = verified;
   676	  message.payload = payload;
   677	}
   678	
   679	export function retryCircuitBlockerMetadata(
   680	  code: string,
   681	  failureClass: string | null | undefined
   682	): Record<string, unknown> {
   683	  const externalRuntime =
   684	    failureClass === "EXTERNAL_RUNTIME"
   685	    && [
   686	      "GEMINX_EXTERNAL_RUNTIME_BOUNDARY",
   687	      "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_RETRY_CIRCUIT_OPEN",
   688	      "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_CUMULATIVE_FAILURE_SATURATION",
   689	      "GEMINX_MAX_TURNS_WITHOUT_PROGRESS",
   690	    ].includes(code);
   691	  if (externalRuntime) {
   692	    return {
   693	      external_runtime_boundary: true,
   694	      blocker_category: "EXTERNAL_RUNTIME_BOUNDARY",
   695	      suspension_class: "HOST_UNREACHABLE_AFTER_CANONICAL_RECOVERY",
   696	    };
   697	  }
   698	  return {
   699	    internal_safety_circuit: true,
   700	    blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
   701	    suspension_class:
   702	      code === "GEMINX_MAX_TURNS_WITHOUT_PROGRESS" ? "MAX_TURNS_WITHOUT_PROGRESS"
   703	        : code === "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_CUMULATIVE_FAILURE_SATURATION" ? "CUMULATIVE_FAILURE_SATURATION"
   704	          : code === "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_MAX_OBJECTIVE_TURNS" ? "MAX_OBJECTIVE_TURNS"
   705	            : code === "GEMINX_PROVIDER_TRANSIENT_CIRCUIT_OPEN" ? "PROVIDER_TRANSIENT_CIRCUIT_OPEN"
   706	              : code === "GEMINX_SPEND_RESERVATION_CONTENTION_CIRCUIT_OPEN" ? "SPEND_RESERVATION_CONTENTION"
   707	                : code === "GEMINX_PROVIDER_OUTCOME_AMBIGUOUS" ? "PROVIDER_OUTCOME_AMBIGUOUS"
   708	                  : "STAGNATION_CIRCUIT_OPEN",
   709	  };
   710	}

   712	function latestSelfRepairFailureClass(message: GeminxMessage): string | null {
   713	  const payload = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
   714	    ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown>
   715	    : {};
   716	  const verified = payload.verified_current_state && typeof payload.verified_current_state === "object" && !Array.isArray(payload.verified_current_state)
   717	    ? payload.verified_current_state as Record<string, unknown>
   718	    : {};
   719	  const receipts = Array.isArray(verified.self_repair_receipts)
   720	    ? verified.self_repair_receipts.filter((item): item is Record<string, unknown> => Boolean(item) && typeof item === "object" && !Array.isArray(item))
   721	    : [];
   722	  const latest = receipts.at(-1);
   723	  return latest ? String(latest.failure_class ?? "").trim() || null : null;
   724	}
   725	
   726	function astraProviderUnavailable(error: unknown): boolean {
   727	  const detail = error instanceof Error
   728	    ? [error.name, error.message, (error as Error & { publicMessage?: unknown }).publicMessage ?? ""].join(" ")
   729	    : String(error);
   730	  return astraCreditExhausted(error) ||
   731	    /Astra request failed|OpenAI returned HTTP (?:429|5\d\d)|timeout|timed out|failed to fetch|fetch failed|network|econnreset|econnrefused|socket|rate limit|overloaded/i.test(detail);
   732	}
   733	
   734	function astraUnavailableForObjective(command: GeminxAutonomousTaskCommand): boolean {
   735	  const state = command.verified_current_state ?? {};
   736	  const reason = String(state.astra_unavailable_reason ?? "").trim();
   737	  if (!reason) return false;
   738	
   739	  // No autonomous paid-provider re-probes. Once Astra is unavailable for a
   740	  // durable objective, DeepSeek owns continuation until an explicit state
   741	  // change clears the provider boundary.
   742	  const explicitReprobeAt = Date.parse(String(state.astra_reprobe_after ?? ""));
   743	  if (Number.isFinite(explicitReprobeAt)) return explicitReprobeAt > Date.now();
   744	  return true;
   745	}
   746	
   747	function retryDelayMs(attemptCount: number): number {
   748	  const exponent = Math.max(0, Math.min(8, attemptCount - 1));
   749	  return Math.min(MAX_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS, 5_000 * 2 ** exponent);
   750	}
   751	
   752	function boundedEvidencePreview(value: unknown, maxChars = 2_400): string | null {
   753	  if (value === undefined || value === null) return null;
   754	  let raw: string;
   755	  try {
   756	    raw = JSON.stringify(value);
   757	  } catch {
   758	    raw = String(value);
   759	  }
   760	  if (raw.length <= maxChars) return raw;
   761	  const marker = `...[compacted ${raw.length - maxChars} chars]...`;
   762	  const remaining = Math.max(200, maxChars - marker.length);
   763	  const head = Math.ceil(remaining / 2);
   764	  const tail = Math.floor(remaining / 2);
   765	  return raw.slice(0, head) + marker + raw.slice(-tail);
   766	}

   768	export function objectiveRequiresMutationEvidence(objective: string, readOnly: boolean): boolean {
   769	  if (readOnly) return false;
   770	  return /\b(?:create|write|delete|remove|edit|modify|implement|fix|repair|deploy|install|publish|send|post|commit|merge|configure|set up|build)\b/i.test(objective);
   771	}

   773	function hasMaterialMutationProgress(trace: ReturnType<ToolRegistry["trace"]>): boolean {
   774	  return trace.some((entry) =>
   775	    entry.status === "succeeded" &&
   776	    approachFamily(entry.tool).endsWith(":mutate")
   777	  );
   778	}

   780	function hasFailedMutationAttempt(trace: ReturnType<ToolRegistry["trace"]>): boolean {
   781	  return trace.some((entry) =>
   782	    entry.status !== "succeeded" &&
   783	    approachFamily(entry.tool).endsWith(":mutate")
   784	  );
   785	}

   787	function compactRetryToolTrace(trace: ReturnType<ToolRegistry["trace"]>): Array<Record<string, unknown>> {
   788	  return trace.slice(-24).map((entry) => ({
   789	    tool_call_id: entry.tool_call_id,
   790	    tool: entry.tool,
   791	    action: entry.action,
   792	    status: entry.status,
   793	    started_at: entry.started_at,
   794	    finished_at: entry.finished_at,
   795	    error: entry.error ?? null,
   796	    result_preview: boundedEvidencePreview(entry.result, 2_400),
   797	  }));

   800	type DisprovenApproachRecord = {
   801	  fingerprint: string;
   802	  failure_class: "TRANSIENT" | "LOGIC" | "PROTECTED_HUMAN_BOUNDARY" | "CAPABILITY" | "EXTERNAL_RUNTIME" | "UNKNOWN";
   803	  code: string;
   804	  approach_families: string[];
   805	  first_observed_at: string;
   806	  last_observed_at: string;
   807	  occurrences: number;
   808	  expires_at: string;
   809	  expires_when: string;
   810	};
   811	
   812	function normalizeFailureFingerprintText(value: unknown): string {
   813	  return String(value ?? "")
   814	    .toLowerCase()
   815	    .replace(/\b0x[a-f0-9]+\b/g, "<addr>")
   816	    .replace(/[a-f0-9]{40,64}/g, "<sha>")
   817	    .replace(/[a-f0-9]{8}-[a-f0-9-]{27,}/g, "<uuid>")
   818	    .replace(/\b(?:line|ln|column|col)\s*[:#]?\s*\d+\b/g, "<source-pos>")
   819	    .replace(/:\d+:\d+\b/g, ":<line>:<col>")
   820	    .replace(/\b\d{4,}\b/g, "<n>")
   821	    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
   822	    .trim()
   823	    .slice(0, 500);
   824	}
   825	
   826	function approachFamily(tool: string): string {
   827	  const name = String(tool ?? "").toLowerCase();
   828	  const family = name.split("_")[0] || "unknown";
   829	  const observation = /(?:^|_)(?:read|get|fetch|list|search|compare|status|health|info|check|verify|observe|inspect|logs?|screenshot|playtest|retest|probe|trace|console)(?:_|$)/.test(name);
   830	  return `${family}:${observation ? "observe" : "mutate"}`;
   831	}
   832	
   833	const FAILURE_TARGET_KEYS = new Set([
   834	  "repository_full_name", "repo_full_name", "owner", "repo", "path", "file_path", "branch", "ref",
   835	  "url", "host_id", "device_id", "package_name", "resource", "target", "target_id", "selector",
   836	  "element_id", "service_id", "deployment_id", "project_id", "window_id", "app_id", "domain"
   837	]);
   838	
   839	function normalizedFailureTarget(tool: string, args: unknown): string {
   840	  const record = args && typeof args === "object" && !Array.isArray(args)
   841	    ? args as Record<string, unknown>
   842	    : {};
   843	  const stable = Object.entries(record)
   844	    .filter(([key, value]) => FAILURE_TARGET_KEYS.has(key) && value !== null && value !== undefined)
   845	    .map(([key, value]) => [key, String(value).trim().toLowerCase().slice(0, 240)] as const)
   846	    .filter(([, value]) => value.length > 0)
   847	    .sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))
   848	    .slice(0, 12);
   849	  return JSON.stringify({
   850	    tool: String(tool ?? "").trim().toLowerCase(),
   851	    target: stable,
   852	  });
   853	}
   854	
   855	function successfulObservationEvidenceKeys(trace: ReturnType<ToolRegistry["trace"]>): string[] {
   856	  return [...new Set(
   857	    trace
   858	      .filter((entry) => entry.status === "succeeded" && approachFamily(entry.tool).endsWith(":observe"))
   859	      .map((entry) => normalizedFailureTarget(entry.tool, entry.args))
   860	  )].slice(-128);
   861	}
   862	
   863	export function recordRetryCircuitObservationProgress(
   864	  message: GeminxMessage,
   865	  trace: ReturnType<ToolRegistry["trace"]>
   866	): boolean {
   867	  const currentKeys = successfulObservationEvidenceKeys(trace);
   868	  if (!currentKeys.length) return false;
   869	
   870	  const payload = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
   871	    ? { ...(message.payload as Record<string, unknown>) }
   872	    : {};
   873	  const verified = payload.verified_current_state && typeof payload.verified_current_state === "object" && !Array.isArray(payload.verified_current_state)
   874	    ? { ...(payload.verified_current_state as Record<string, unknown>) }
   875	    : {};
   876	  const previous = verified.retry_circuit && typeof verified.retry_circuit === "object" && !Array.isArray(verified.retry_circuit)
   877	    ? { ...(verified.retry_circuit as Record<string, unknown>) }
   878	    : {};
   879	  const priorKeys = Array.isArray(previous.observation_evidence_keys)
   880	    ? previous.observation_evidence_keys.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string" && item.length > 0).slice(-128)
   881	    : [];
   882	  const seen = new Set(priorKeys);
   883	  const novel = currentKeys.filter((key) => !seen.has(key));
   884	  if (!novel.length) return false;
   885	
   886	  verified.retry_circuit = {
   887	    ...previous,
   888	    fingerprint: null,
   889	    consecutive_same_failure: 0,
   890	    turns_without_progress: 0,
   891	    strategy_change_count: 0,
   892	    strategy_change_required: false,
   893	    total_objective_turns: Math.max(0, Number(previous.total_objective_turns ?? 0) || 0),
   894	    failure_occurrences:
   895	      previous.failure_occurrences && typeof previous.failure_occurrences === "object" && !Array.isArray(previous.failure_occurrences)
   896	        ? previous.failure_occurrences
   897	        : {},
   898	    provider_transient_failures: Math.max(0, Number(previous.provider_transient_failures ?? 0) || 0),
   899	    provider_transient_fingerprint:
   900	      typeof previous.provider_transient_fingerprint === "string" ? previous.provider_transient_fingerprint : null,
   901	    observation_evidence_keys: [...priorKeys, ...novel].slice(-128),
   902	    recorded_attempt: null,
   903	    observed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   904	  };
   905	  payload.verified_current_state = verified;
   906	  message.payload = payload;
   907	  return true;
   908	}

   910	function retryFingerprintToolCalls(trace: ReturnType<ToolRegistry["trace"]>): Array<{
   911	  tool: string;
   912	  status: string;
   913	  error?: string | null;
   914	  args?: Record<string, unknown>;
   915	}> {
   916	  return trace.map((entry) => ({
   917	    tool: entry.tool,
   918	    status: entry.status,
   919	    error: entry.error,
   920	    args: entry.args,
   921	  }));
   922	}
   923	
   924	export function buildDisprovenApproachRecord(
   925	  input: {
   926	    code: string;
   927	    failure_class?: "TRANSIENT" | "LOGIC" | "PROTECTED_HUMAN_BOUNDARY" | "CAPABILITY" | "EXTERNAL_RUNTIME" | "UNKNOWN";
   928	    tool_calls?: Array<{ tool: string; status: string; error?: string | null; args?: Record<string, unknown> }>;
   929	  },
   930	  now = new Date()
   931	): DisprovenApproachRecord {
   932	  const failureClass = input.failure_class ?? "UNKNOWN";
   933	  const relevantCalls = (input.tool_calls ?? []).filter((call) => call.status !== "succeeded");
   934	  const calls = relevantCalls.length > 0 ? relevantCalls : (input.tool_calls ?? []);
   935	  const approachFamilies = [...new Set(calls.map((call) => approachFamily(call.tool)))].slice(0, 12);
   936	  const targetResources = [...new Set(calls.map((call) => normalizedFailureTarget(call.tool, call.args)))].slice(0, 12);
   937	  const errorShape = calls
   938	    .map((call) => normalizeFailureFingerprintText(call.error))
   939	    .filter(Boolean)
   940	    .slice(0, 8);
   941	
   942	  const fingerprint = createHash("sha256")
   943	    .update(JSON.stringify({
   944	      code: input.code,
   945	      failure_class: failureClass,
   946	      approach_families: approachFamilies,
   947	      target_resources: targetResources,
   948	      error_shape: errorShape,
   949	    }))
   950	    .digest("hex")
   951	    .slice(0, 24);
   952	
   953	  const observedAt = now.toISOString();
   954	  const expiresAt = new Date(now.getTime() + 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1_000).toISOString();
   955	  return {
   956	    fingerprint,
   957	    failure_class: failureClass,
   958	    code: input.code,
   959	    approach_families: approachFamilies,
   960	    first_observed_at: observedAt,
   961	    last_observed_at: observedAt,
   962	    occurrences: 1,
   963	    expires_at: expiresAt,
   964	    expires_when: "Material target/runtime/code identity changes, or the failure fingerprint no longer reproduces.",
   965	  };
   966	}
   967	
   968	function mergeDisprovenApproach(
   969	  existing: unknown,
   970	  next: DisprovenApproachRecord,
   971	  now = Date.now()
   972	): { records: DisprovenApproachRecord[]; repeated: boolean } {
   973	  const records = Array.isArray(existing)
   974	    ? existing.filter((item): item is DisprovenApproachRecord => {
   975	        if (!item || typeof item !== "object" || Array.isArray(item)) return false;
   976	        const record = item as Partial<DisprovenApproachRecord>;
   977	        const expiry = Date.parse(String(record.expires_at ?? ""));
   978	        return typeof record.fingerprint === "string" && (!Number.isFinite(expiry) || expiry > now);
   979	      })
   980	    : [];
   981	
   982	  const previous = records.find((item) => item.fingerprint === next.fingerprint);
   983	  const merged = previous
   984	    ? {
   985	        ...next,
   986	        first_observed_at: previous.first_observed_at,
   987	        occurrences: Math.max(1, Number(previous.occurrences ?? 1)) + 1,
   988	      }
   989	    : next;
   990	
   991	  return {
   992	    records: [...records.filter((item) => item.fingerprint !== next.fingerprint), merged].slice(-16),
   993	    repeated: merged.occurrences > 1,
   994	  };
   995	}
   996	
   997	function heartbeatJob(store: CommsStore, messageId: string): void {
   998	  const latest = store.getMessage(messageId);
   999	  if (!latest || latest.status !== "RUNNING") return;
  1000	  latest.last_heartbeat_at = new Date().toISOString();
  1001	  store.saveMessage(latest);
  1002	}

  1004	function taskPackageFromPayload(payload: unknown): GeminxTaskPackage | null {
  1005	  if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object" || Array.isArray(payload)) return null;
  1006	  const record = payload as Record<string, unknown>;
  1007	  const candidate = record.task_package ?? (record.command === "RUN_TASK_PACKAGE" ? record.task : null);
  1008	  if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object" || Array.isArray(candidate)) return null;
  1009	  return candidate as GeminxTaskPackage;
  1010	}
