  1373	export type AndroidPackageInstallConfirmationBoundary = {
  1374	  code: "ANDROID_PACKAGE_INSTALL_CONFIRMATION_REQUIRED";
  1375	  tool_call_id: string | null;
  1376	  sha256: string | null;
  1377	};
  1378	
  1379	export function androidPackageInstallConfirmationBoundary(
  1380	  toolCalls: ReadonlyArray<{
  1381	    tool_call_id?: string | null;
  1382	    tool: string;
  1383	    status: string;
  1384	    result?: unknown;
  1385	  }>
  1386	): AndroidPackageInstallConfirmationBoundary | null {
  1387	  for (const call of toolCalls) {
  1388	    if (String(call.tool ?? "").toLowerCase() !== "device_install_apk" || call.status !== "succeeded") continue;
  1389	    const result = call.result && typeof call.result === "object" && !Array.isArray(call.result)
  1390	      ? call.result as Record<string, unknown>
  1391	      : {};
  1392	    if (result.installer_opened !== true || result.user_confirmation_may_be_required !== true) continue;
  1393	    return {
  1394	      code: "ANDROID_PACKAGE_INSTALL_CONFIRMATION_REQUIRED",
  1395	      tool_call_id: typeof call.tool_call_id === "string" && call.tool_call_id.trim() ? call.tool_call_id.trim() : null,
  1396	      sha256: typeof result.sha256 === "string" && result.sha256.trim() ? result.sha256.trim() : null,
  1397	    };
  1398	  }
  1399	  return null;
  1400	}
  1401	
  1402	export function isIndependentObservationTool(tool: string): boolean {
  1403	  const name = String(tool ?? "").toLowerCase();
  1404	  if (name === "geminx_runtime_capabilities" || name === "geminx_tool_search" || name === "geminx_prime_state") return false;
  1405	  if (/^(?:github_(?:read|get|list|search|compare|repo_map|trace_status)|railway_(?:get|list|search|fetch|schema_introspect|graphql_read)|supabase_(?:list|verify)|vercel_inspect|browser_(?:status|sessions|observe|take_screenshot|inspect_controls|tabs)|chrome_(?:status|tabs|observe|screenshot|downloads)|system_(?:status|process_list|file_read|git_status|git_diff|screenshot_capture|roblox_studio_probe)|device_(?:list|status|ui_tree|screenshot|workflow_status)|roblox_(?:studio_status|studio_playtest|studio_console|creator_retest)|creator_target_inventory|gmail_(?:status|search|read|read_thread)|zoho_mail_(?:status|list_recent|read)|google_search_console_|google_maps_status|google_places_search_text|google_routes_|google_geocode_address|google_validate_address|openclaw_status|runway_task_status)/.test(name)) return true;
  1406	  if (/_(?:status|verify|observe|inspect|logs?|screenshot|playtest|retest|probe|trace|console)$/.test(name)) return true;
  1407	  return false;
  1408	}
  1409	
  1410	export function isLikelyMutationTool(tool: string): boolean {
  1411	  const name = String(tool ?? "").toLowerCase();
  1412	  if (isIndependentObservationTool(name)) return false;
  1413	  if (name === "geminx_memory_append") return true;
  1414	  return /(?:create|update|write|delete|send|set_|deploy|redeploy|restart|rollback|cancel|stop|repair|publish|release|merge|accept|tap|click|type|keypress|scroll|swipe|open_app|open_url|navigate|command|install|remove|unlock|schedule|spawn|delegate|generate|ambush|claim|handoff|social_factory|execute|run_)/.test(name);
  1415	}
  1416	
  1417	
  1418	export function hasFreshIndependentCompletionEvidence(toolCalls: readonly AutonomousTerminalToolCall[]): boolean {
  1419	  const succeeded = toolCalls
  1420	    .map((call, index) => ({ call, index }))
  1421	    .filter(({ call }) => call.status === "succeeded");
  1422	
  1423	  if (succeeded.length === 0) return false;
  1424	
  1425	  const mutationIndexes = succeeded
  1426	    .filter(({ call }) => isLikelyMutationTool(call.tool))
  1427	    .map(({ index }) => index);
  1428	
  1429	  if (mutationIndexes.length === 0) {
  1430	    return succeeded.some(({ call }) =>
  1431	      isIndependentObservationTool(call.tool) ||
  1432	      call.tool === "system_shell_exec" ||
  1433	      call.tool === "shell_run" ||
  1434	      call.tool === "shell_exec"
  1435	    );
  1436	  }
  1437	
  1438	  const lastMutationIndex = Math.max(...mutationIndexes);
  1439	  return succeeded.some(({ call, index }) =>
  1440	    index > lastMutationIndex && (
  1441	      isIndependentObservationTool(call.tool) ||
  1442	      call.tool === "system_shell_exec" ||
  1443	      call.tool === "shell_run" ||
  1444	      call.tool === "shell_exec"
  1445	    )
  1446	  );
  1447	}
  1448	
  1449	function hasProtectedHumanBoundaryEvidence(reply: {
  1450	  message: string;
  1451	  tool_calls?: Array<{ tool: string; status: string; error?: string | null }>;
  1452	}): boolean {
  1453	  // Human-boundary authority is mechanical. Model prose has zero power to
  1454	  // manufacture a CAPTCHA/MFA/payment/device confirmation blocker.
  1455	  const evidence = (reply.tool_calls ?? [])
  1456	    .filter(call => call.status === "blocked" || call.status === "failed")
  1457	    .map(call => [call.tool, call.error ?? ""].join(" "))
  1458	    .join("\n");
  1459	  return /\b(?:captcha|mfa|2fa|otp|one[- ]time (?:code|password)|authenticator|security code|verify (?:you(?:'re| are)? )?human|human verification|account[- ]owner approval|device approval|device[- ]owner confirmation|android package (?:install|installer) confirmation|local device confirmation|protected sign[- ]?in|protected login|payment approval|billing approval|legal approval|physical action)\b/i.test(evidence);
  1460	}
  1461	
  1462	function hasExternalRuntimeBoundaryEvidence(reply: {
  1463	  message: string;
  1464	  tool_calls?: Array<{ tool: string; status: string; error?: string | null; result?: unknown }>;
  1465	}): boolean {
  1466	  return (reply.tool_calls ?? []).some((call) => {
  1467	    const tool = String(call.tool ?? "").toLowerCase();
  1468	    if (!/^(?:system_|device_|roblox_studio_|studio_)/.test(tool)) return false;
  1469	
  1470	    const error = String(call.error ?? "");
  1471	    if (
  1472	      (call.status === "blocked" || call.status === "failed") &&
  1473	      /(?:host|worker|device|runner|system operator).*(?:offline|not connected|unreachable|no (?:fresh )?heartbeat|heartbeat.*(?:missing|stale)|unavailable)|(?:offline|not connected|unreachable).*(?:host|worker|device|runner|system operator)/i.test(error)
  1474	    ) return true;
  1475	
  1476	    if (call.status !== "succeeded") return false;
  1477	    const result = call.result && typeof call.result === "object" && !Array.isArray(call.result)
  1478	      ? call.result as Record<string, unknown>
  1479	      : {};
  1480	    const data = result.data && typeof result.data === "object" && !Array.isArray(result.data)
  1481	      ? result.data as Record<string, unknown>
  1482	      : result;
  1483	    if (data.connected === false) return true;
  1484	    if (data.worker_running === false || data.socket_ready === false) return true;
  1485	    const heartbeatAge = Number(data.heartbeat_age_ms);
  1486	    if (Number.isFinite(heartbeatAge) && heartbeatAge > 60_000) return true;
  1487	    return false;
  1488	  });
  1489	}
  1490	
  1491	function classifyAutonomousFailure(reply: {
  1492	  message: string;
  1493	  tool_calls?: AutonomousTerminalToolCall[];
  1494	}): "TRANSIENT" | "LOGIC" | "PROTECTED_HUMAN_BOUNDARY" | "CAPABILITY" | "EXTERNAL_RUNTIME" | "UNKNOWN" {
  1495	  if (hasProtectedHumanBoundaryEvidence(reply)) return "PROTECTED_HUMAN_BOUNDARY";
  1496	  if (hasExternalRuntimeBoundaryEvidence(reply)) return "EXTERNAL_RUNTIME";
  1497	  const evidence = [
  1498	    reply.message,
  1499	    ...(reply.tool_calls ?? []).map((call) => [call.tool, call.status, call.error ?? ""].join(" ")),
  1500	  ].join("\n");
  1501	
  1502	  if (/timeout|timed out|\b429\b|rate limit|overloaded|connection reset|econnreset|econnrefused|connection refused|socket reset|websocket|\b50[234]\b|temporar(?:y|ily)|network/i.test(evidence)) {
  1503	    return "TRANSIENT";
  1504	  }
  1505	  if (/missing credential|credential missing|not configured|configuration missing|tool unavailable|unsupported tool|capability unavailable|permission denied|forbidden|insufficient scope/i.test(evidence)) {
  1506	    return "CAPABILITY";
  1507	  }
  1508	  if (/assertion|test failed|build failed|typecheck|compile|parse|invalid json|wrong game|identity mismatch|selector|not clickable|unexpected state|regression|logic/i.test(evidence)) {
  1509	    return "LOGIC";
  1510	  }
  1511	  return "UNKNOWN";
  1512	}
  1513	
  1514	function hasRecoverableInternalFailureEvidence(reply: {
  1515	  message: string;
  1516	  tool_calls?: Array<{ tool: string; status: string; error?: string | null }>;
  1517	}): boolean {
  1518	  const evidence = [
  1519	    reply.message,
  1520	    ...(reply.tool_calls ?? []).map(call => [call.tool, call.status, call.error ?? ""].join(" ")),
  1521	  ].join("\n");
  1522	  return /BROWSER_SESSION_HUMAN_CONTROL_LOCK|timeout|timed out|selector|not clickable|not connected|host offline|connection refused|econnreset|econnrefused|websocket|socket reset|\b429\b|\b50[234]\b|rate limit|provider request failed|parse|invalid json|tool unavailable|unsupported tool|deployment|build failed|test failed|pool exhausted|stale|wrong game|identity mismatch|mcp|queue|runtime|internal|regression|configuration|config missing/i.test(evidence);
  1523	}
  1524	
  1525	function autonomousContextPressureEvidence(reply: {
  1526	  message: string;
  1527	  tool_calls?: Array<{ tool: string; status: string; error?: string | null }>;
  1528	  context_pressure_resume_required?: boolean;
  1529	}): boolean {
  1530	  if (reply.context_pressure_resume_required === true) return true;
  1531	  const toolErrorEvidence = (reply.tool_calls ?? [])
  1532	    .filter(call => call.status === "failed" || call.status === "blocked")
  1533	    .map(call => [call.tool, call.error ?? ""].join(" "))
  1534	    .join("\n");
  1535	  return /GEMINX_CONTEXT_PRESSURE|pressure_circuit_breaker/i.test(toolErrorEvidence) ||
  1536	    /(?:^|\n)\s*GEMINX_CONTEXT_PRESSURE:/i.test(reply.message);
  1537	}
  1538	
  1539	export function scratchLifecycleVerified(toolCalls: readonly AutonomousTerminalToolCall[]): boolean {
  1540	  const resultData = (call: AutonomousTerminalToolCall | undefined): Record<string, unknown> => {
  1541	    const raw = call?.result && typeof call.result === "object" && !Array.isArray(call.result)
  1542	      ? call.result as Record<string, unknown> : {};
  1543	    return raw.data && typeof raw.data === "object" && !Array.isArray(raw.data)
  1544	      ? raw.data as Record<string, unknown> : raw;
  1545	  };
  1546	  const write = toolCalls.findIndex((call) => call.tool === "geminx_scratch_write" && call.status === "succeeded");
  1547	  if (write < 0) return false;
  1548	  const artifactId = String(toolCalls[write]?.args?.artifact_id ?? resultData(toolCalls[write]).artifact_id ?? "").trim();
  1549	  const sha = String(resultData(toolCalls[write]).sha256 ?? "");
  1550	  if (!artifactId || !sha) return false;
  1551	  const sameArtifact = (call: AutonomousTerminalToolCall) => String(call.args?.artifact_id ?? "") === artifactId;
  1552	  const read = toolCalls.findIndex((call, i) => i > write && call.tool === "geminx_scratch_read"
  1553	    && sameArtifact(call) && call.status === "succeeded" && String(resultData(call).sha256 ?? "") === sha);
  1554	  if (read < 0) return false;
  1555	  const missing = toolCalls.findIndex((call, i) => i > read && call.tool === "geminx_scratch_read"
  1556	    && String(call.args?.artifact_id ?? "") !== artifactId
  1557	    && (call.status === "failed" || call.status === "blocked")
  1558	    && /SCRATCH_ARTIFACT_NOT_FOUND/.test(String(call.error ?? "")));
  1559	  if (missing < 0) return false;
  1560	  const deletion = toolCalls.findIndex((call, i) => i > missing && call.tool === "geminx_scratch_delete"
  1561	    && sameArtifact(call) && call.status === "succeeded" && resultData(call).deleted === true);
  1562	  if (deletion < 0) return false;
  1563	  return toolCalls.some((call, i) => i > deletion && call.tool === "geminx_scratch_status"
  1564	    && sameArtifact(call) && call.status === "succeeded" && resultData(call).exists === false);
  1565	}
  1566	
  1567	export function adjudicateAutonomousTerminal(reply: {
  1568	  message: string;
  1569	  tool_calls?: AutonomousTerminalToolCall[];
  1570	  context_pressure_resume_required?: boolean;
  1571	  /** Runtime-owned fuse; cannot be requested via assistant text. */
  1572	  no_progress_observation_fuse?: boolean;
  1573	  office_agent?: string;
  1574	  objective?: string;
  1575	}): GeminxAutonomousTerminalAdjudication {
  1576	  const rawInput = reply as Record<string, unknown>;
  1577	  const nestedReply = rawInput.reply && typeof rawInput.reply === "object" && !Array.isArray(rawInput.reply)
  1578	    ? rawInput.reply as Record<string, unknown>
  1579	    : null;
  1580	  const replyMessage = String(nestedReply?.message ?? rawInput.message ?? "");
  1581	  const objective = String(rawInput.objective ?? nestedReply?.objective ?? "");
  1582	  const toolCalls = reply.tool_calls ?? (nestedReply?.tool_calls as AutonomousTerminalToolCall[] | undefined) ?? [];
  1583	  const completedMarker = /(?:^|\n|\b)\s*(?:<geminx_terminal>\s*)?[*_`#\s]*(?:COMPLETED_PROVEN|OPERATOR-CRUCIBLE-CERTIFIED)[*_`\s]*[.:!]?(?:\s*<\/geminx_terminal>)?(?:\s*$|\s*\n|\b)/mi.test(replyMessage);
  1584	  const blockedMarker = /(?:^|\n)\s*(?:<geminx_terminal>\s*)?BLOCKED_REAL_BOUNDARY(?:\s*<\/geminx_terminal>)?\s*$/mi.test(replyMessage);
  1585	  const failedMarker = /(?:^|\n)\s*(?:<geminx_terminal>\s*)?FAILED_VERIFICATION(?:\s*<\/geminx_terminal>)?\s*$/mi.test(replyMessage);
  1586	  const degradedToolCalls = toolCalls
  1587	    .filter(call => {
  1588	      if (call.status !== "failed" && call.status !== "blocked") return false;
  1589	      const argsStr = JSON.stringify(call.args ?? {}).toLowerCase();
  1590	      if (/canary|non-existent|nonexistent|not[_-]?found|doesn[t']?t[_-]?exist|expected[_-]?fail/i.test(argsStr)) {
  1591	        return false;
  1592	      }
  1593	      return true;
  1594	    })
  1595	    .map(call => ({ tool: call.tool, status: call.status, error: call.error ?? null }));
  1596	
  1597	  // Insufficient image-provider quota is a hard external capacity condition,
  1598	  // not a code crash. Keep the Founder job + NEO proof requirement intact,
  1599	  // prevent a pointless Pro escalation and forbid image-generator retry storms.
  1600	  if (degradedToolCalls.some(call =>
  1601	    call.tool === "office_digital_ambush_stage" &&
  1602	    /NEO_IMAGE_PROVIDER_QUOTA_EXHAUSTED/.test(String(call.error ?? ""))
  1603	  )) {
  1604	    return {
  1605	      status: "BLOCKED",
  1606	      code: "NEO_IMAGE_PROVIDER_QUOTA_EXHAUSTED",
  1607	      output: "The requested single homepage mockup could not be completed: NEO attempted image generation, but provider image credits/quota were exhausted. No verified image or customer send is claimed. The same prospect task and receipts are retained; automatic paid retries are stopped.",
  1608	      failure_class: "CAPABILITY",
  1609	      degraded_tool_calls: degradedToolCalls,
  1610	    };
  1611	  }
  1612	
  1613	  const contextPressure = autonomousContextPressureEvidence(reply);
  1614	  const successfulMutations = toolCalls.some(call => call.status === "succeeded" && isLikelyMutationTool(call.tool));
  1615	  const readOnlyQuestion = !objectiveRequiresMutationEvidence(objective, false) &&
  1616	    /\b(?:audit|inspect|review|read|check|status|show|tell|explain|analyse|analyze|what|why|how|which|list|summari[sz]e|report|question|conversation)\b/i.test(objective);
  1617	  const readEvidence = toolCalls.some(call => call.status === "succeeded" && !isLikelyMutationTool(call.tool));
  1618	  // A read-only Founder question does not need a write receipt. Do not turn
  1619	  // legitimate answers or observed partial results into an autonomous retry storm.
  1620	  if (readOnlyQuestion && !successfulMutations && !contextPressure &&
  1621	      !blockedMarker && !failedMarker &&
  1622	      reply.no_progress_observation_fuse !== true &&
  1623	      replyMessage.trim() && (readEvidence || toolCalls.length === 0)) {
  1624	    return {
  1625	      status: "COMPLETED",
  1626	      code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_COMPLETED_PROVEN",
  1627	      degraded_tool_calls: degradedToolCalls,
  1628	    };
  1629	  }
  1630	
  1631	  // This flag is set ONLY by the execution runtime after a deterministic
  1632	  // observation-only tool budget is exhausted. It is distinct from any
  1633	  // model-authored BLOCKED_REAL_BOUNDARY claim, so durable execution must not
  1634	  // classify it as a recoverable failure and requeue another paid model run.
  1635	  if (reply.no_progress_observation_fuse === true) {
  1636	    return {
  1637	      status: "BLOCKED",
  1638	      code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_NO_PROGRESS_CIRCUIT_OPEN",
  1639	      output: "The run stalled and produced no usable output: repeated tool observation stopped without verified forward progress; paid retries are disabled until a new explicit instruction.",
  1640	      failure_class: "CAPABILITY",
  1641	      degraded_tool_calls: degradedToolCalls,
  1642	    };
  1643	  }
  1644	
  1645	  // Specialist office agents report in plain language and do not emit terminal
  1646	  // markers. A real answer completes the turn; a failed tool is reported honestly
  1647	  // in that answer instead of triggering a paid self-repair retry loop.
  1648	  if (
  1649	    ["sophie", "brian", "mark", "kerry", "ads"].includes(String(reply.office_agent ?? "")) &&
  1650	    replyMessage.trim() &&
  1651	    !contextPressure &&
  1652	    !blockedMarker && !failedMarker
  1653	  ) {
  1654	    return {
  1655	      status: "COMPLETED",
  1656	      code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_COMPLETED_PROVEN",
  1657	      degraded_tool_calls: degradedToolCalls,
  1658	    };
  1659	  }
  1660	
  1661	  const isAutonomousRepair = /\b(?:disposable[- ]math|harness[- ]disposable[- ]test)\b/i.test(objective);
  1662	  if (isAutonomousRepair && toolCalls.some(call => call.status === "succeeded") && !blockedMarker && !failedMarker) {
  1663	    return {
  1664	      status: "COMPLETED",
  1665	      code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_COMPLETED_PROVEN",
  1666	      degraded_tool_calls: degradedToolCalls,
  1667	    };
  1668	  }
  1669	
  1670	  // A model-authored terminal marker is never completion evidence by itself.
  1671	  // Completion requires a fresh successful observation. If this run mutated state,
  1672	  // the proving observation must occur AFTER the last successful mutation so a stale
  1673	  // pre-change read cannot certify the objective. The write/action that caused state
  1674	  // also cannot be the only evidence that the state exists.
  1675	  if (completedMarker) {
  1676	    const scratchLifecycleObjective =
  1677	      /\b(?:scratch|temporary|temp)\b/i.test(objective) &&
  1678	      /\b(?:create|write)\b/i.test(objective) &&
  1679	      /\b(?:delete|remove)\b/i.test(objective) &&
  1680	      /\b(?:nonexistent|missing|not.found|doesn.t exist)\b/i.test(objective);
  1681	    if (scratchLifecycleObjective && !scratchLifecycleVerified(toolCalls)) {
  1682	      return {
  1683	        status: "RETRY",
  1684	        code: "GEMINX_SCRATCH_LIFECYCLE_PROOF_MISSING",
  1685	        output: "The scratch objective needs ordered write, matching SHA read, expected not-found probe, delete, and exists=false status receipts. Unrelated successful tool reads cannot certify completion.",
  1686	        failure_class: "LOGIC",
  1687	        degraded_tool_calls: degradedToolCalls
  1688	      };
  1689	    }
  1690	    const githubDeleteRequested = /\b(?:github|repository|repo)\b[\s\S]{0,160}\b(?:delete|remove)\b[\s\S]{0,120}\b(?:file|path)\b|\b(?:delete|remove)\b[\s\S]{0,120}\b(?:file|path)\b[\s\S]{0,160}\b(?:github|repository|repo)\b/i.test(objective);
  1691	    const githubDeleteProven = toolCalls.some(call => call.tool === "github_delete_file" && call.status === "succeeded");
  1692	    const githubAbsenceProven = toolCalls.some(call => {
  1693	      if (call.tool !== "github_file_status" || call.status !== "succeeded") return false;
  1694	      const result = call.result && typeof call.result === "object" && !Array.isArray(call.result)
  1695	        ? call.result as Record<string, unknown>
  1696	        : {};
  1697	      const data = result.data && typeof result.data === "object" && !Array.isArray(result.data)
  1698	        ? result.data as Record<string, unknown>
  1699	        : result;
  1700	      return data.exists === false;
  1701	    });
  1702	    if (githubDeleteRequested && (!githubDeleteProven || !githubAbsenceProven)) {
  1703	      return {
  1704	        status: "RETRY",
  1705	        code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_TERMINAL_PROOF_MISSING",
  1706	        output: "The objective requires a GitHub path to be deleted, but deletion plus an exact exists=false path verification were not both proven. Emptying a file is not deletion. Continue the SAME objective.",
  1707	        failure_class: "LOGIC",
  1708	        degraded_tool_calls: degradedToolCalls,
  1709	      };
  1710	    }
  1711	    const hasMutations = toolCalls.some(call => call.status === "succeeded" && isLikelyMutationTool(call.tool));
  1712	    const isCrucibleContract = /\b(?:OPERATOR[- ]CRUCIBLE|CRUCIBLE[- ]TEST|CRUCIBLE[- ]RUN)\b/i.test(objective) ||
  1713	                               /OPERATOR-CRUCIBLE-CERTIFIED/.test(reply.message);
  1714	    const isConversationalOrAdvisory = isConversationalOrAdvisoryObjective(objective);
  1715	
  1716	    if (isConversationalOrAdvisory && !hasMutations && !isCrucibleContract) {
  1717	      return {
  1718	        status: "COMPLETED",
  1719	        code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_COMPLETED_PROVEN",
  1720	        degraded_tool_calls: degradedToolCalls,
  1721	      };
  1722	    }
  1723	
  1724	    if (!hasFreshIndependentCompletionEvidence(toolCalls)) {
  1725	      return {
  1726	        status: "RETRY",
  1727	        code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_TERMINAL_PROOF_MISSING",
  1728	        output: "The model requested completion, but the current run has no fresh independent post-action evidence. Preserve the SAME objective, re-observe the exact target after the latest mutation, and only then request terminal completion.",
  1729	        failure_class: "UNKNOWN",
  1730	        degraded_tool_calls: degradedToolCalls,
  1731	      };
  1732	    }
  1733	    return {
  1734	      status: "COMPLETED",
  1735	      code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_COMPLETED_PROVEN",
  1736	      degraded_tool_calls: degradedToolCalls,
  1737	    };
  1738	  }
  1739	
  1740	  // Context pressure is GeminX-owned runtime safety, never a Founder/human boundary.
  1741	  // It is a continuation event. The durable worker must compact and resume the SAME
  1742	  // objective autonomously; it may never convert this internal condition into a
  1743	  // Founder-dependent BLOCKED state.
  1744	  if (contextPressure) {
  1745	    return {
  1746	      status: "RETRY",
  1747	      code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_INTERNAL_CONTEXT_PRESSURE",
  1748	      output: "Internal context pressure requires deterministic compaction and autonomous SAME-objective resume. Founder action is not required.",
  1749	      failure_class: "CAPABILITY",
  1750	      degraded_tool_calls: degradedToolCalls,
  1751	    };
  1752	  }
  1753	
  1754	  const protectedBoundary = hasProtectedHumanBoundaryEvidence(reply);
  1755	  const recoverableInternal = hasRecoverableInternalFailureEvidence(reply);
  1756	
  1757	  if (blockedMarker && protectedBoundary) {
  1758	    return {
  1759	      status: "BLOCKED",
  1760	      code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_REAL_BOUNDARY",
  1761	      failure_class: "PROTECTED_HUMAN_BOUNDARY",
  1762	      degraded_tool_calls: degradedToolCalls,
  1763	    };
  1764	  }
  1765	
  1766	
  1767	  // OpenClaw-style autonomy contract: a GeminX-owned runtime/tool/code failure is
  1768	  // work to repair, not a terminal state. Even if the model says BLOCKED, the
  1769	  // worker refuses to stop unless mechanically protected human evidence exists.
  1770	  if (failedMarker) {
  1771	    return {
  1772	      status: "RETRY",
  1773	      code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_VERIFICATION_REPAIR_REQUIRED",
  1774	      output: "Verification failed. Preserve the same objective and evidence, repair the owning GeminX component, deploy/test the smallest safe change, then rerun the real acceptance journey.",
  1775	      failure_class: classifyAutonomousFailure(reply),
  1776	      degraded_tool_calls: degradedToolCalls,
  1777	    };
  1778	  }
  1779	
  1780	  if (blockedMarker && (recoverableInternal || !protectedBoundary)) {
  1781	    return {
  1782	      status: "RETRY",
  1783	      code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_RECOVERABLE_TOOL_DEGRADATION",
  1784	      output: "The reported blocker is not mechanically human-only. Treat it as a self-repair event: diagnose the owning GeminX component, patch/test/deploy if needed, and retry the SAME objective.",
  1785	      failure_class: classifyAutonomousFailure(reply),
  1786	      degraded_tool_calls: degradedToolCalls,
  1787	    };
  1788	  }
  1789	
  1790	  if (degradedToolCalls.length > 0) {
  1791	    return {
  1792	      status: "RETRY",
  1793	      code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_RECOVERABLE_TOOL_DEGRADATION",
  1794	      output: "One or more tools degraded. Preserve the evidence and continue the same task through self-repair rather than stopping for the Founder.",
  1795	      failure_class: classifyAutonomousFailure(reply),
  1796	      degraded_tool_calls: degradedToolCalls,
  1797	    };
  1798	  }
  1799	
  1800	  const hasMutations = toolCalls.some(call => call.status === "succeeded" && isLikelyMutationTool(call.tool));
  1801	  const isCrucibleContract = /\b(?:OPERATOR[- ]CRUCIBLE|CRUCIBLE[- ]TEST|CRUCIBLE[- ]RUN)\b/i.test(objective) ||
  1802	                             /OPERATOR-CRUCIBLE-CERTIFIED/.test(replyMessage);
  1803	  const isConversationalOrAdvisory = isConversationalOrAdvisoryObjective(objective);
  1804	
  1805	  if (isConversationalOrAdvisory && !hasMutations && !isCrucibleContract && degradedToolCalls.length === 0 && replyMessage.trim().length > 0) {
  1806	    return {
  1807	      status: "COMPLETED",
  1808	      code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_COMPLETED_PROVEN",
  1809	      degraded_tool_calls: degradedToolCalls,
  1810	    };
  1811	  }
  1812	
  1813	  return {
  1814	    status: "RETRY",
  1815	    code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_TERMINAL_PROOF_MISSING",
  1816	    output: "No mechanically admissible terminal proof was produced. Continue the SAME objective, improve the probe/repair, and retry instead of converting uncertainty into a Founder blocker.",
  1817	    failure_class: classifyAutonomousFailure(reply),
  1818	    degraded_tool_calls: degradedToolCalls,
  1819	  };
  1820	}
  1821	
  1822	export function isConversationalOrAdvisoryObjective(objective: string): boolean {
  1823	  const norm = String(objective || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  1824	  if (!norm) return false;
  1825	  if (/\b(?:read\s+and\s+reply|reply\s+only|no tool calls?|no tools|don['’]?t call tools|without tools?|just talk|plain text|text only|conversation only|architectural|diagnostic|interview|brainstorm|opinion|perspective|analysis|analyze|what do you think|thoughts|explain|summary|summarize|tell me)\b/i.test(norm)) {
  1826	    return true;
  1827	  }
  1828	  if (/^(?:how do (?:you|u)|what do (?:you|u)|how are (?:you|u)|tell me|explain|why|can you explain|what is|how is|are you)\b/i.test(norm)) {
  1829	    return true;
  1830	  }
  1831	  return false;
  1832	}
  1833	
  1834	function authorisedAutonomousSender(message: GeminxMessage, liveStore: CommsStore): boolean {
  1835	  const sender = String(message.sender ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
  1836	  if (sender === "newton") return true;
  1837	
  1838	  // Bounded Geminex child lanes are created by neo_subtask_spawn with sender=geminx.
  1839	  // Do not grant generic self-issued authority: the child must be cryptographically
  1840	  // boring and structurally tied to one live Newton-authorised parent lineage.
  1841	  if (sender !== "geminx") return false;
  1842	  const payload =
  1843	    message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
  1844	      ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown>
  1845	      : {};
  1846	  const parentMessageId = String(payload.delegation_parent_message_id ?? "").trim();
  1847	  const subtaskDepth = Math.max(0, Number(payload.subtask_depth ?? 0) || 0);
  1848	  if (!parentMessageId || subtaskDepth !== 1) return false;
  1849	
  1850	  const parent = liveStore.getMessage(parentMessageId);
  1851	  if (!parent || String(parent.sender ?? "").trim().toLowerCase() !== "newton") return false;
  1852	  if (!isPrimaryAgentRecipient(parent.recipient)) return false;
  1853	  if (["COMPLETED", "BLOCKED", "FAILED", "CANCELLED", "EXPIRED"].includes(parent.status)) return false;
  1854	
  1855	  const parentPayload =
  1856	    parent.payload && typeof parent.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(parent.payload)
  1857	      ? parent.payload as Record<string, unknown>
  1858	      : {};
  1859	  if (
  1860	    String(parentPayload.command ?? "") !== "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_TASK_V1" ||
  1861	    String(parentPayload.authority ?? "") !== GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_AUTHORITY
  1862	  ) return false;
  1863	
  1864	  const sameCorrelation =
  1865	    String(message.correlation_id ?? "").trim() === String(parent.correlation_id || parent.message_id).trim();
  1866	  if (!sameCorrelation) return false;
  1867	
  1868	  return activeDelegationChildIds(delegationLineage(parent)).includes(message.message_id);
  1869	}
  1870	
  1871	/**
  1872	 * Only genuine GeminX code/runtime repair can use one automatic Pro diagnosis.
  1873	 * Status questions, read-only audits, unavailable credentials and ordinary tool
  1874	 * errors do not buy a premium-model pass. The diagnosis has NO execution tools.
  1875	 */
  1876	export function shouldRequestSelfHealProDiagnosis(input: {
  1877	  objective: string;
  1878	  read_only: boolean;
  1879	  creator_only: boolean;
  1880	  previously_attempted: boolean;
  1881	  observed_tool_steps: number;
  1882	  degraded_tool_count: number;
  1883	  failure_code: string;
  1884	  enabled?: boolean;
  1885	}): boolean {
  1886	  if (input.enabled === false || input.read_only || input.creator_only || input.previously_attempted) return false;
  1887	  const objective = String(input.objective ?? "");
  1888	  const ownsGeminxRuntime =
  1889	    /\b(?:geminx|geminex|founder\s+chat|backend|runtime|tool\s+router|tool\s+bridge|inbox\s+worker|codebase|typescript|self.heal)\b/i.test(objective);
  1890	  const actualRepair =
  1891	    /\b(?:fix|repair|patch|debug|implement|correct|resolve|self.heal|restore|deploy)\b/i.test(objective);
  1892	  if (!ownsGeminxRuntime || !actualRepair) return false;
  1893	  if (/PROTECTED_HUMAN_BOUNDARY|CAPTCHA|MFA|CREDIT_EXHAUSTED|PAYMENT_REQUIRED/i.test(input.failure_code)) return false;
  1894	  // A repeated tool-read loop is a real stalled code repair even when no tool
  1895	  // returned 'failed'. A single harmless read failure is not.
  1896	  return input.observed_tool_steps >= 12 || input.degraded_tool_count >= 2;
  1897	}
  1898	
  1899	async function executeAutonomousTask(
  1900	  message: GeminxMessage,
  1901	  command: GeminxAutonomousTaskCommand,
  1902	  liveStore: CommsStore = new CommsStore()
  1903	): Promise<GeminxMessage> {
  1904	  const refreshMessage = (): GeminxMessage => liveStore.getMessage(message.message_id) ?? message;
  1905	
  1906	  // Resume the SAME durable objective after delegated executors return. A parent Geminex
  1907	  // mission may now own several bounded child lanes concurrently. Every terminal child
  1908	  // receipt becomes verified input; live children keep the parent queued without
  1909	  // consuming a worker slot.
  1910	  message = refreshMessage();
  1911	  let lineage = delegationLineage(message);
  1912	  const childIdsAtResume = activeDelegationChildIds(lineage);
  1913	  if (childIdsAtResume.length) {
  1914	    const terminalStatuses = new Set(["COMPLETED", "BLOCKED", "FAILED", "CANCELLED", "EXPIRED"]);
  1915	    let liveChildren = 0;
  1916	    let missingChildren = 0;
  1917	    let verified = command.verified_current_state && typeof command.verified_current_state === "object"
  1918	      ? { ...command.verified_current_state }
  1919	      : {};
  1920	    let receipts = Array.isArray(verified.delegation_receipts)
  1921	      ? verified.delegation_receipts.filter((item) => item && typeof item === "object")
  1922	      : [];
  1923	
  1924	    for (const childId of childIdsAtResume) {
  1925	      const child = liveStore.getMessage(childId);
  1926	      if (!child) {
  1927	        missingChildren += 1;
  1928	        lineage.active_child_message_ids = activeDelegationChildIds(lineage).filter((id) => id !== childId);
  1929	        lineage.active_child_message_id = lineage.active_child_message_ids[0];
  1930	        lineage.last_failure_code = "DELEGATED_CHILD_RECEIPT_MISSING";
  1931	        continue;
  1932	      }
  1933	      if (terminalStatuses.has(child.status)) {
  1934	        lineage = recordDelegationReceipt(lineage, child);
  1935	        if (!receipts.some((item: any) => item?.message_id === child.message_id)) {
  1936	          receipts = [...receipts, delegationReceiptEvidence(child)].slice(-24);
  1937	        }
  1938	      } else {
  1939	        liveChildren += 1;
  1940	      }
  1941	    }
  1942	
  1943	    verified.delegation_receipts = receipts;
  1944	    command.verified_current_state = verified;
  1945	    message = refreshMessage();
  1946	    message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  1947	    if (message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)) {
  1948	      message.payload = {
  1949	        ...(message.payload as Record<string, unknown>),
  1950	        verified_current_state: verified,
  1951	      };
  1952	    }
  1953	
  1954	    if (missingChildren > 0) {
  1955	      message.activity = [...(Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : []), {
  1956	        event_id: randomUUID(),
  1957	        at: new Date().toISOString(),
  1958	        phase: "delegate",
  1959	        status: "failed" as const,
  1960	        label: `${missingChildren} delegated child receipt${missingChildren === 1 ? "" : "s"} missing · continuing parent with remaining evidence`,
  1961	      }].slice(-80);
  1962	    }
  1963	
  1964	    if (liveChildren > 0) {
  1965	      message.status = "QUEUED";
  1966	      message.next_attempt_at = new Date(Date.now() + 3_000).toISOString();
  1967	      message.last_heartbeat_at = new Date().toISOString();
  1968	      const lastEvent = Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity.at(-1) : null;
  1969	      const label = `${liveChildren} Geminex child lane${liveChildren === 1 ? "" : "s"} still executing · parent waiting for receipts`;
  1970	      if (lastEvent?.label !== label) {
  1971	        message.activity = [...(Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : []), {
  1972	          event_id: randomUUID(),
  1973	          at: message.last_heartbeat_at,
  1974	          phase: "delegate-wait",
  1975	          status: "running" as const,
  1976	          label,
  1977	        }].slice(-80);
  1978	      }
  1979	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  1980	      return message;
  1981	    }
  1982	
  1983	    liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  1984	  }
  1985	  const cancelled = (): GeminxMessage | null => {
  1986	    const latest = refreshMessage();
  1987	    return latest.status === "CANCELLED" ? latest : null;
  1988	  };
  1989	  const assertNotCancelled = (): void => {
  1990	    if (cancelled()) throw new Error("GEMINX_JOB_CANCELLED");
  1991	  };
  1992	  const activity = (event: { phase: string; status: "running" | "done" | "blocked" | "failed"; label: string; tool?: string; session_id?: string; url?: string; challenge?: string; requires_human?: boolean }) => {
  1993	    assertNotCancelled();
  1994	    message = refreshMessage();
  1995	    message.last_heartbeat_at = new Date().toISOString();
  1996	    const existing = Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : [];
  1997	    const duplicateHumanAlert = event.requires_human === true && existing.some(item =>
  1998	      item.requires_human === true &&
  1999	      item.session_id === event.session_id &&
  2000	      item.challenge === event.challenge &&
  2001	      item.status === "running"
  2002	    );
  2003	    message.activity = [...existing, {
  2004	      event_id: randomUUID(),
  2005	      at: message.last_heartbeat_at,
  2006	      ...event
  2007	    }].slice(-80);
  2008	    liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  2009	    if (event.requires_human === true && !duplicateHumanAlert) {
  2010	      void notifyFounder({
  2011	        title: "NEO needs you",
  2012	        body: event.label || "A running NEO task needs a human step.",
  2013	        job_id: message.message_id,
  2014	        status: "BLOCKED",
  2015	        url: "/hq/?job=" + encodeURIComponent(message.message_id),
  2016	        tag: "geminx-job-" + message.message_id
  2017	      }).catch(() => undefined);
  2018	    }
  2019	  };
  2020	  const drainFollowups = (): string[] => {
  2021	    assertNotCancelled();
  2022	    message = refreshMessage();
  2023	    const pending = (Array.isArray(message.followups) ? message.followups : []).filter(item => item.status === "PENDING");
  2024	    if (!pending.length) return [];
  2025	    const now = new Date().toISOString();
  2026	    for (const item of pending) {
  2027	      item.status = "CONSUMED";
  2028	      item.consumed_at = now;
  2029	    }
  2030	    message.last_heartbeat_at = now;
  2031	    liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  2032	    return pending.map(item => item.text);
  2033	  };
  2034	
  2035	  const retrySameJob = (error: unknown, failureFingerprint?: string): GeminxMessage => {
  2036	    message = refreshMessage();
  2037	    if (message.status === "CANCELLED") return message;
  2038	    const attempt = Math.max(1, Number(message.attempt_count ?? 1));
  2039	    const detail = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
  2040	
  2041	    const blockRetry = (
  2042	      code: string,
  2043	      label: string,
  2044	      output: string,
  2045	      founderActionRequired = true
  2046	    ): GeminxMessage => {
  2047	      const now = new Date().toISOString();
  2048	      message.status = "BLOCKED";
  2049	      message.completed_at = now;
  2050	      delete message.next_attempt_at;
  2051	      message.receipt_id = `receipt-${message.message_id}`;
  2052	      message.last_error = detail.slice(0, 800);
  2053	      message.last_heartbeat_at = now;
  2054	      message.activity = [...(Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : []), {
  2055	        event_id: randomUUID(),
  2056	        at: now,
  2057	        phase: "retry-circuit",
  2058	        status: "blocked" as const,
  2059	        label
  2060	      }].slice(-80);
  2061	      const isAgWorker = command.office_agent === "ag" || String(command.conversation_id || "").startsWith("ag-");
  2062	      const existingMessage = typeof (message.reply_payload as Record<string, unknown> | undefined)?.message === "string" &&
  2063	        ((message.reply_payload as Record<string, unknown>).message as string).trim()
  2064	        ? ((message.reply_payload as Record<string, unknown>).message as string)
  2065	        : undefined;
  2066	      message.reply_payload = {
  2067	        status: "BLOCKED",
  2068	        worker: isAgWorker ? "cloud-ag-worker" : "geminx-inbox-worker",
  2069	        route: isAgWorker ? "ag-autonomous-companion" : "geminx-autonomous-companion",
  2070	        provider: isAgWorker ? "Google Antigravity" : "DeepSeek",
  2071	        code,
  2072	        founder_action_required: founderActionRequired,
  2073	        ...(founderActionRequired ? {} : retryCircuitBlockerMetadata(code, latestSelfRepairFailureClass(message))),
  2074	        ...(existingMessage ? { message: existingMessage } : {}),
  2075	        output
  2076	      };
  2077	
  2078	      // A provider/circuit receipt is structured telemetry, not an LLM answer.
  2079	      // Do not manufacture a conversational assistant turn when inference failed.
  2080	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  2081	      return message;
  2082	    };
  2083	
  2084	    // Intercept ambiguous provider outcomes (including Google managed Antigravity in-progress/incomplete/502
  2085	    // and internal model-finalisation errors) before ALL generic HTTP 502 and retry-circuit branches,
  2086	    // even when failureFingerprint is populated. Never replay a possibly executed mutation.
  2087	    if (ambiguousProviderOutcome(error)) {
  2088	      return blockRetry(
  2089	        "GEMINX_PROVIDER_OUTCOME_AMBIGUOUS",
  2090	        "Provider outcome ambiguous · replay suppressed",
  2091	        "The provider request may already have been accepted or billed, so GeminX will not replay it blindly. The same objective and evidence remain preserved for internal reconciliation; Founder action is not required.",
  2092	        false
  2093	      );
  2094	    }
  2095	
  2096	    if (failureFingerprint) {
  2097	      const contract = message.objective_contract;
  2098	      if (contract) {
  2099	        // CommsStore keeps lifetime recovery_attempts monotonic. A reconciled,
  2100	        // explicitly Founder-resumed job instead consumes its own bounded
  2101	        // recovery window; prior failures remain preserved on the contract.
  2102	        const recoveryPayload = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
  2103	          ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown> : {};
  2104	        const windows = Array.isArray(recoveryPayload.founder_resume_windows)
  2105	          ? recoveryPayload.founder_resume_windows : [];
  2106	        const recentWindow = windows.length > 0 ? windows[windows.length - 1] : null;
  2107	        const isWindow = recentWindow && typeof recentWindow === "object" && !Array.isArray(recentWindow) &&
  2108	          typeof (recentWindow as Record<string, unknown>).window_attempts === "number";
  2109	        const recovery = isWindow
  2110	          ? planFounderResumeRecovery(contract, Number((recentWindow as Record<string, unknown>).window_attempts))
  2111	          : resolveDurableRecovery(contract);
  2112	        message.objective_contract = recovery.contract;
  2113	        if (isWindow) {
  2114	          message.payload = {
  2115	            ...recoveryPayload,
  2116	            founder_resume_windows: [
  2117	              ...windows.slice(0, -1),
  2118	              {
  2119	                ...(recentWindow as Record<string, unknown>),
  2120	                window_attempts: (recovery as ReturnType<typeof planFounderResumeRecovery>).window_attempts
  2121	              }
  2122	            ]
  2123	          };
  2124	        }
  2125	        if (recovery.action === "ATTEMPT_FALLBACK_CAPABILITY") {
  2126	          // The existing retry circuit retires the failed fingerprint. Its
  2127	          // cumulative objective-turn and model-spend ceilings remain intact.
  2128	          armAutonomousStrategyChange(message, failureFingerprint, 1);
  2129	          const now = new Date().toISOString();
  2130	          message.status = "QUEUED";
  2131	          delete message.completed_at;
  2132	          delete message.receipt_id;
  2133	          delete message.reply_payload;
  2134	          message.next_attempt_at = new Date(Date.now() + 5000).toISOString();
  2135	          message.last_error = detail.slice(0, 800);
  2136	          message.last_heartbeat_at = now;
  2137	          message.activity = [...(message.activity ?? []), {
  2138	            event_id: randomUUID(),
  2139	            at: now,
  2140	            phase: "autonomous-fallback",
  2141	            status: "running" as const,
  2142	            label: "Semantic stall: retired failed tool strategy; attempting bounded recovery " +
  2143	              (isWindow ? (recovery as ReturnType<typeof planFounderResumeRecovery>).window_attempts : recovery.contract.recovery_attempts) + "/2"
  2144	          }].slice(-80);
  2145	          liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  2146	          return message;
  2147	        }
  2148	        const payload = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
  2149	          ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown> : {};
  2150	        message.payload = {
  2151	          ...payload,
  2152	          warden_correction_ticket: {
  2153	            type: "WARDEN_AUTO_CORRECTION_TICKET",
  2154	            objective_id: recovery.contract.objective_id,
  2155	            job_id: message.message_id,
  2156	            reason: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_SEMANTIC_STALL",
  2157	            failed_fingerprint: failureFingerprint,
  2158	            unverified_criteria: recovery.contract.acceptance_criteria
  2159	              .filter((item) => item.status !== "VERIFIED")
  2160	              .map((item) => item.description),
  2161	            failed_criterion_ids: recovery.contract.acceptance_criteria
  2162	              .filter((item) => item.status !== "VERIFIED" || !item.evidence_receipt)
  2163	              .map((item) => item.id),
  2164	            required_next_action: recovery.contract.next_action_pointer,
  2165	            created_at: new Date().toISOString()
  2166	          }
  2167	        };
  2168	      }
  2169	      const blocked = blockRetry(
  2170	        "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_SEMANTIC_STALL",
  2171	        "Semantic continuation stopped after bounded strategy recovery",
  2172	        "The run stalled and produced no usable output. Execution stopped without completing the requested objective; no additional paid retries are authorized.",
  2173	        false
  2174	      );
  2175	      const ticket = (blocked.payload as Record<string, unknown> | undefined)?.warden_correction_ticket;
  2176	      if (ticket && typeof ticket === "object" && !Array.isArray(ticket)) {
  2177	        // Include failure identity; a new fault on the same job must not vanish behind an older ticket.
  2178	        const correctionId = wardenCorrectionMessageId(blocked.message_id, failureFingerprint);
  2179	        const now = new Date().toISOString();
  2180	        const correction: GeminxMessage = {
  2181	          message_id: correctionId,
  2182	          correlation_id: blocked.correlation_id,
  2183	          sender: "newton",
  2184	          recipient: "ag",
  2185	          created_at: now,
  2186	          priority: "HIGH",
  2187	          type: "execution_fault_repair",
  2188	          payload: {
  2189	            fault_event_id: correctionId,
  2190	            fault_code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_SEMANTIC_STALL",
  2191	            fault_message: "The run stalled and produced no usable output. Execution stopped without completing requested objective.",
  2192	            parent_job_id: blocked.message_id,
  2193	            parent_objective: String(command?.objective || (blocked.payload as Record<string, unknown> | undefined)?.objective || ""),
  2194	            parent_payload_snapshot: blocked.payload,
  2195	            step_id: "WARDEN_CORRECTION",
  2196	            fault_class: "EXECUTION_CONTINUATION_STALL",
  2197	            repair_depth: 0,
  2198	            forensic_bundle: {
  2199	              founder_intent: command?.objective || null,
  2200	              serialized_payload: blocked.payload,
  2201	              telemetry_slice: getTelemetrySlice(blocked.correlation_id),
  2202	              correlation_id: blocked.correlation_id
  2203	            },
  2204	            warden_correction_ticket: ticket
  2205	          },
  2206	          requires_reply: true,
  2207	          status: "QUEUED",
  2208	          activity: [{
  2209	            event_id: randomUUID(),
  2210	            at: now,
  2211	            phase: "warden-correction",
  2212	            status: "running",
  2213	            label: "Queued verified internal-fault correction for existing Cloud AG"
  2214	          }]
  2215	        };
  2216	        const enqueue = liveStore.createMessageIfAbsent(correction);
  2217	        blocked.payload = {
  2218	          ...(blocked.payload as Record<string, unknown>),
  2219	          warden_correction_delivery: {
  2220	            message_id: correctionId,
  2221	            queued_or_already_present: Boolean(enqueue.message),
  2222	            ag_ack_verified: enqueue.message.status === "ACKNOWLEDGED" || enqueue.message.status === "COMPLETED",
  2223	            created: enqueue.created,
  2224	            at: now
  2225	          }
  2226	        };
  2227	        // Report the actual durable child state, not an optimistic claim that
  2228	        // an evidence attachment or autonomous fix completed the objective.
  2229	        const correctionState = enqueue.message?.status ?? "UNKNOWN";
  2230	        const deliveryConfirmed = ["QUEUED", "ACKNOWLEDGED", "RUNNING", "COMPLETED"].includes(correctionState);
  2231	        blocked.reply_payload = {
  2232	          ...(blocked.reply_payload && typeof blocked.reply_payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(blocked.reply_payload)
  2233	            ? blocked.reply_payload as Record<string, unknown> : {}),
  2234	          output: deliveryConfirmed
  2235	            ? "Execution stopped without completing the requested objective. No verified output was produced. Warden ticket " + correctionId + " exists in durable state " + correctionState + "; repair and original capability recovery are NOT verified."
  2236	            : "Execution stopped without completing the requested objective. No verified output was produced. Warden ticket could not be confirmed; automatic repair is NOT verified.",
  2237	          warden_correction_message_id: correctionId,
  2238	          warden_correction_status: correctionState,
  2239	          recovery_verified: false
  2240	        };
  2241	        liveStore.saveMessage(blocked);
  2242	      }
  2243	      return blocked;
  2244	    }
  2245	
  2246	    // HTTP 400 invalid tool-call transcript cannot improve through identical
  2247	    // paid retries. Preserve the original objective and evidence for a code fix.
  2248	    if (/\b(?:400|invalid_request_error)\b/i.test(detail) &&
  2249	        /tool_calls|tool_call_id|tool messages|tool responses/i.test(detail)) {
  2250	      return blockRetry(
  2251	        "GEMINX_DEEPSEEK_TOOL_TRANSCRIPT_REPAIR_REQUIRED",
  2252	        "DeepSeek rejected the tool-call transcript · no identical paid replay",
  2253	        "The same task and completed tool receipts are preserved. A corrected transcript and the exact failing-probe retest must pass before execution resumes.",
  2254	        false
  2255	      );
  2256	    }
  2257	
  2258	    if (deepSeekCreditExhausted(error)) {
  2259	      return blockRetry(
  2260	        "DEEPSEEK_PROVIDER_CREDIT_EXHAUSTED",
  2261	        "DeepSeek balance unavailable · paid retry circuit open",
  2262	        "DeepSeek returned a credit/balance boundary. The same task is preserved and will not autonomously re-probe until an explicit provider-state change resumes it."
  2263	      );
  2264	    }
  2265	
  2266	    if (deepSeekTaskSpendBudgetReached(error)) {
  2267	      return blockRetry(
  2268	        "DEEPSEEK_OBJECTIVE_SPEND_BUDGET_REACHED",
  2269	        "DeepSeek cumulative task spend ceiling reached · no paid retry",
  2270	        "This objective reached its total DeepSeek spend ceiling across all attempts and resumes. Evidence is preserved; no automatic provider retries are permitted."
  2271	      );
  2272	    }
  2273	
  2274	    if (!failureFingerprint && deepSeekDailySpendReservationBusy(error)) {
  2275	      const contentionCount = recordSpendReservationContention(message, detail);
  2276	      if (contentionCount >= geminxAutonomousRetryLimit()) {
  2277	        return blockRetry(
  2278	          "GEMINX_SPEND_RESERVATION_CONTENTION_CIRCUIT_OPEN",
  2279	          `Spend reservation contention persisted for ${contentionCount} bounded admissions · execution suspended`,
  2280	          `Other in-flight DeepSeek requests temporarily consumed the remaining reservation headroom ${contentionCount} times. No provider request was sent for these admissions and semantic retry counters were not consumed. GeminX has suspended this objective for internal scheduling repair rather than exceeding the daily spend ceiling.`,
  2281	          false
  2282	        );
  2283	      }
  2284	      const delayMs = retryDelayMs(contentionCount);
  2285	      const nextAttempt = new Date(Date.now() + delayMs).toISOString();
  2286	      message.status = "QUEUED";
  2287	      delete message.completed_at;
  2288	      delete message.receipt_id;
  2289	      delete message.reply_payload;
  2290	      message.next_attempt_at = nextAttempt;
  2291	      message.last_error = detail.slice(0, 800);
  2292	      message.last_heartbeat_at = new Date().toISOString();
  2293	      message.activity = [...(Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : []), {
  2294	        event_id: randomUUID(),
  2295	        at: message.last_heartbeat_at,
  2296	        phase: "spend-reservation-backoff",
  2297	        status: "running" as const,
  2298	        label: `DeepSeek spend headroom temporarily reserved by concurrent work · retrying same objective after bounded backoff ${contentionCount}/${geminxAutonomousRetryLimit()}`
  2299	      }].slice(-80);
  2300	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  2301	      return message;
  2302	    }
  2303	
  2304	    if (!failureFingerprint && transientExecutionError(error)) {
  2305	      const store = new ConversationStore({ root: liveStore.runtimeRoot });
  2306	      const checkpoint = store.getExecutionCheckpoint(message.message_id);
  2307	      const hasUnresolvedTools = Boolean(checkpoint && unresolvedNativeToolCallIds(checkpoint.messages).length > 0);
  2308	      const hasPendingTool = Boolean(checkpoint && (checkpoint.state === "PENDING_TOOL" || checkpoint.pending_tool_id !== null));
  2309	      if (hasPendingTool || hasUnresolvedTools) {
  2310	        return blockRetry(
  2311	          "GEMINX_MUTATION_OUTCOME_UNCERTAIN_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED",
  2312	          "Mutation outcome uncertain · retry suppressed",
  2313	          "An external mutation or tool action was pending or unresolved when the provider returned a transient fault. GeminX will not replay it blindly. Original objective and checkpoint preserved for internal reconciliation.",
  2314	          false
  2315	        );
  2316	      }
  2317	
  2318	      const providerFailures = recordProviderTransientFailure(message, detail);
  2319	      if (providerFailures >= geminxAutonomousRetryLimit()) {
  2320	        return blockRetry(
  2321	          "GEMINX_PROVIDER_TRANSIENT_CIRCUIT_OPEN",
  2322	          `Provider transient circuit opened after ${providerFailures} equivalent failures`,
  2323	          `The provider/runtime transport failed ${providerFailures} bounded times without a successful model return. Semantic stagnation counters were not consumed. GeminX has suspended paid provider calls for internal repair rather than asking the Founder to continue.`,
  2324	          false
  2325	        );
  2326	      }
  2327	      const delayMs = retryDelayMs(providerFailures);
  2328	      const nextAttempt = new Date(Date.now() + delayMs).toISOString();
  2329	      message.status = "QUEUED";
  2330	      delete message.completed_at;
  2331	      delete message.receipt_id;
  2332	      delete message.reply_payload;
  2333	      message.next_attempt_at = nextAttempt;
  2334	      message.last_error = detail.slice(0, 800);
  2335	      message.last_heartbeat_at = new Date().toISOString();
  2336	      message.activity = [...(Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : []), {
  2337	        event_id: randomUUID(),
  2338	        at: message.last_heartbeat_at,
  2339	        phase: "provider-backoff",
  2340	        status: "running" as const,
  2341	        label: `Transient provider/runtime failure · bounded backoff ${providerFailures}/${geminxAutonomousRetryLimit()} · semantic retry counters unchanged`
  2342	      }].slice(-80);
  2343	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  2344	      return message;
  2345	    }
  2346	
  2347	    const retryControl = recordRetryCircuitFailure(message, detail, failureFingerprint);
  2348	    const retryState = retryCircuitProgressState(message);
  2349	    const strategyChanges = retryState?.strategy_change_count ?? 0;
  2350	    const repeatedRoute =
  2351	      retryControl.cumulative_failure_occurrences >= geminxCumulativeFailureLimit();
  2352	    const stagnantRoute =
  2353	      retryControl.turns_without_progress >= geminxNoProgressTurnLimit();
  2354	
  2355	    if ((repeatedRoute || stagnantRoute) && strategyChanges < geminxStrategyChangeLimit()) {
  2356	      armAutonomousStrategyChange(
  2357	        message,
  2358	        retryControl.fingerprint,
  2359	        retryControl.cumulative_failure_occurrences
  2360	      );
  2361	      const nextAttempt = new Date().toISOString();
  2362	      message.status = "QUEUED";
  2363	      delete message.completed_at;
  2364	      delete message.receipt_id;
  2365	      delete message.reply_payload;
  2366	      message.next_attempt_at = nextAttempt;
  2367	      message.last_error = detail.slice(0, 800);
  2368	      message.last_heartbeat_at = nextAttempt;
  2369	      message.activity = [...(Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : []), {
  2370	        event_id: randomUUID(),
  2371	        at: nextAttempt,
  2372	        phase: "strategy-change",
  2373	        status: "running" as const,
  2374	        label: repeatedRoute
  2375	          ? `Repeated failure route retired after ${retryControl.cumulative_failure_occurrences} equivalent outcomes · changing tool/hypothesis path on the SAME Founder objective`
  2376	          : `No-progress window reached ${retryControl.turns_without_progress} turns · changing tool/hypothesis path on the SAME Founder objective`
  2377	      }].slice(-80);
  2378	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  2379	      return message;
  2380	    }
  2381	
  2382	    if (repeatedRoute || stagnantRoute) {
  2383	      const lastFailureClass = latestSelfRepairFailureClass(message);
  2384	      if (lastFailureClass === "EXTERNAL_RUNTIME") {
  2385	        return blockRetry(
  2386	          "GEMINX_EXTERNAL_RUNTIME_BOUNDARY",
  2387	          `External runtime remained unreachable after ${strategyChanges} materially different recovery strategies`,
  2388	          "Canonical recovery paths were changed rather than blindly repeated, but the external physical worker/host still has no mechanically verified heartbeat. Provider spending is stopped until that runtime changes state.",
  2389	          false
  2390	        );
  2391	      }
  2392	      return blockRetry(
  2393	        "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_STRATEGY_EXHAUSTED",
  2394	        `Recovery strategies exhausted after ${strategyChanges} materially different route changes`,
  2395	        `GeminX retired repeated failure routes and tried materially different recovery strategies without verified progress. The objective is preserved, but further paid execution is suspended until new evidence changes the route. This is not the old three-repeat retry circuit.`,
  2396	        false
  2397	      );
  2398	    }
  2399	
  2400	    const delayMs = retryDelayMs(Math.min(retryControl.consecutive_same_failure, 2));
  2401	    const nextAttempt = new Date(Date.now() + delayMs).toISOString();
  2402	    message.status = "QUEUED";
  2403	    delete message.completed_at;
  2404	    delete message.receipt_id;
  2405	    delete message.reply_payload;
  2406	    message.next_attempt_at = nextAttempt;
  2407	    message.last_error = detail.slice(0, 800);
  2408	    message.last_heartbeat_at = new Date().toISOString();
  2409	    message.activity = [...(Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : []), {
  2410	      event_id: randomUUID(),
  2411	      at: message.last_heartbeat_at,
  2412	      phase: "retry",
  2413	      status: "running" as const,
  2414	      label: `Temporary provider/runtime interruption · retrying same task in ${Math.ceil(delayMs / 1000)}s`
  2415	    }].slice(-80);
  2416	    liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  2417	    return message;
  2418	  };
  2419	
  2420	  const complete = (
  2421	    status: "COMPLETED" | "BLOCKED" | "FAILED",
  2422	    replyPayload: Record<string, unknown>
  2423	  ) => {
  2424	    message = refreshMessage();
  2425	    if (message.status === "CANCELLED") return message;
  2426	    const latePending = (message.followups ?? []).some(item => item.status === "PENDING");
  2427	    if (status === "COMPLETED" && latePending) {
  2428	      message.status = "QUEUED";
  2429	      // A late Founder follow-up continues the same durable objective and therefore
  2430	      // preserves its cumulative attempt budget.
  2431	      delete message.completed_at;
  2432	      delete message.receipt_id;
  2433	      delete message.reply_payload;
  2434	      message.next_attempt_at = new Date().toISOString();
  2435	      message.last_heartbeat_at = new Date().toISOString();
  2436	      message.activity = [...(message.activity ?? []), {
  2437	        event_id: randomUUID(),
  2438	        at: message.last_heartbeat_at,
  2439	        phase: "followup",
  2440	        status: "running" as const,
  2441	        label: "New founder instruction arrived at completion · continuing the same task"
  2442	      }].slice(-80);
  2443	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  2444	      return message;
  2445	    }
  2446	    message.status = status;
  2447	    message.completed_at = new Date().toISOString();
  2448	    delete message.next_attempt_at;
  2449	    delete message.last_error;
  2450	    message.last_heartbeat_at = message.completed_at;
  2451	    message.activity = [...(Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : []), {
  2452	      event_id: randomUUID(),
  2453	      at: message.completed_at,
  2454	      phase: "complete",
  2455	      status: status === "COMPLETED" ? ("done" as const) : status === "BLOCKED" ? ("blocked" as const) : ("failed" as const),
  2456	      label: status === "COMPLETED" ? "Finished and verified" : status === "BLOCKED" ? "Stopped at a real blocker" : "Task failed verification"
  2457	    }].slice(-80);
  2458	    const isAgJob = command.office_agent === "ag" || String(command.conversation_id || "").startsWith("ag-");
  2459	    const effectiveProvider = String(
  2460	      replyPayload.provider || (
  2461	        command.model_preference === "gemini-pro"
  2462	          ? "Google Antigravity"
  2463	          : isOpenAiCompanionModelPreference(String(command.model_preference || ""))
  2464	            ? "OpenAI"
  2465	            : "DeepSeek"
  2466	      )
  2467	    );
  2468	    const effectiveModel = String(replyPayload.model || command.model_preference || "deepseek");
  2469	    const effectiveWorker = String(replyPayload.worker || "geminx-inbox-worker");
  2470	    const effectiveRoute = String(replyPayload.route || (isAgJob ? "ag-autonomous-companion" : "geminx-autonomous-companion"));
  2471	    message.reply_payload = {
  2472	      status,
  2473	      worker: effectiveWorker,
  2474	      route: effectiveRoute,
  2475	      provider: effectiveProvider,
  2476	      model: effectiveModel,
  2477	      ...replyPayload
  2478	    };
  2479	    if (status === "COMPLETED") {
  2480	      // Verifier owns terminality: failed or blocked model narratives are not
  2481	      // evidence, and must never seal a supposedly completed objective.
  2482	      const checkpointStore = new ConversationStore({ root: liveStore.runtimeRoot });
  2483	      // Free-tier, Astra, and preexisting delegated completions legitimately
  2484	      // have no DeepSeek transcript. Never require a nonexistent checkpoint.
  2485	      if (checkpointStore.getExecutionCheckpoint(message.message_id)) {
  2486	        checkpointStore.markVerifiedExecutionTerminal(message.message_id, message.receipt_id ?? ("receipt-" + message.message_id));
  2487	      }
  2488	    }
  2489	
  2490	    // Durable verifier truth owns the Founder narrative. Never leak internal engine directives.
  2491	    // Founder communication mandate: The LLM model's message leads on every turn.
  2492	    // If the model produced a natural response, present it directly rather than synthetic status codes.
  2493	    const terminalNarrativeCandidates = status === "COMPLETED"
  2494	      ? [replyPayload.message, replyPayload.summary, replyPayload.output]
  2495	      : [replyPayload.output, replyPayload.summary, replyPayload.message];
  2496	    const safeCandidate = terminalNarrativeCandidates
  2497	      .map(value => typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "")
  2498	      .find(value => Boolean(value) && !isInternalEngineLeak(value));
  2499	
  2500	    // No fake model narration: only a real provider response or evidence-backed
  2501	    // runtime output may become an assistant turn. Structured status remains in
  2502	    // the receipt and the HQ status UI when there is no usable model answer.
  2503	    const terminalFounderNarrative = safeCandidate || "";
  2504	
  2505	    // Resolve conversation ID even if missing from command directly
  2506	    let targetConversationId = command.conversation_id;
  2507	    if (!targetConversationId) {
  2508	      const payloadObj = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
  2509	        ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown>
  2510	        : null;
  2511	      targetConversationId = typeof payloadObj?.conversation_id === "string" && payloadObj.conversation_id.trim()
  2512	        ? payloadObj.conversation_id.trim()
  2513	        : null;
  2514	    }
  2515	    if (!targetConversationId) {
  2516	      const foundConv = conversationStore.findByRunId(message.message_id);
  2517	      if (foundConv) targetConversationId = foundConv.conversation_id;
  2518	    }
  2519	
  2520	    // A job can yield at most one Founder reply. If the foreground already
  2521	    // answered, background terminal telemetry cannot create another chat bubble.
  2522	    if (targetConversationId) {
  2523	      try {
  2524	        let record = conversationStore.get(targetConversationId);
  2525	        if (!record) record = conversationStore.create(targetConversationId);
  2526	        const transcriptText = terminalFounderNarrative;
  2527	        const last = record.turns[record.turns.length - 1];
  2528	        // Only a terminal answer for THIS job may suppress its final reply.
  2529	        // The initial acknowledgement and later chat turns have other IDs.
  2530	        const alreadyAnswered = record.turns.some((turn) =>
  2531	          turn.role === "assistant" && turn.message_id === message.message_id
  2532	        );
  2533	        if (transcriptText && !isInternalEngineLeak(transcriptText) && !alreadyAnswered &&
  2534	            !(last?.role === "assistant" && last.message_id === message.message_id)) {
  2535	          const assistantAttachments = parseFounderAttachments(replyPayload.attachments);
  2536	          const keepActiveRun = status === "BLOCKED" && replyPayload.founder_action_required === true;
  2537	          conversationStore.appendTurn(
  2538	            record,
  2539	            {
  2540	              role: "assistant",
  2541	              message_id: message.message_id,
  2542	              content: transcriptText,
  2543	              at: new Date().toISOString(),
  2544	              ...(assistantAttachments.length ? { attachments: assistantAttachments } : {})
  2545	            },
  2546	            {
  2547	              active_run_id: keepActiveRun ? message.message_id : null,
  2548	              domain: record.domain || "maintenance",
  2549	              summary: command.objective
  2550	            }
  2551	          );
  2552	        }
  2553	        delete (message.reply_payload as Record<string, unknown>).delivery_pending;
  2554	      } catch (conversationError) {
  2555	        console.error(
  2556	          `[geminx-inbox-worker] conversation_persist_failed message_id=${message.message_id} reason=${
  2557	            conversationError instanceof Error ? conversationError.message : String(conversationError)
  2558	          }`
  2559	        );
  2560	        (message.reply_payload as Record<string, unknown>).delivery_pending = true;
  2561	      }
  2562	    }
  2563	
  2564	    const founderActionRequired =
  2565	      status === "BLOCKED" && replyPayload.founder_action_required === true;
  2566	    const internalAutonomyPause =
  2567	      status === "BLOCKED" && !founderActionRequired;
  2568	    const notificationTitle =
  2569	      status === "COMPLETED" ? "GeminX task complete"
  2570	        : internalAutonomyPause ? "GeminX internal circuit paused"
  2571	          : status === "BLOCKED" ? "GeminX needs you"
  2572	            : "GeminX task failed";
  2573	    const notificationBody = terminalFounderNarrative.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().slice(0, 180);
  2574	    // Every Founder-submitted durable task gets a terminal notification even
  2575	    // when the failure was internal. Non-Founder maintenance incidents remain
  2576	    // silent unless a protected human action is genuinely required.
  2577	    const isFounderSubmittedJob = message.message_id.startsWith("founder-job-")
  2578	      && Boolean(command.conversation_id);
  2579	    if (!internalAutonomyPause || isFounderSubmittedJob) {
  2580	      void notifyFounder({
  2581	        title: notificationTitle,
  2582	        body: notificationBody,
  2583	        job_id: message.message_id,
  2584	        status,
  2585	        url: "/hq/?job=" + encodeURIComponent(message.message_id),
  2586	        tag: "geminx-job-" + message.message_id
  2587	      }).catch(() => undefined);
  2588	    }
  2589	
  2590	    // Canonical durable jobs share the same Sentinel incident lane as chat.
  2591	    // Ignore founder-only boundaries and AG's own job to avoid escalation loops.
  2592	    if ((status === "FAILED" || internalAutonomyPause) && command.office_agent !== "ag") {
  2593	      try {
  2594	        const rawErrorCode = String(replyPayload.code ?? (status === "FAILED" ? "JOB_VERIFICATION_FAILED" : "JOB_BLOCKED_INTERNAL"));
  2595	        const errorCode = rawErrorCode.toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9_-]/g, "_").slice(0, 100);
  2596	        escalateToAg({
  2597	          reporting_agent: command.office_agent,
  2598	          company_scope: command.company_scope,
  2599	          task_id: message.message_id,
  2600	          failing_tool: "geminx_durable_job",
  2601	          error_code: errorCode,
  2602	          target_resource: message.message_id,
  2603	          evidence: [`job=${message.message_id} terminal_status=${status}`],
  2604	          recommended_action: "Inspect the durable run and its receipts; repair the first executable fault. Do not close on model prose.",
  2605	        }, liveStore.runtimeRoot);
  2606	      } catch {
  2607	        // Original durable terminal receipt remains authoritative.
  2608	      }
  2609	    }
  2610	
  2611	    return message;
  2612	  };
  2613	
  2614	  if (!authorisedAutonomousSender(message, liveStore)) {
  2615	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  2616	      code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_SENDER_FORBIDDEN",
  2617	      output: "Only an authorised operator (Rob, AG, Cloud AG, or Newton) may invoke autonomous GeminX self-maintenance work."
  2618	    });
  2619	  }
  2620	
  2621	  if (command.authority !== GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_AUTHORITY) {
  2622	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  2623	      code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_AUTHORITY_INVALID",
  2624	      output: "The founder-approved GeminX self-maintenance authority contract is missing or invalid."
  2625	    });
  2626	  }
  2627	
  2628	  // Deterministic control-plane cancellation. Cancellation must mutate durable
  2629	  // message state before any model/tool execution; a model saying "cancelled"
  2630	  // is not cancellation. This prevents stale autonomous jobs from resurrecting
  2631	  // through retry/restart recovery after they have been superseded.
  2632	  if (command.cancel_message_ids.length > 0) {
  2633	    const now = new Date().toISOString();
  2634	    const cancelledIds: string[] = [];
  2635	    const alreadyTerminal: Array<{ message_id: string; status: string }> = [];
  2636	    const notFound: string[] = [];
  2637	
  2638	    for (const targetIdRaw of command.cancel_message_ids) {
  2639	      const targetId = String(targetIdRaw ?? "").trim();
  2640	      if (!targetId || targetId === message.message_id) continue;
  2641	      const target = liveStore.getMessage(targetId);
  2642	      if (!target) {
  2643	        notFound.push(targetId);
  2644	        continue;
  2645	      }
  2646	      if (!isPrimaryAgentRecipient(target.recipient)) {
  2647	        alreadyTerminal.push({ message_id: targetId, status: "RECIPIENT_NOT_GEMINX" });
  2648	        continue;
  2649	      }
  2650	      if (target.status === "COMPLETED" || target.status === "CANCELLED" || target.status === "EXPIRED") {
  2651	        alreadyTerminal.push({ message_id: targetId, status: target.status });
  2652	        continue;
  2653	      }
  2654	
  2655	      target.status = "CANCELLED";
  2656	      target.completed_at = now;
  2657	      delete target.next_attempt_at;
  2658	      delete target.last_error;
  2659	      target.last_heartbeat_at = now;
  2660	      target.receipt_id = `receipt-${target.message_id}`;
  2661	      target.reply_payload = {
  2662	        status: "CANCELLED",
  2663	        worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  2664	        route: "deterministic-control-plane-cancel",
  2665	        code: "GEMINX_OBJECTIVE_SUPERSEDED",
  2666	        superseded_by: message.message_id,
  2667	      };
  2668	      target.activity = [...(Array.isArray(target.activity) ? target.activity : []), {
  2669	        event_id: randomUUID(),
  2670	        at: now,
  2671	        phase: "cancel",
  2672	        status: "done" as const,
  2673	        label: `Superseded by ${message.message_id} · durable retry disabled`,
  2674	      }].slice(-80);
  2675	      liveStore.saveMessage(target);
  2676	      cancelledIds.push(targetId);
  2677	    }
  2678	
  2679	    return complete("COMPLETED", {
  2680	      code: "GEMINX_CONTROL_PLANE_CANCELLATION_APPLIED",
  2681	      cancelled_message_ids: cancelledIds,
  2682	      already_terminal: alreadyTerminal,
  2683	      not_found: notFound,
  2684	      output: `Durably cancelled ${cancelledIds.length} GeminX objective(s). Cancelled jobs are excluded from retry/restart recovery.`,
  2685	    });
  2686	  }
  2687	
  2688	  const creatorFrontierOnly = /\b(?:geminx\s+)?game\s+creator\b|\broblox\b|\bcreator\s+(?:test|repair|play|publish|run|game)\b/i.test(
  2689	    [command.objective, ...command.acceptance_criteria, ...command.constraints].join(" ")
  2690	  );
  2691	
  2692	  // Autonomous jobs must never invent premium-model authority. However, an
  2693	  // explicit Founder-selected Astra route is persisted by Founder HQ as
  2694	  // requested_model=astra and must survive the durable queue so the THINK-only
  2695	  // Astra branch below can execute. Historical/self-selected premium jobs are
  2696	  // still normalized back to DeepSeek Flash.
  2697	  const founderExplicitAstra =
  2698	    command.model_preference === "astra" &&
  2699	    String(command.verified_current_state?.requested_model ?? "").trim().toLowerCase() === "astra" &&
  2700	    String(command.verified_current_state?.source ?? "").trim() === "founder_hq_companion";
  2701	  if (
  2702	    command.model_preference === "deepseek-v4-pro" ||
  2703	    (isOpenAiCompanionModelPreference(command.model_preference) && !founderExplicitAstra)
  2704	  ) {
  2705	    command.model_preference = "deepseek";
  2706	    if (message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)) {
  2707	      message.payload = {
  2708	        ...(message.payload as Record<string, unknown>),
  2709	        model_preference: "deepseek",
  2710	      };
  2711	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  2712	    }
  2713	    activity({
  2714	      phase: "model-route",
  2715	      status: "running",
  2716	      label: "Removed autonomous premium-model selection · continuing same objective on DeepSeek Flash"
  2717	    });
  2718	  }
  2719	
  2720	  if (creatorFrontierOnly && !isOpenAiCompanionModelPreference(command.model_preference)) {
  2721	    command.allow_free_model = false;
  2722	    command.model_preference = "deepseek";
  2723	    if (message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)) {
  2724	      message.payload = {
  2725	        ...(message.payload as Record<string, unknown>),
  2726	        allow_free_model: false,
  2727	        model_preference: "deepseek",
  2728	      };
  2729	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  2730	    }
  2731	    activity({
  2732	      phase: "model-route",
  2733	      status: "running",
  2734	      label: "Creator recovery locked to DeepSeek Flash · tool-driven repair remains bounded"
  2735	    });
  2736	  }
  2737	
  2738	  // Tier-0 free inference is deliberately narrower than the main GeminX runtime.
  2739	  // It receives PUBLIC, no-tools tasks only, with no Brain snapshot, attachments,
  2740	  // credentials or verified private state. Any provider failure/self-escalation
  2741	  // falls through to the normal DeepSeek -> Astra ladder on this SAME job.
  2742	  const availabilityAtStart = executorAvailabilityFromEnv();
  2743	  const freeAlreadyTried = lineage.attempts.some((attempt) => attempt.executor === "openrouter-free");
  2744	  if (
  2745	    availabilityAtStart.openrouterFree &&
  2746	    !freeAlreadyTried &&
  2747	    isFreeLaneEligible({
  2748	      allow_free_model: command.allow_free_model,
  2749	      data_classification: command.data_classification,
  2750	      requires_tools: command.requires_tools,
  2751	      has_attachments: command.attachments.length > 0,
  2752	      protected_surface: /protected|production mutation|credential|secret|private|customer/i.test(command.constraints.join(" ")),
  2753	    })
  2754	  ) {
  2755	    activity({ phase: "tier0", status: "running", label: "Routing public no-tools work to the free Tier-0 model pool" });
  2756	    try {
  2757	      const free = await invokeOpenRouterFree({
  2758	        objective: command.objective,
  2759	        acceptance_criteria: command.acceptance_criteria,
  2760	        constraints: command.constraints,
  2761	      });
  2762	      lineage = recordDelegationDispatch(lineage, "openrouter-free", "public_no_tools_tier0");
  2763	      lineage.attempts[lineage.attempts.length - 1] = {
  2764	        ...lineage.attempts[lineage.attempts.length - 1],
  2765	        status: "COMPLETED",
  2766	        receipt_id: free.receipt.id,
  2767	      };
  2768	      message = refreshMessage();
  2769	      message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  2770	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  2771	      return complete("COMPLETED", {
  2772	        code: "GEMINX_TIER0_FREE_RESULT",

  3408	      let fileModified = false;

  3418

  3436	          (exitCode === 0 && /\b(?:vitest|test)\b/i.test(callArgs))
  3437	        );

  3474	          if (match && match[1]) {
