  3479	          if (commitMatch && commitMatch[1]) {
  3480	            branchSha = commitMatch[1];

  3482	        }

  3485	      const ghToken = process.env.GEMINX_GITHUB_TOKEN || process.env.GITHUB_TOKEN || process.env.GEMINX_REPO_ACCESS_TOKEN;

  3499	            }
  3500	          }

  3502	          console.warn("[inbox-worker] V10 remote GitHub API check error:", fetchErr);
  3503	        }
  3504	      }

  3533	    let adjudication = adjudicateAutonomousTerminal({

  3536	      no_progress_observation_fuse: reply.no_progress_observation_fuse === true,

  3538	      objective: [command.objective, ...(command.acceptance_criteria ?? [])].join("\n"),
  3539	      tool_calls: (reply.tool_calls ?? []).map((call: any) => ({
  3540	        tool: call.tool,
  3541	        status: call.status,
  3542	        error: call.error ?? null,
  3543	        args: call.args,
  3544	        result: call.result ?? null,
  3545	      })),
  3546	    });
  3547	
  3548	    // Only the native tool registry's actual result objects may advance a
  3549	    // durable criterion. Model speech, returned text and naked success flags
  3550	    // cannot manufacture VERIFIED. Persist before terminal adjudication.
  3551	    message = refreshMessage();
  3552	    if (message.objective_contract) {
  3553	      const promotion = promoteDurableCriteriaFromToolReceipts(
  3554	        message.objective_contract,
  3555	        (reply.tool_calls ?? []).map((call: any) => ({
  3556	          tool: call.tool,
  3557	          status: call.status,
  3558	          args: call.args,
  3559	          result: call.result ?? null
  3560	        }))
  3561	      );
  3562	      if (promotion.newly_verified.length) {
  3563	        message.objective_contract = promotion.contract;
  3564	        message.activity = [...(message.activity ?? []), {
  3565	          event_id: randomUUID(),
  3566	          at: new Date().toISOString(),

  3595	      }
  3596	    }
  3597	
  3598	    if (adjudication.code === "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_INTERNAL_CONTEXT_PRESSURE") {

  3600	      // checkpoint. Fail closed until a replay-safe native checkpoint exists.
  3601	      const trace = registry.trace(correlationId);
  3602	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  3603	        code: "GEMINX_INTERNAL_CONTEXT_PRESSURE_CHECKPOINT_REQUIRED",

  3605	        blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",

  3607	        tool_trace: compactRetryToolTrace(trace)
  3608	      });
  3609	    }
  3610	
  3611	    if (adjudication.status !== "COMPLETED" && adjudication.status !== "BLOCKED") {
  3612	      const availabilityRaw = executorAvailabilityFromEnv();

  3614	        ? { ...availabilityRaw, openai: false }
  3615	        : availabilityRaw;
  3616	      const trace = registry.trace(correlationId);
  3617	      const agTool =
  3618	        !creatorFrontierOnly && availability.ag
  3619	          ? selectAgToolFailover(trace, lineage)
  3620	          : null;
  3621	
  3622	      // AG is only used where its cloud worker has a mechanically proven executor
  3623	      // for the exact failed tool family. Arbitrary objectives are never "delegated"
  3624	      // to AG merely to make the graph look autonomous.
  3625	      if (agTool) {
  3626	        const child = buildAgDelegatedToolMessage(
  3627	          message,
  3628	          agTool,
  3629	          command.objective,
  3630	          command.acceptance_criteria
  3631	        );

  3636	          `failed_tool:${agTool.tool}:${adjudication.code}`,
  3637	          child.message_id,
  3638	          agTool.tool

  3640	        lineage.last_failure_code = adjudication.code;
  3641	        message = refreshMessage();
  3642	        message.delegation_lineage = lineage;

  3644	        delete message.completed_at;
  3645	        delete message.receipt_id;

  3650	          event_id: randomUUID(),

  3658	        return message;
  3659	      }
  3660	
  3661	
  3662	    }
  3663	
  3664	    if (adjudication.status === "RETRY") {
  3665	      const trace = registry.trace(correlationId);
  3666	      const mutationObjective = objectiveRequiresMutationEvidence(command.objective, command.read_only);
  3667	      const scratchObjective = mutationObjective && /\b(?:scratch|temporary file|temp file)\b/i.test(command.objective);
  3668	      const mutationProgress = scratchObjective
  3669	        ? trace.some((entry) => entry.status === "succeeded" && /^(?:geminx_scratch_write|geminx_scratch_delete)$/.test(entry.tool))

  3671	      const failedMutation = hasFailedMutationAttempt(trace);

  3677	      const observationProgress = !mutationObjective && !mutationProgress && !failedMutation;
  3678	      if (mutationProgress || observationProgress) {
  3679	        // Always refresh BEFORE mutating the durable retry state. Refreshing

  3682	        message = refreshMessage();
  3683	        const progressed = mutationProgress
  3684	          ? (recordRetryCircuitMaterialProgress(message), true)
  3685	          : recordRetryCircuitObservationProgress(message, trace);
  3686	        if (progressed) {
  3687	          liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  3688	          if (message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)) {
  3689	            const progressedVerified = (message.payload as Record<string, unknown>).verified_current_state;
  3690	            if (progressedVerified && typeof progressedVerified === "object" && !Array.isArray(progressedVerified)) {
  3691	              command.verified_current_state = { ...(progressedVerified as Record<string, unknown>) };
  3692	            }
  3693	          }
  3694	        }
  3695	      }
  3696	      const verified = command.verified_current_state && typeof command.verified_current_state === "object"
  3697	        ? { ...command.verified_current_state }
  3698	        : {};
  3699	      const repairs = Array.isArray(verified.self_repair_receipts)
  3700	        ? verified.self_repair_receipts.filter((item) => item && typeof item === "object")
  3701	        : [];
  3702	      verified.self_repair_receipts = [...repairs, {
  3703	        attempt: Number(message.attempt_count ?? 1),
  3704	        provider: reply.provider,
  3705	        model: reply.model,
  3706	        code: adjudication.code,
  3707	        failure_class: adjudication.failure_class ?? "UNKNOWN",
  3708	        degraded_tool_calls: adjudication.degraded_tool_calls,
  3709	        tool_trace: compactRetryToolTrace(trace),
  3710	        last_attempt_summary: visibleMessage.slice(0, 4_000),
  3711	        observed_at: new Date().toISOString(),

  3713	      const disproven = buildDisprovenApproachRecord({
  3714	        code: adjudication.code,
  3715	        failure_class: adjudication.failure_class,
  3716	        tool_calls: retryFingerprintToolCalls(trace),
  3717	      });
  3718	      const graveyard = mergeDisprovenApproach(verified.disproven_approaches, disproven);
  3719	      verified.disproven_approaches = graveyard.records;
  3720	      if (graveyard.repeated) {
  3721	        verified.repeated_failure_fingerprint = {
  3722	          fingerprint: disproven.fingerprint,

  3725	        };

  3727	      } else {
  3728	        delete verified.repeated_failure_fingerprint;

  3730	      }

  3732	      message = refreshMessage();
  3733	      message.payload = {
  3734	        ...(message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
  3735	          ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown>
  3736	          : {}),
  3737	        verified_current_state: verified,
  3738	      };
  3739	      message.activity = [...(message.activity ?? []), {
  3740	        event_id: randomUUID(),
  3741	        at: new Date().toISOString(),

  3743	        status: "running" as const,
  3744	        label: adjudication.failure_class === "EXTERNAL_RUNTIME"

  3748	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  3749	
  3750	      const existingProDiagnosis = verified.self_heal_pro_diagnostic;
  3751	      const eligibleForPro = shouldRequestSelfHealProDiagnosis({

  3756	        observed_tool_steps: trace.length,

  3758	        failure_code: adjudication.code,
  3759	        enabled: process.env.GEMINX_SELF_HEAL_PRO_ENABLED?.trim() !== "0",
  3760	      });
  3761	      if (eligibleForPro) {
  3762	        // Persist the attempt BEFORE the provider request. On provider timeout or
  3763	        // worker restart we must never buy a second Pro diagnosis for this job.
  3764	        const at = new Date().toISOString();
  3765	        verified.self_heal_pro_diagnostic = {
  3766	          status: "ATTEMPTED",
  3767	          at,

  3778	        };

  3784	            [
  3785	              "You are the one-shot DeepSeek V4 Pro DIAGNOSTIC tier for a failed GeminX code repair.",

  3832	          }].slice(-80);
  3833	          liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  3834	          console.log(`[SELF-HEAL] phase=flash-executor status=QUEUED job_id=${message.message_id} diagnostic_model=${diagnosis.model} fingerprint=${disproven.fingerprint.slice(0, 18)}`);
  3835	          await pushReceiptToBrain(message).catch((error) => {
  3836	            console.warn(`[SELF-HEAL] phase=receipt status=FAILED job_id=${message.message_id} error_class=${error instanceof Error ? error.name : "unknown"}`);

  3839	        } catch (error) {
  3840	          verified.self_heal_pro_diagnostic = {

  3848	          message = refreshMessage();
  3849	          message.payload = {

  3857	        }
  3858	      }
  3859	      if (message.objective_contract) {

  3865	      }
  3866	      return complete("BLOCKED", {

  3870	        founder_action_required: false,
  3871	        blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  3872	        // A semantic stall is a failure to complete the action, not a reason

  3880	    }
  3881	
  3882	    const adjudicationOutput = adjudication.output
  3883	      ? normalizeAutonomousRecoveryClaims(String(adjudication.output), recoveryTruth)

  3885	
  3886	    message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  3887	    liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  3888	    const noProgressFuse =
  3889	      adjudication.status === "BLOCKED" &&
  3890	      adjudication.code === "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_NO_PROGRESS_CIRCUIT_OPEN";
  3891	    const protectedHumanBoundary =
  3892	      adjudication.status === "BLOCKED" &&
  3893	      adjudication.failure_class === "PROTECTED_HUMAN_BOUNDARY";
  3894	    const externalRuntimeBoundary =
  3895	      adjudication.status === "BLOCKED" &&
  3896	      adjudication.failure_class === "EXTERNAL_RUNTIME";
  3897	    return complete(adjudication.status, {
  3898	      code: adjudication.code,
  3899	      ...(noProgressFuse ? {
  3900	        founder_action_required: false,
  3901	        blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  3902	        internal_safety_circuit: true,
  3903	      } : {}),
  3904	      ...(protectedHumanBoundary ? {
  3905	        founder_action_required: true,
  3906	        blocker_category: "PROTECTED_HUMAN_BOUNDARY",

  3909	        founder_action_required: false,

  3918	    });
  3919	  } catch (error) {
  3920	    const cancelledJob = cancelled();
  3921	    if (cancelledJob || (error instanceof Error && error.message === "GEMINX_JOB_CANCELLED")) {
  3922	      return cancelledJob ?? refreshMessage();
  3923	    }
  3924	    if (error instanceof BrowserHumanTakeoverRequired) {

  3928	      delete message.receipt_id;

  3933	        status: "WAITING_HUMAN",

  3940	        output: error.challenge
  3941	          ? "A protected browser challenge was mechanically detected. The same task/session is preserved for resume after the protected human step."
  3942	          : "Browser human control is active, but no protected challenge detail was supplied."
  3943	      };
  3944	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  3945	      return message;
  3946	    }
  3947	
  3948	    // DeepSeek's explicit 402/429 is a provider boundary, not assistant speech.
  3949	    // Switch the SAME durable objective to live Gemini only if no native tool
  3950	    // action may have occurred. Do not replay an unknown external mutation.
  3951	    const errorDetail = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
  3952	    const priorModel = String(command.model_preference ?? "deepseek").toLowerCase();
  3953	    const checkpointForFailover = new ConversationStore({ root: liveStore.runtimeRoot })
  3954	      .getExecutionCheckpoint(message.message_id);
  3955	    const checkpointMessages = checkpointForFailover?.messages ?? [];
  3956	    const verifiedBeforeFailover: Record<string, unknown> = command.verified_current_state &&
  3957	      typeof command.verified_current_state === "object"
  3958	      ? { ...command.verified_current_state } : {};
  3959	    const googleConfigured = Boolean(
  3960	      process.env.GEMINX_ANTIGRAVITY_API_KEY ||
  3961	      process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY ||
  3962	      process.env.GOOGLE_API_KEY ||
  3963	      process.env.GEMINX_GEMINI_LIVE_API_KEY
  3964	    );
  3965	    const failoverPlan = planDeepSeekGoogleFailover({
  3966	      failure: errorDetail,

  3969	      googleAlreadyAttempted: Boolean(verifiedBeforeFailover.provider_failover_google_attempted_at),
  3970	      toolReceipts: registry.trace(correlationId).length,

  3973	      unresolvedToolCalls: unresolvedNativeToolCallIds(checkpointMessages).length
  3974	    });
  3975	    if (failoverPlan === "REROUTE_GOOGLE" && !creatorFrontierOnly) {
  3976	      const now = new Date().toISOString();
  3977	      verifiedBeforeFailover.provider_failover_google_attempted_at = now;
  3978	      verifiedBeforeFailover.provider_failover_source = "deepseek";

  3981	      command.model_preference = "gemini-pro";
  3982	      command.verified_current_state = verifiedBeforeFailover;
  3983	      message = refreshMessage();
  3984	      message.payload = {
  3985	        ...(message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
  3986	          ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown> : {}),
  3987	        model_preference: "gemini-pro",
  3988	        verified_current_state: verifiedBeforeFailover,
  3989	      };
  3990	      message.status = "QUEUED";
  3991	      delete message.completed_at;
  3992	      delete message.receipt_id;
  3993	      delete message.reply_payload;
  3994	      message.next_attempt_at = new Date(Date.now() + 1000).toISOString();
  3995	      message.last_error = errorDetail.slice(0, 800);
  3996	      message.last_heartbeat_at = now;
  3997	      message.activity = [...(message.activity ?? []), {
  3998	        event_id: randomUUID(),
  3999	        at: now,
  4000	        phase: "model-failover",
  4001	        status: "running" as const,
  4002	        label: "DeepSeek provider boundary · continuing the same objective on live Gemini (no tool replay)"
  4003	      }].slice(-80);
  4004	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4005	      return message;
  4006	    }
  4007	    if (failoverPlan === "RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED") {
  4008	      message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  4009	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4010	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4011	        code: "GEMINX_PROVIDER_FAILOVER_TOOL_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED",
  4012	        founder_action_required: false,
  4013	        blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  4014	        output: errorDetail,
  4015	        delegation_lineage: lineage
  4016	      });
  4017	    }
  4018	    if (deepSeekCreditExhausted(error)) {
  4019	      return retrySameJob(error);
  4020	    }
  4021	
  4022	    if (isOpenAiCompanionModelPreference(command.model_preference) && astraProviderUnavailable(error)) {
  4023	      const observedAt = new Date();
  4024	      const creditExhausted = astraCreditExhausted(error);
  4025	      const verified = command.verified_current_state && typeof command.verified_current_state === "object"
  4026	        ? { ...command.verified_current_state }
  4027	        : {};
  4028	      verified.astra_unavailable_reason = creditExhausted
  4029	        ? "credit_balance_exhausted"
  4030	        : "provider_temporarily_unavailable";
  4031	      verified.astra_unavailable_at = observedAt.toISOString();
  4032	      delete verified.astra_reprobe_after;
  4033	      verified.astra_provider_boundary = creditExhausted
  4034	        ? "FOUNDER_PAYMENT_OR_TOPUP_MAY_BE_REQUIRED_TO_RESTORE_ASTRA"
  4035	        : "OPTIONAL_PROVIDER_TEMPORARILY_UNAVAILABLE";
  4036	      verified.next_action = "CONTINUE_WITH_DEEPSEEK_FLASH";
  4037	      command.verified_current_state = verified;
  4038	      command.model_preference = "deepseek";
  4039	      message = refreshMessage();
  4040	      message.payload = {
  4041	        ...(message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
  4042	          ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown>
  4043	          : {}),
  4044	        model_preference: command.model_preference,
  4045	        verified_current_state: verified,
  4046	      };
  4047	      message.status = "QUEUED";
  4048	      delete message.completed_at;
  4049	      delete message.receipt_id;
  4050	      delete message.reply_payload;
  4051	      delete message.next_attempt_at;
  4052	      message.last_error = undefined;
  4053	      message.last_heartbeat_at = observedAt.toISOString();
  4054	      message.activity = [...(message.activity ?? []), {
  4055	        event_id: randomUUID(),
  4056	        at: message.last_heartbeat_at,
  4057	        phase: "model-reroute",
  4058	        status: "running" as const,
  4059	        label: creditExhausted
  4060	          ? "Astra credits unavailable · continuing same objective with DeepSeek Flash · no automatic paid re-probe"
  4061	          : "Astra temporarily unavailable · continuing same objective with DeepSeek Flash · no automatic paid re-probe"
  4062	      }].slice(-80);
  4063	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4064	      return message;
  4065	    }
  4066	
  4067	    const executionErrorText = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
  4068	    // Flash can exhaust a turn after fully recorded tool receipts without
  4069	    // producing founder-visible text. A CLEAN, receipt-complete native transcript
  4070	    // can continue without replaying its past actions. Pending/unknown tool
  4071	    // outcomes still fail closed rather than duplicating a possible mutation.
  4072	    if (executionErrorText.includes("MODEL_DID_NOT_PRODUCE_A_USABLE_REPLY")) {
  4073	      let evidence: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  4074	      try {
  4075	        const parsed = JSON.parse(executionErrorText);
  4076	        if (parsed && typeof parsed === "object" && !Array.isArray(parsed)) {
  4077	          evidence = parsed as Record<string, unknown>;
  4078	        }
  4079	      } catch {
  4080	        // Old/unstructured exceptions are never eligible for automated replay.
  4081	      }
  4082	      const checkpoint = new ConversationStore({ root: liveStore.runtimeRoot })
  4083	        .getExecutionCheckpoint(message.message_id);
  4084	      if (canResumeNoReplyFromNativeCheckpoint(checkpoint, command.objective)) {
  4085	        const failures = Array.isArray(evidence.tool_failures)
  4086	          ? evidence.tool_failures.filter(row => row && typeof row === "object") as Record<string, unknown>[]
  4087	          : [];
  4088	        const railwayDenied = failures.some(row =>
  4089	          String(row.tool ?? "").startsWith("railway_") &&
  4090	          /Not Authorized|RAILWAY_HTTP_(?:401|403)|RAILWAY_AUTH_REJECTED/i.test(String(row.error ?? "")));
  4091	        const githubMissing = failures.some(row =>
  4092	          row.tool === "github_read_file_range" &&
  4093	          /owner, repo and path are required/.test(String(row.error ?? "")));
  4094	        const verified = command.verified_current_state && typeof command.verified_current_state === "object"
  4095	          ? { ...command.verified_current_state } : {};
  4096	        verified.no_reply_native_checkpoint_recovery = {
  4097	          original_correlation_id: message.correlation_id,
  4098	          model: evidence.model ?? "unknown",
  4099	          tool_failures: failures.slice(-8).map(row => ({
  4100	            tool: String(row.tool ?? ""),
  4101	            status: String(row.status ?? ""),
  4102	            error: String(row.error ?? "").slice(0, 180)
  4103	          })),
  4104	          next_action: railwayDenied
  4105	            ? "Railway read-only auth was rejected: do not retry identical Railway credentials or repeat the same Railway reads. Continue authorised GitHub/browser/preview code work and report exact pending Railway access after other phases."
  4106	            : githubMissing
  4107	              ? "Use explicit owner, repo, valid file path and exact ref. Discover actual file paths with github_repo_map first; never replay an empty github_read_file_range call."
  4108	              : "Continue from confirmed native tool receipts without replaying past actions. Choose a different executable path; never repeat an ambiguous write.",
  4109	          observed_at: new Date().toISOString()
  4110	        };
  4111	        command.verified_current_state = verified;
  4112	        message = refreshMessage();
  4113	        message.payload = {
  4114	          ...(message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
  4115	            ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown> : {}),
  4116	          verified_current_state: verified
  4117	        };
  4118	        message.activity = [...(message.activity ?? []), {
  4119	          event_id: randomUUID(),
  4120	          at: new Date().toISOString(),
  4121	          phase: "model-recovery",
  4122	          status: "running" as const,
  4123	          label: "Model returned no usable final text · native tool receipts were complete; continuing same job from clean checkpoint"
  4124	        }].slice(-80);
  4125	        liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4126	        return retrySameJob(error, "MODEL_NO_REPLY_CLEAN_CHECKPOINT");
  4127	      }
  4128	      // A pending or unmatched tool has an unknown side effect. Never replay it.
  4129	      message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  4130	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4131	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4132	        code: "GEMINX_MODEL_NO_REPLY_NATIVE_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED",
  4133	        founder_action_required: false,
  4134	        blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  4135	        output: "The model returned no usable text. The native checkpoint has a pending, mismatched or incomplete tool receipt; the possible external action was NOT repeated.",
  4136	        delegation_lineage: lineage
  4137	      });
  4138	    }
  4139	
  4140	    if (executionErrorText.includes("AGX_HARNESS_NOT_CONFIGURED") || executionErrorText.includes("AGX_HARNESS_MCP_NOT_VERIFIED")) {
  4141	      message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  4142	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4143	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4144	        code: executionErrorText.includes("AGX_HARNESS_NOT_CONFIGURED")
  4145	          ? "AGX_HARNESS_NOT_CONFIGURED"
  4146	          : "AGX_HARNESS_MCP_NOT_VERIFIED",
  4147	        founder_action_required: false,
  4148	        blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_CREDENTIAL_MISSING",
  4149	        output: executionErrorText,
  4150	        delegation_lineage: lineage
  4151	      });
  4152	    }
  4153	
  4154	    // Legacy Gemini no-reply reconciliation handling
  4155	    if (executionErrorText.includes("GEMINX_GEMINI_NO_REPLY_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED")) {
  4156	      const store = new ConversationStore({ root: liveStore.runtimeRoot });
  4157	      const checkpoint = store.getExecutionCheckpoint(message.message_id);
  4158	      const toolTrace = registry.trace(correlationId);
  4159	      message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  4160	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4161	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4162	        code: "GEMINX_GEMINI_NO_REPLY_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED",
  4163	        founder_action_required: false,
  4164	        internal_safety_circuit: true,
  4165	        blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  4166	        output: `Google Gemini returned no usable final answer after executing tools. Tool receipts: ${toolTrace.length}. Replay suppressed to prevent duplicating completed actions.`,
  4167	        delegation_lineage: lineage
  4168	      });
  4169	    }
  4170	
  4171	    // A local office-role policy rejection happens BEFORE any Google Interactions
  4172	    // request. Its preflight/read receipt is not uncertain mutating work, and
  4173	    // it must not be reported as an absent provider interaction ID.
  4174	    if (executionErrorText.includes("AGX_HARNESS_LOCAL_PREPROVIDER_DENIED")) {
  4175	      message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  4176	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4177	      const localCode = executionErrorText.match(/AGX_HARNESS_LOCAL_PREPROVIDER_DENIED:\s*([A-Z0-9_]+)/i)?.[1]
  4178	        || "LOCAL_ROLE_TOOL_DENIED";
  4179	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4180	        code: "AGX_HARNESS_LOCAL_PREPROVIDER_DENIED",
  4181	        founder_action_required: false,
  4182	        internal_safety_circuit: true,
  4183	        blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  4184	        output: `Google managed dispatch was blocked by local policy before contacting Google (${localCode}). No provider interaction exists, no paid inference was made and no mutations were replayed.`,
  4185	        delegation_lineage: lineage
  4186	      });
  4187	    }
  4188	
  4189	    // For Google managed Antigravity: distinguish pending/incomplete interactions or
  4190	    // potential mutations from clean terminal failures. Never replay uncertain mutating work.
  4191	    // Preserve the original job ID, interaction ID, checkpoint, receipts and lease.
  4192	    const modelPref = String(command.model_preference || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  4193	    const isManagedRoute = modelPref === "antigravity" ||
  4194	      executionErrorText.includes("AGX_HARNESS_") ||
  4195	      executionErrorText.includes("Google managed Antigravity");
  4196	    if (isManagedRoute && (
  4197	        executionErrorText.includes("AGX_HARNESS_NOT_COMPLETED") ||
  4198	        executionErrorText.includes("Google managed Antigravity did not complete") ||
  4199	        (error instanceof AntigravityCompanionError && modelPref === "antigravity"))) {
  4200	      const store = new ConversationStore({ root: liveStore.runtimeRoot });
  4201	      const conversationRecord = command.conversation_id ? store.get(command.conversation_id) : null;
  4202	      const managedSession = conversationRecord?.managed_antigravity_session;
  4203	      const checkpoint = store.getExecutionCheckpoint(message.message_id);
  4204	      const toolTrace = registry.trace(correlationId);
  4205	      const externalToolTrace = toolTrace.filter(
  4206	        t => t.tool !== "antigravity_harness_status" && t.tool !== "antigravity_harness_execute"
  4207	      );
  4208	      const remoteToolCallsMatch = executionErrorText.match(/remote_tool_calls=(\d+)/i);
  4209	      const remoteToolCount = remoteToolCallsMatch ? Number(remoteToolCallsMatch[1]) : 0;
  4210	      const hasToolCalls = externalToolTrace.length > 0 || remoteToolCount > 0 ||
  4211	        Boolean(checkpoint?.messages?.some(m => m.role === "tool" || Boolean(m.tool_calls?.length)));
  4212	      const statusMatch = executionErrorText.match(/status=([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)/i);
  4213	      const idMatch = executionErrorText.match(/interaction_id=([^\s]+)/i);
  4214	      const sessionStatus = statusMatch?.[1] ?? managedSession?.status ?? "unknown";
  4215	      const effectiveInteractionId = (idMatch?.[1] && idMatch[1] !== "none" ? idMatch[1] : undefined) ?? managedSession?.interaction_id;
  4216	      const isPendingOrIncomplete = ["in_progress", "incomplete", "requires_action", "pending", "unknown"].includes(sessionStatus) ||
  4217	        /in_progress|incomplete|requires_action|pending/i.test(executionErrorText);
  4218	
  4219	      const validInteractionId = Boolean(
  4220	        effectiveInteractionId &&
  4221	        !effectiveInteractionId.startsWith("err-") &&
  4222	        !effectiveInteractionId.startsWith("fail-")
  4223	      );
  4224	      if (hasToolCalls || (isPendingOrIncomplete && validInteractionId)) {
  4225	        message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  4226	        liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4227	        let llmExplanation = "";
  4228	        try {
  4229	          const diag = await generateAntigravityCompanionReplyWithTools(
  4230	            `Summarize in 2-3 direct sentences for Rob what just happened on this turn and what the current status is, without corporate fluff or asterisks. Objective: ${String(command.objective || "").slice(0, 500)}. Provider status: ${sessionStatus}. Interaction ID: ${effectiveInteractionId ?? "none"}. External tool calls recorded: ${externalToolTrace.length + remoteToolCount}. Diagnostic detail: ${executionErrorText.slice(0, 700)}`,
  4231	            correlationId,
  4232	            null
  4233	          );
  4234	          llmExplanation = String(diag.message || "").trim();
  4235	        } catch {
  4236	          llmExplanation = "";
  4237	        }
  4238	        const technicalFooter = `Google managed Antigravity ${sessionStatus === "completed" ? "completed without a usable final answer" : `did not complete (status=${sessionStatus})`}. External tool receipts: ${externalToolTrace.length + remoteToolCount}. Uncertain mutating work was not replayed. Original job ID, interaction ID (${effectiveInteractionId ?? "none"}), checkpoint, and receipts preserved for reconciliation.`;
  4239	        return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4240	          code: "GEMINX_MANAGED_INTERACTION_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED",
  4241	          founder_action_required: false,
  4242	          internal_safety_circuit: true,
  4243	          blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  4244	          output: llmExplanation ? `${llmExplanation}\n\n[${technicalFooter}]` : `${executionErrorText}\n\n[${technicalFooter}]`,
  4245	          interaction_id: effectiveInteractionId,
  4246	          environment_id: managedSession?.environment_id,
  4247	          provider_status: sessionStatus,
  4248	          delegation_lineage: lineage
  4249	        });
  4250	      }
  4251	
  4252	      // Clean terminal failure with zero tool calls: route through retry circuit under failure fingerprint
  4253	      return retrySameJob(error, "AGX_HARNESS_TERMINAL_FAILURE");
  4254	    }
  4255	    if (executionErrorText.includes("BLOCKED_TURN_0_UNBOUND_CONNECTOR")) {
  4256	      message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  4257	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4258	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4259	        code: "BLOCKED_TURN_0_UNBOUND_CONNECTOR",
  4260	        founder_action_required: false,
  4261	        blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_CREDENTIAL_MISSING",
  4262	        llm_calls: 0,
  4263	        output: executionErrorText,
  4264	        delegation_lineage: lineage
  4265	      });
  4266	    }
  4267	    if (/GEMINX_(?:TOOL_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED|EXECUTION_ALREADY_TERMINAL|EXECUTION_CHECKPOINT_[A-Z_]+)/i.test(executionErrorText)) {
  4268	      message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  4269	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4270	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4271	        code: "GEMINX_NATIVE_TRANSCRIPT_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED",
  4272	        founder_action_required: false,
  4273	        blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  4274	        output: executionErrorText,
  4275	        delegation_lineage: lineage
  4276	      });
  4277	    }
  4278	    if (/DEEPSEEK_(?:OBJECTIVE_LIFETIME_BUDGET_REACHED|TASK_SPEND_BUDGET_REACHED|PROVIDER_REQUEST_BUDGET_REACHED|OBJECTIVE_LEDGER_LOCK_UNAVAILABLE|OBJECTIVE_VELOCITY_FUSE_TRIPPED)|GEMINX_(?:WORKFLOW_SCHEMA_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED|CONTEXT_PRESSURE_PREFLIGHT)/i.test(executionErrorText)) {
  4279	      message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  4280	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4281	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4282	        code: "GEMINX_INTERNAL_FINITE_BUDGET_OR_CAPACITY_BOUNDARY",
  4283	        founder_action_required: false,
  4284	        blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  4285	        output: executionErrorText,
  4286	        delegation_lineage: lineage
  4287	      });
  4288	    }
  4289	    if (deepSeekDailySpendReservationBusy(error)) {
  4290	      return retrySameJob(error);
  4291	    }
  4292	    if (/DEEPSEEK_DAILY_SPEND_BUDGET_REACHED|DeepSeek daily spend budget reached/i.test(executionErrorText)) {
  4293	      message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  4294	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4295	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4296	        code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_SPEND_BUDGET_REACHED",
  4297	        founder_action_required: false,
  4298	        mutable_policy_boundary: true,
  4299	        recoverable_after_material_budget_change: true,
  4300	        delegation_lineage: lineage,
  4301	        output: executionErrorText
  4302	      });
  4303	    }
  4304	
  4305	    if (transientExecutionError(error)) {
  4306	      return retrySameJob(error);
  4307	    }
  4308	
  4309	    // Any unclassified internal failure is not permission to switch paid models
  4310	    // and requeue a fresh execution from scratch. Preserve the failure receipt.
  4311	    message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  4312	    liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4313	    return complete("FAILED", {
  4314	      code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_EXECUTION_ERROR_NO_AUTORETRY",
  4315	      founder_action_required: false,
  4316	      blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  4317	      delegation_lineage: lineage,
  4318	      output: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)
  4319	    });
  4320	  }
  4321	}
  4322	
  4323	
  4324	function frontierReasonCommand(payload: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | null {
  4325	  if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object" || Array.isArray(payload)) return null;
  4326	  const value = payload as Record<string, unknown>;
  4327	  if (String(value.command ?? "") !== "GEMINX_FRONTIER_REASON") return null;
  4328	  return value;
  4329	}
  4330	
  4331	async function executeFrontierReason(message: GeminxMessage, command: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<GeminxMessage> {
  4332	  const complete = (status: "COMPLETED" | "BLOCKED" | "FAILED", reply: Record<string, unknown>) => {
  4333	    message.status = status;
  4334	    message.completed_at = new Date().toISOString();
  4335	    message.receipt_id = `receipt-${message.message_id}`;
  4336	    message.reply_payload = {
  4337	      status,
  4338	      worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  4339	      route: "frontier-reasoning",
  4340	      ...reply,
  4341	    };
  4342	    return message;
  4343	  };
  4344	
  4345	  if (message.sender.toLowerCase() !== "newton") {
  4346	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4347	      code: "FRONTIER_SENDER_FORBIDDEN",
  4348	      output: "Only the governed Newton controller may invoke the frontier reasoning command.",
  4349	    });
  4350	  }
  4351	
  4352	  const authority = String(command.authority ?? "");
  4353	  if (authority !== "ROB_PREAPPROVED_FRONTIER_REASONING_V1") {
  4354	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4355	      code: "FRONTIER_AUTHORITY_INVALID",
  4356	      output: "Frontier reasoning requires the founder-approved authority contract.",
  4357	    });
  4358	  }
  4359	
  4360	  const taskType = String(command.task_type ?? "reasoning") as any;
  4361	  let route;
  4362	  try {
  4363	    route = routeGeminxFrontier({
  4364	      task_type: taskType,
  4365	      prior_verified_failures: Number(command.prior_verified_failures ?? 0),
  4366	      behavioral_score: typeof command.behavioral_score === "number" ? command.behavioral_score : null,
  4367	      conflicting_evidence: command.conflicting_evidence === true,
  4368	      unfamiliar_problem: command.unfamiliar_problem === true,
  4369	      protected_surface_proposal: command.protected_surface_proposal === true,
  4370	      force_frontier: command.force_frontier === true,
  4371	      deepseek_api_key_present: Boolean(env("DEEPSEEK_API_KEY")),
  4372	      openai_api_key_present: openAiSpendEnabled() && Boolean(env("OPENAI_API_KEY")),
  4373	      deepseek_available: true,
  4374	      openai_available: openAiSpendEnabled(),
  4375	      astra_effort: typeof command.astra_effort === "string" ? command.astra_effort as any : undefined,
  4376	    });
  4377	  } catch (error) {
  4378	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4379	      code: "FRONTIER_TASK_TYPE_UNSUPPORTED",
  4380	      output: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unsupported frontier task type.",
  4381	      task_type: taskType,
  4382	    });
  4383	  }
  4384	
  4385	  if (route.provider !== "openai" || route.model !== "gpt-6-astra") {
  4386	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4387	      code: "FRONTIER_ESCALATION_NOT_SELECTED",
  4388	      model_route: route,
  4389	      output: route.provider === "none"
  4390	        ? route.reason
  4391	        : "Canonical routing kept this objective on DeepSeek; use the normal task path instead of spending frontier budget.",
  4392	    });
  4393	  }
  4394	
  4395	  const objective = String(command.objective ?? "").trim();
  4396	  if (!objective) {
  4397	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4398	      code: "FRONTIER_OBJECTIVE_REQUIRED",
  4399	      output: "A non-empty objective is required.",
  4400	    });
  4401	  }
  4402	
  4403	  const brainSnapshot = await configuredAgentBrainContextRetriever().retrieve(objective, {
  4404	    activeDomain: /roblox|creator|game/i.test(objective) ? "roblox" : "geminx",
  4405	    activeGoal: objective,
  4406	  });
  4407	
  4408	  if (
  4409	    brainSnapshot.status !== "attached" ||
  4410	    brainSnapshot.revision_verified !== true ||
  4411	    !brainSnapshot.revision ||
  4412	    !brainSnapshot.content_sha256 ||
  4413	    brainSnapshot.truncated === true
  4414	  ) {
  4415	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4416	      code: "GEMINX_AGENT_BRAIN_BOOT_UNVERIFIED",
  4417	      blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  4418	      founder_action_required: false,
  4419	      nonterminal: true,
  4420	      output: "Canonical Geminex Brain boot is unavailable, revision-unverified, integrity-unbound or truncated. Governed mutation remains fail-closed until the same objective can prove an exact Brain revision and full-content digest.",
  4421	      evidence: {
  4422	        status: brainSnapshot.status,
  4423	        repository: brainSnapshot.repository ?? null,
  4424	        requested_ref: brainSnapshot.requested_ref ?? null,
  4425	        revision: brainSnapshot.revision ?? null,
  4426	        revision_verified: brainSnapshot.revision_verified === true,
  4427	        content_sha256: brainSnapshot.content_sha256 ?? null,
  4428	        truncated: brainSnapshot.truncated === true,
  4429	        truncated_paths: brainSnapshot.truncated_paths ?? [],
  4430	        missing_required_paths: brainSnapshot.missing_required_paths ?? [],
  4431	        sources: brainSnapshot.sources,
  4432	      },
  4433	    });
  4434	  }
  4435	
  4436	  const suppliedVerifiedState = command.verified_current_state;
  4437	  const verifiedState = {
  4438	    supplied: suppliedVerifiedState ?? {},
  4439	    shared_agent_brain: {
  4440	      status: brainSnapshot.status,
  4441	      sources: brainSnapshot.sources,
  4442	      context: brainSnapshot.context,
  4443	      repository: brainSnapshot.repository,
  4444	      requested_ref: brainSnapshot.requested_ref,
  4445	      revision: brainSnapshot.revision,
  4446	      revision_verified: brainSnapshot.revision_verified,
  4447	      content_sha256: brainSnapshot.content_sha256,
  4448	      truncated: brainSnapshot.truncated,
  4449	      truncated_paths: brainSnapshot.truncated_paths,
  4450	      missing_required_paths: brainSnapshot.missing_required_paths,
  4451	    },
  4452	  };
  4453	
  4454	  const arrayOfStrings = (value: unknown): string[] =>
  4455	    Array.isArray(value) ? value.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string" && Boolean(item.trim())).map(item => item.trim()) : [];
  4456	
  4457	  const context = compileFrontierContext({
  4458	    objective,
  4459	    acceptance_criteria: arrayOfStrings(command.acceptance_criteria),
  4460	    verified_current_state: verifiedState,
  4461	    relevant_failures: Array.isArray(command.relevant_failures) ? command.relevant_failures : [],
  4462	    relevant_receipts: Array.isArray(command.relevant_receipts) ? command.relevant_receipts : [],
  4463	    repo_system_map: command.repo_system_map ?? null,
  4464	    permissions: arrayOfStrings(command.permissions),
  4465	    doctrine: arrayOfStrings(command.doctrine),
  4466	    tools: arrayOfStrings(command.tools),
  4467	    budget: command.budget && typeof command.budget === "object" && !Array.isArray(command.budget)
  4468	      ? command.budget as { max_objective_usd?: number; max_output_tokens?: number }
  4469	      : undefined,
  4470	  });
  4471	
  4472	  try {
  4473	    const budget = command.budget && typeof command.budget === "object" && !Array.isArray(command.budget)
  4474	      ? command.budget as Record<string, unknown>
  4475	      : {};
  4476	    const objectiveId = String(command.objective_id ?? message.correlation_id ?? message.message_id);
  4477	    const astra = await invokeAstra({
  4478	      context,
  4479	      reasoning_effort: route.reasoning_effort as any,
  4480	      objective_id: objectiveId,
  4481	      max_output_tokens: typeof budget.max_output_tokens === "number" ? budget.max_output_tokens : undefined,
  4482	      max_objective_usd: typeof budget.max_objective_usd === "number" ? budget.max_objective_usd : undefined,
  4483	    });
  4484	
  4485	    const learning = astra.result.learning_result;
  4486	    const learningPersistence = learning.should_persist
  4487	      ? await persistFrontierLearningCandidate({
  4488	          provider: astra.provider,
  4489	          model: astra.model,
  4490	          objective_id: objectiveId,
  4491	          source_receipt_id: `receipt-${message.message_id}`,
  4492	          task_status: astra.result.task_result.status,
  4493	          lesson_type: learning.lesson_type,
  4494	          title: learning.title,
  4495	          summary: learning.summary,
  4496	          reusable_when: learning.reusable_when,
  4497	          invalidation_conditions: learning.invalidation_conditions,
  4498	          evidence_required: learning.evidence_required,
  4499	          task_evidence: astra.result.task_result.evidence,
  4500	          observed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  4501	        }).catch((error) => ({
  4502	          status: "failed" as const,
  4503	          path: null,
  4504	          fingerprint: null,
  4505	          promotion_state: "CANDIDATE_UNVERIFIED" as const,
  4506	          error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
  4507	        }))
  4508	      : {
  4509	          status: "skipped" as const,
  4510	          path: null,
  4511	          fingerprint: null,
  4512	          promotion_state: "NOT_APPLICABLE" as const,
  4513	        };
  4514	
  4515	    return complete("COMPLETED", {
  4516	      code: "ASTRA_FRONTIER_RESULT",
  4517	      model_route: route,
  4518	      frontier_receipt: astra,
  4519	      learning_persistence: learningPersistence,
  4520	    });
  4521	  } catch (error) {
  4522	    return complete("FAILED", {
  4523	      code: "ASTRA_FRONTIER_EXECUTION_FAILED",
  4524	      model_route: route,
  4525	      output: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
  4526	    });
  4527	  }
  4528	}

  4531	type PennyPodBridgeAction = "MOLTBOOK_POST" | "MOLTBOOK_COMMENT";
  4532	
  4533	function pennyPodBridgeCommand(payload: unknown): {
  4534	  action: PennyPodBridgeAction;
  4535	  authority: string;
  4536	  created_at: string;
  4537	  reason: string;
  4538	  payload: Record<string, unknown>;
  4539	} | null {
  4540	  if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object" || Array.isArray(payload)) return null;
  4541	  const value = payload as Record<string, unknown>;
  4542	  const command = String(value.command ?? "");
  4543	  const action: PennyPodBridgeAction | null =
  4544	    command === "PENNY_POD_MOLTBOOK_POST"
  4545	      ? "MOLTBOOK_POST"
  4546	      : command === "PENNY_POD_MOLTBOOK_COMMENT"
  4547	        ? "MOLTBOOK_COMMENT"
  4548	        : null;
  4549	  if (!action) return null;
  4550	  if (!value.payload || typeof value.payload !== "object" || Array.isArray(value.payload)) return null;
  4551	  return {
  4552	    action,
  4553	    authority: String(value.authority ?? ""),
  4554	    created_at: String(value.created_at ?? ""),
  4555	    reason: String(value.reason ?? ""),
  4556	    payload: value.payload as Record<string, unknown>,
  4557	  };
  4558	}
  4559	
  4560	async function executePennyPodBridge(message: GeminxMessage, command: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof pennyPodBridgeCommand>>) {
  4561	  const token = env("PENNY_POD_BRIDGE_TOKEN");
  4562	  const baseUrl = env("PENNY_POD_INTERNAL_URL") || "https://penny-pod-a2a-production.up.railway.app";
  4563	  if (!token) throw new Error("PENNY_POD_BRIDGE_TOKEN_MISSING");
  4564	
  4565	  const response = await fetch(baseUrl.replace(/\/$/, "") + "/api/internal/social-command", {
  4566	    method: "POST",
  4567	    headers: {
  4568	      "authorization": "Bearer " + token,
  4569	      "content-type": "application/json",
  4570	      "accept": "application/json",
  4571	    },
  4572	    body: JSON.stringify({
  4573	      message_id: message.message_id,
  4574	      action: command.action,
  4575	      authority: command.authority,
  4576	      created_at: command.created_at,
  4577	      reason: command.reason,
  4578	      payload: command.payload,
  4579	    }),
  4580	    signal: typeof AbortSignal.timeout === "function" ? AbortSignal.timeout(20_000) : undefined,
  4581	  });
  4582	  const raw = await response.text();
  4583	  let body: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  4584	  try {
  4585	    body = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) as Record<string, unknown> : {};
  4586	  } catch {
  4587	    body = { raw: raw.slice(0, 500) };
  4588	  }
  4589	  return { response, body };
  4590	}

  4593	type SmartSiteProspectBusAction = "GET" | "UPSERT";
  4594	const DIGITAL_AMBUSH_AUTHORITY = "ROB_PREAPPROVED_DIGITAL_AMBUSH_V1";
  4595	const BUSINESS_SOLUTIONS_SITE_ID = "entreprenuity-business-solutions";
  4596	
  4597	function smartSiteProspectBusCommand(payload: unknown): {
  4598	  action: SmartSiteProspectBusAction;
  4599	  authority: string;
  4600	  site_id: string;
  4601	  slug: string;
  4602	  prospect: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  4603	} | null {
  4604	  if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object" || Array.isArray(payload)) return null;
  4605	  const value = payload as Record<string, unknown>;
  4606	  const command = String(value.command ?? "");
  4607	  const action: SmartSiteProspectBusAction | null =
  4608	    command === "ENTREPRENUITY_SMART_SITE_PROSPECT_GET"
  4609	      ? "GET"
  4610	      : command === "ENTREPRENUITY_SMART_SITE_PROSPECT_UPSERT"
  4611	        ? "UPSERT"
  4612	        : null;
  4613	  if (!action) return null;
  4614	
  4615	  const prospect = value.prospect && typeof value.prospect === "object" && !Array.isArray(value.prospect)
  4616	    ? value.prospect as Record<string, unknown>
  4617	    : null;
  4618	
  4619	  return {
  4620	    action,
  4621	    authority: String(value.authority ?? ""),
  4622	    site_id: String(value.site_id ?? ""),
  4623	    slug: String(value.slug ?? prospect?.slug ?? ""),
  4624	    prospect,
  4625	  };
  4626	}
  4627	
  4628	async function executeSmartSiteProspectBus(
  4629	  message: GeminxMessage,
  4630	  command: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof smartSiteProspectBusCommand>>,
  4631	  store: SmartSiteStore
  4632	): Promise<GeminxMessage> {
  4633	  const complete = async (
  4634	    status: "COMPLETED" | "BLOCKED" | "FAILED",
  4635	    replyPayload: Record<string, unknown>
  4636	  ): Promise<GeminxMessage> => {
  4637	    message.status = status;
  4638	    message.completed_at = new Date().toISOString();
  4639	    message.receipt_id = `receipt-${message.message_id}`;
  4640	    message.reply_payload = {
  4641	      status,
  4642	      worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  4643	      route: "smart-site-prospect-bus",
  4644	      ...replyPayload,
  4645	    };
  4646	    return message;
  4647	  };
  4648	
  4649	  if (message.sender.toLowerCase() !== "newton") {
  4650	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4651	      code: "SMART_SITE_PROSPECT_BUS_SENDER_FORBIDDEN",
  4652	      output: "Only the governed Newton controller may invoke the Digital Ambush prospect bus.",
  4653	    });
  4654	  }
  4655	  if (command.authority !== DIGITAL_AMBUSH_AUTHORITY) {
  4656	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4657	      code: "SMART_SITE_PROSPECT_BUS_AUTHORITY_INVALID",
  4658	      output: "Digital Ambush prospect mutation authority is missing or invalid.",
  4659	    });
  4660	  }
  4661	  if (command.site_id !== BUSINESS_SOLUTIONS_SITE_ID) {
  4662	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4663	      code: "SMART_SITE_PROSPECT_BUS_SITE_FORBIDDEN",
  4664	      output: "The prospect bus is restricted to the canonical Business Solutions site.",
  4665	    });
  4666	  }
  4667	  if (!command.slug || !/^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{2,119}$/i.test(command.slug)) {
  4668	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4669	      code: "SMART_SITE_PROSPECT_BUS_SLUG_INVALID",
  4670	      output: "A valid canonical prospect slug is required.",
  4671	    });
  4672	  }
  4673	
  4674	  try {
  4675	    if (command.action === "GET") {
  4676	      const prospect = store.getProspect(command.site_id, command.slug);
  4677	      if (!prospect) {
  4678	        return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4679	          code: "SMART_SITE_PROSPECT_NOT_FOUND",
  4680	          found: false,
  4681	          site_id: command.site_id,
  4682	          slug: command.slug,
  4683	        });
  4684	      }
  4685	      return complete("COMPLETED", {
  4686	        code: "SMART_SITE_PROSPECT_FOUND",
  4687	        found: true,
  4688	        prospect,
  4689	      });
  4690	    }
  4691	
  4692	    if (!command.prospect) {
  4693	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4694	        code: "SMART_SITE_PROSPECT_PAYLOAD_REQUIRED",
  4695	        output: "UPSERT requires a bounded prospect object.",
  4696	      });
  4697	    }
  4698	
  4699	    const prospect = store.upsertProspect(
  4700	      { ...command.prospect, slug: command.slug },
  4701	      command.site_id,
  4702	      { allowVerifiedIdentityCorrection: true }
  4703	    );
  4704	    return complete("COMPLETED", {
  4705	      code: "SMART_SITE_PROSPECT_UPSERTED",
  4706	      found: true,
  4707	      prospect,
  4708	    });
  4709	  } catch (error) {
  4710	    return complete("FAILED", {
  4711	      code: "SMART_SITE_PROSPECT_BUS_ERROR",
  4712	      output: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
  4713	    });
  4714	  }
  4715	}
  4716	
  4717	function githubHeaders(token: string) {
  4718	  return {
  4719	    Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
  4720	    Accept: "application/vnd.github+json",
  4721	    "X-GitHub-Api-Version": GITHUB_API_VERSION,
  4722	    "User-Agent": "GeminX-Inbox-Worker/1.0"
  4723	  };
  4724	}
  4725	
  4726	function contentsUrl(filePath: string, ref?: string): string {
  4727	  const encodedPath = filePath.split("/").map(part => encodeURIComponent(part)).join("/");
  4728	  const base = `https://api.github.com/repos/${GITHUB_REPO}/contents/${encodedPath}`;
  4729	  return ref ? `${base}?ref=${encodeURIComponent(ref)}` : base;
  4730	}
  4731	
  4732	function redactReceiptValue(value: unknown): unknown {
  4733	  if (value === undefined) return undefined;
  4734	  try {
  4735	    const raw = JSON.stringify(value);
  4736	    const redacted = redactSensitive(raw);
  4737	    return typeof redacted === "string" ? JSON.parse(redacted) : value;
  4738	  } catch {
  4739	    return "[UNSERIALIZABLE]";
  4740	  }
  4741	}
  4742	
  4743	type ReceiptSnapshot = Record<string, unknown>;
  4744	
  4745	function receiptTerminalStatus(value: unknown): boolean {
  4746	  return new Set(["COMPLETED", "BLOCKED", "FAILED", "CANCELLED", "EXPIRED", "WAITING_HUMAN"])
  4747	    .has(String(value ?? "").trim().toUpperCase());
  4748	}
  4749	
  4750	function receiptAttempt(value: ReceiptSnapshot): number {
  4751	  const parsed = Number(value.attempt_count ?? 0);
  4752	  return Number.isFinite(parsed) && parsed >= 0 ? parsed : 0;
  4753	}
  4754	
  4755	function receiptCompletedAt(value: ReceiptSnapshot): number | null {
  4756	  const parsed = Date.parse(String(value.completed_at ?? ""));
  4757	  return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : null;
  4758	}
  4759	
  4760	function receiptActivityLength(value: ReceiptSnapshot): number {
  4761	  return Array.isArray(value.activity) ? value.activity.length : 0;
  4762	}
  4763	
  4764	export function receiptSnapshotSupersedesLocal(
  4765	  canonical: ReceiptSnapshot,
  4766	  local: ReceiptSnapshot
  4767	): boolean {
  4768	  if (String(canonical.message_id ?? "") !== String(local.message_id ?? "")) return false;
  4769	
  4770	  const canonicalCorrelation = String(canonical.correlation_id ?? "").trim();
  4771	  const localCorrelation = String(local.correlation_id ?? "").trim();
  4772	  if (canonicalCorrelation && localCorrelation && canonicalCorrelation !== localCorrelation) return false;
  4773	
  4774	  const canonicalAttempt = receiptAttempt(canonical);
  4775	  const localAttempt = receiptAttempt(local);
  4776	  if (canonicalAttempt > localAttempt) return true;
  4777	  if (canonicalAttempt < localAttempt) return false;
  4778	
  4779	  const canonicalTerminal = receiptTerminalStatus(canonical.status);
  4780	  const localTerminal = receiptTerminalStatus(local.status);
  4781	  if (canonicalTerminal && !localTerminal) return true;
  4782	  if (!canonicalTerminal && localTerminal) return false;
  4783	
  4784	  const canonicalCompleted = receiptCompletedAt(canonical);
  4785	  const localCompleted = receiptCompletedAt(local);
  4786	  if (canonicalCompleted !== null && localCompleted !== null) {
  4787	    if (canonicalCompleted > localCompleted) return true;
  4788	    if (canonicalCompleted < localCompleted) return false;
  4789	  } else if (canonicalCompleted !== null && localCompleted === null) {
  4790	    return true;
  4791	  } else if (canonicalCompleted === null && localCompleted !== null) {
  4792	    return false;
  4793	  }
  4794	
  4795	  return String(canonical.status ?? "") === String(local.status ?? "")
  4796	    && receiptActivityLength(canonical) >= receiptActivityLength(local);
  4797	}
  4798	
  4799	function decodeGithubReceipt(content: unknown): ReceiptSnapshot | null {
  4800	  const encoded = String(content ?? "").replace(/\s+/g, "");
  4801	  if (!encoded) return null;
  4802	  try {
  4803	    const decoded = Buffer.from(encoded, "base64").toString("utf8");
  4804	    const parsed = JSON.parse(decoded);
  4805	    return parsed && typeof parsed === "object" && !Array.isArray(parsed)
  4806	      ? parsed as ReceiptSnapshot
  4807	      : null;
  4808	  } catch {
  4809	    return null;
  4810	  }
  4811	}
  4812	
  4813	async function pushReceiptToBrain(message: GeminxMessage): Promise<void> {
  4814	  const token = env("GEMINX_REPO_ACCESS_TOKEN");
  4815	  if (!token) {
  4816	    console.warn(`[geminx-inbox-worker] receipt_not_persisted message_id=${message.message_id} reason=repo_access_missing`);
  4817	    return;
  4818	  }
  4819	
  4820	  const receiptPath = `Agents/GEMINX/Outbox/ToNewton/${message.message_id}/RECEIPT.json`;
  4821	  const receiptContent = {
  4822	    receipt_id: message.receipt_id || `receipt-${message.message_id}`,
  4823	    message_id: message.message_id,
  4824	    correlation_id: message.correlation_id,
  4825	    sender: message.sender,
  4826	    recipient: message.recipient,
  4827	    status: message.status,
  4828	    priority: message.priority,
  4829	    type: message.type,
  4830	    created_at: message.created_at,
  4831	    acknowledged_at: message.acknowledged_at,
  4832	    completed_at: message.completed_at,
  4833	    attempt_count: message.attempt_count,
  4834	    last_error: redactReceiptValue(message.last_error),
  4835	    activity: redactReceiptValue(message.activity),
  4836	    delegation_lineage: redactReceiptValue(message.delegation_lineage),
  4837	    reply_payload: redactReceiptValue(message.reply_payload)
  4838	  };
  4839	  const encodedContent = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(receiptContent, null, 2)).toString("base64");
  4840	
  4841	  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= 5; attempt += 1) {
  4842	    let existingSha: string | undefined;
  4843	    const existing = await fetch(contentsUrl(receiptPath, GITHUB_BRANCH), {
  4844	      headers: githubHeaders(token)
  4845	    });
  4846	
  4847	    if (existing.ok) {
  4848	      const metadata = await existing.json() as { sha?: string };
  4849	      existingSha = metadata.sha;
  4850	    } else if (existing.status !== 404) {
  4851	      throw new Error(`RECEIPT_LOOKUP_FAILED_${existing.status}`);
  4852	    }
  4853	
  4854	    const body: Record<string, unknown> = {
  4855	      message: `geminx: persist execution receipt to Newton for ${message.message_id} [skip ci]`,
  4856	      content: encodedContent,
  4857	      branch: GITHUB_BRANCH
  4858	    };
  4859	    if (existingSha) body.sha = existingSha;
  4860	
  4861	    const response = await fetch(contentsUrl(receiptPath), {
  4862	      method: "PUT",
  4863	      headers: {
  4864	        ...githubHeaders(token),
  4865	        "Content-Type": "application/json"
  4866	      },
  4867	      body: JSON.stringify(body)
  4868	    });
  4869	
  4870	    if (response.ok) return;
  4871	    if (response.status !== 409) {
  4872	      throw new Error(`RECEIPT_WRITE_FAILED_${response.status}`);
  4873	    }
  4874	
  4875	    // A concurrent webhook/worker may have persisted this exact terminal receipt
  4876	    // between our GET and PUT. Reconcile before writing again: identical content
  4877	    // is success, while genuinely different content is rebased on the next loop.
  4878	    const reconciled = await fetch(contentsUrl(receiptPath, GITHUB_BRANCH), {
  4879	      headers: githubHeaders(token)
  4880	    });
  4881	    if (reconciled.ok) {
  4882	      const current = await reconciled.json() as { content?: string; sha?: string };
  4883	      const currentContent = String(current.content ?? "").replace(/\s+/g, "");
  4884	      if (currentContent && currentContent === encodedContent.replace(/\s+/g, "")) {
  4885	        console.log(`[geminx-inbox-worker] receipt_write_reconciled message_id=${message.message_id} attempt=${attempt} action=ALREADY_PERSISTED`);
  4886	        return;
  4887	      }
  4888	
  4889	      const canonicalReceipt = decodeGithubReceipt(current.content);
  4890	      if (canonicalReceipt && receiptSnapshotSupersedesLocal(canonicalReceipt, receiptContent)) {
  4891	        console.log(
  4892	          `[geminx-inbox-worker] receipt_write_reconciled message_id=${message.message_id} attempt=${attempt} action=CANONICAL_NEWER canonical_attempt=${receiptAttempt(canonicalReceipt)} local_attempt=${receiptAttempt(receiptContent)} canonical_status=${String(canonicalReceipt.status ?? "UNKNOWN")} local_status=${String(receiptContent.status ?? "UNKNOWN")}`
  4893	        );
  4894	        return;
  4895	      }
  4896	    } else if (reconciled.status !== 404) {
  4897	      throw new Error(`RECEIPT_RECONCILE_FAILED_${reconciled.status}`);
  4898	    }
  4899	
  4900	    if (attempt === 5) {
  4901	      throw new Error("RECEIPT_WRITE_FAILED_409");
  4902	    }
  4903	    console.warn(`[geminx-inbox-worker] receipt_write_conflict message_id=${message.message_id} attempt=${attempt} action=REFRESH_AND_RETRY_RECEIPT`);
  4904	    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, attempt * 125));
  4905	  }
  4906	}
  4907	
  4908	function scheduledFounderMessageCommand(payload: unknown): {
  4909	  text: string;
  4910	  title: string;
  4911	  conversation_id: string | null;
  4912	} | null {
  4913	  if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object" || Array.isArray(payload)) return null;
  4914	  const value = payload as Record<string, unknown>;
  4915	  if (String(value.command ?? "") !== "GEMINX_SCHEDULED_MESSAGE_V1") return null;
  4916	  const text = String(value.text ?? "").trim().slice(0, 2000);
  4917	  if (!text) return null;
  4918	  const title = String(value.title ?? "GeminX").trim().slice(0, 120) || "GeminX";
  4919	  const conversationId = typeof value.conversation_id === "string" && value.conversation_id.trim()
  4920	    ? value.conversation_id.trim()
  4921	    : null;
  4922	  return { text, title, conversation_id: conversationId };
  4923	}
  4924	
  4925	async function executeScheduledFounderMessage(
  4926	  message: GeminxMessage,
  4927	  command: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof scheduledFounderMessageCommand>>,
  4928	  commsStore: CommsStore
  4929	): Promise<GeminxMessage> {
  4930	  const deliveredAt = new Date().toISOString();
  4931	  let conversationWritten = false;
  4932	
  4933	  if (command.conversation_id) {
  4934	    try {
  4935	      const record = conversationStore.get(command.conversation_id);
  4936	      if (record) {
  4937	        conversationStore.appendTurn(record, {
  4938	          role: "assistant",
  4939	          message_id: message.message_id,
  4940	          content: command.text,
  4941	          at: deliveredAt
  4942	        });
  4943	        conversationWritten = true;
  4944	      }
  4945	    } catch {
  4946	      conversationWritten = false;
  4947	    }
  4948	  }
  4949	
  4950	  // A successful timer firing is not successful Founder delivery. If the
  4951	  // conversation record is absent/unwritable, report the failure truthfully.
  4952	  const deliveryStatus = conversationWritten ? "COMPLETED" : "BLOCKED";
  4953	  const deliveryCode = conversationWritten
  4954	    ? "GEMINX_SCHEDULED_MESSAGE_DELIVERED"
  4955	    : "GEMINX_SCHEDULED_CONVERSATION_DELIVERY_UNVERIFIED";
  4956	  const push = await notifyFounder({
  4957	    title: conversationWritten ? command.title : "GeminX follow-up delivery failed",
  4958	    body: conversationWritten ? command.text : "The scheduled message ran but could not be written to its Founder HQ conversation. The original message and receipt remain preserved.",
  4959	    job_id: message.message_id,
  4960	    status: deliveryStatus,
  4961	    url: command.conversation_id ? "/hq/?conversation=" + encodeURIComponent(command.conversation_id) : "/hq/",
  4962	    tag: "geminx-scheduled-" + message.message_id
  4963	  }).catch(() => ({ sent: 0, failed: 0, removed: 0 }));
  4964	
  4965	  message.status = deliveryStatus;
  4966	  message.completed_at = deliveredAt;
  4967	  message.last_heartbeat_at = deliveredAt;
  4968	  message.receipt_id = "receipt-" + message.message_id;
  4969	  message.reply_payload = {
  4970	    status: deliveryStatus,
  4971	    code: deliveryCode,
  4972	    worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  4973	    delivered_at: deliveredAt,
  4974	    conversation_written: conversationWritten,
  4975	    push
  4976	  };
  4977	  message.activity = [...(Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : []), {
  4978	    event_id: randomUUID(),
  4979	    at: deliveredAt,
  4980	    phase: "scheduled-delivery",
  4981	    status: conversationWritten ? "done" as const : "blocked" as const,
  4982	    label: conversationWritten ? "Scheduled Founder follow-up delivered" : "Scheduled Founder follow-up delivery unverified"
  4983	  }].slice(-80);
  4984	  commsStore.saveMessage(message);
  4985	  console.log(`[geminx-inbox-worker] scheduled_delivery message_id=${message.message_id} conversation_written=${conversationWritten} push_sent=${push.sent} push_failed=${push.failed} push_removed=${push.removed}`);
  4986	  return message;
  4987	}
  4988	
  4989	
  4990	type AutonomousDependencyGate =
  4991	  | { state: "READY"; completed: string[] }
  4992	  | { state: "WAITING"; pending: Array<{ message_id: string; status: GeminxMessage["status"] }> }
  4993	  | { state: "BLOCKED"; reason: string; failed: Array<{ message_id: string; status: string }> };
  4994	
  4995	function autonomousDependencyGate(
  4996	  command: GeminxAutonomousTaskCommand,
  4997	  commsStore: CommsStore
  4998	): AutonomousDependencyGate {
  4999	  const ids = [...new Set(command.depends_on_message_ids.map((id) => id.trim()).filter(Boolean))];
  5000	  if (ids.length === 0) return { state: "READY", completed: [] };
  5001	
  5002	  const completed: string[] = [];
  5003	  const pending: Array<{ message_id: string; status: GeminxMessage["status"] }> = [];
  5004	  const failed: Array<{ message_id: string; status: string }> = [];
  5005	
  5006	  for (const id of ids) {
  5007	    const dependency = commsStore.getMessage(id);
  5008	    if (!dependency) {
  5009	      failed.push({ message_id: id, status: "MISSING" });
  5010	      continue;
  5011	    }
  5012	    if (dependency.status === "COMPLETED") {
  5013	      completed.push(id);
  5014	      continue;
  5015	    }
  5016	    if (["BLOCKED", "FAILED", "CANCELLED", "EXPIRED"].includes(dependency.status)) {
  5017	      failed.push({ message_id: id, status: dependency.status });
  5018	      continue;
  5019	    }
  5020	    pending.push({ message_id: id, status: dependency.status });
  5021	  }
  5022	
  5023	  if (failed.length > 0) {
  5024	    return {
  5025	      state: "BLOCKED",
  5026	      reason: "One or more declared dependencies are missing or terminally failed.",
  5027	      failed,
  5028	    };
  5029	  }
  5030	  if (pending.length > 0) return { state: "WAITING", pending };
  5031	  return { state: "READY", completed };
  5032	}
  5033

  5036	  return [
  5037	    "Creator inventory is owned by this production backend. For current Roblox target/run capacity truth use creator_target_inventory directly; do not ask Rob for a Railway token merely to inspect GeminX's own Creator pool. That deterministic tool releases at most one exact terminal disposable binding and independently fails closed on permanent/reserved, validated/published, Founder-test/public, historically published, mismatched or actively leased targets. Use creator_release_unpublished_target only when the Founder objective names one exact binding to release.",
  5038	    "Creator founder-journey loop: when the objective is to test, repair or finish GeminX Game Creator, do not treat backend health, API success, a green deployment or an internal stage receipt as sufficient acceptance. Use the browser lane to open the real Founder-facing Creator, select/open the exact persisted game/run under test, attempt the same Play/Open-in-Roblox journey Rob would perform, and observe the actual end-user result. If that journey fails, preserve the SAME run/game/artifact/universe/place/version identity, diagnose from browser/runtime/tool evidence, make the smallest repair in the existing canonical owner, then repeat the SAME founder journey. Continue this diagnose -> repair -> retest loop until exact receipts prove the game opens/playability succeeds or a genuine protected human/provider boundary remains. Do not create a replacement game/run merely to make the test pass.",
  5039	    "Creator model lane: autonomous Creator repair stays on DeepSeek Flash. Spend more effort on live tools, exact evidence, compact retries and durable self-repair rather than self-promoting to Pro or Astra. Keep the SAME durable task and accumulated evidence, do not fork a duplicate repair campaign, and after any repair return to the exact same browser/user journey and test again.",

Immediately inspect current system/Studio capability and continue the SAME objective through system_* / roblox_studio_* / ROBLOX_AI tools when connected. Preserve run/game/universe/place/version identity across the handoff. If yes, the probe is insufficient. Prefer outcome proof over mechanism proof. After every repair, rerun the real Founder journey, not merely the easiest API/unit check.",
  5042	  ];
