  5044	
  5045	/** A Cloud AG CommsStore envelope must only be claimed by the Railway Cloud AG worker.
  5046	 * Any envelope addressed to AG (via recipient, office_agent, or ag- conversation)
  5047	 * is owned exclusively by the dedicated Cloud AG worker.
  5048	 */
  5049	export function isDedicatedCloudAgDelivery(
  5050	  message: Pick<GeminxMessage, "recipient" | "type" | "payload">
  5051	): boolean {
  5052	  const recipient = String(message.recipient ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
  5053	  const payload = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown> : null;
  5054	  const officeAgent = String(payload?.office_agent ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
  5055	  const convId = String((message as any).conversation_id ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
  5056	
  5057	  return recipient === "ag" || officeAgent === "ag" || convId.startsWith("ag-");
  5058	}
  5059	
  5060	export async function processGeminxInboxMessage(
  5061	  message: GeminxMessage,
  5062	  commsStore = new CommsStore(),
  5063	  taskEngine = new GeminxTaskEngineService(),
  5064	  smartSiteStore = new SmartSiteStore()
  5065	): Promise<GeminxMessage> {
  5066	  if (!isPrimaryAgentRecipient(message.recipient)) return message;
  5067	  if (isDedicatedCloudAgDelivery(message)) return message; // Cloud worker owns ACK+reply; no DeepSeek race.
  5068	
  5069	  // A sweep works from a queue snapshot. Another worker/recovery path can make
  5070	  // that same logical message terminal before this snapshot starts executing.
  5071	  // Always re-read canonical state before admission so a stale QUEUED object can
  5072	  // never resurrect a BLOCKED/FAILED/COMPLETED objective or emit a duplicate
  5073	  // Founder-facing reply.
  5074	  const canonicalAtAdmission = commsStore.getMessage(message.message_id);
  5075	  if (canonicalAtAdmission) {
  5076	    const canonicalStatus = String(canonicalAtAdmission.status ?? "").toUpperCase();
  5077	    if (["COMPLETED", "BLOCKED", "FAILED", "CANCELLED", "EXPIRED", "WAITING_HUMAN"].includes(canonicalStatus)) {
  5078	      console.log(
  5079	        `[geminx-inbox-worker] stale_admission_suppressed message_id=${message.message_id} stale_status=${message.status} canonical_status=${canonicalStatus}`
  5080	      );
  5081	      return canonicalAtAdmission;
  5082	    }
  5083	    // Continue from the freshest persisted state rather than an older sweep copy.
  5084	    message = canonicalAtAdmission;
  5085	  }
  5086	
  5087	  if (message.status === "CANCELLED") return message;
  5088	  if (message.status !== "QUEUED" || !dueForAttempt(message)) return message;
  5089	
  5090	  const autonomousPreflight = autonomousTaskCommand(message.payload);
  5091	  if (autonomousPreflight && autonomousPreflight.depends_on_message_ids.length > 0) {
  5092	    const dependencyGate = autonomousDependencyGate(autonomousPreflight, commsStore);
  5093	    const now = new Date();
  5094	
  5095	    if (dependencyGate.state === "BLOCKED") {
  5096	      const at = now.toISOString();
  5097	      message.status = "BLOCKED";
  5098	      message.completed_at = at;
  5099	      message.last_heartbeat_at = at;
  5100	      delete message.next_attempt_at;
  5101	      message.receipt_id = `receipt-${message.message_id}`;
  5102	      message.reply_payload = {
  5103	        status: "BLOCKED",
  5104	        worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  5105	        route: "geminx-autonomous-companion",
  5106	        code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_DEPENDENCY_FAILED",
  5107	        output: dependencyGate.reason,
  5108	        dependencies: dependencyGate.failed,
  5109	      };
  5110	      message.activity = [...(Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : []), {
  5111	        event_id: randomUUID(),
  5112	        at,
  5113	        phase: "dependency-gate",
  5114	        status: "blocked" as const,
  5115	        label: "Dependency failed or is missing · parent objective held closed"
  5116	      }].slice(-80);
  5117	      commsStore.saveMessage(message);
  5118	      await pushReceiptToBrain(message).catch(() => undefined);
  5119	      return message;
  5120	    }
  5121	
  5122	    if (dependencyGate.state === "WAITING") {
  5123	      const at = now.toISOString();
  5124	      message.status = "QUEUED";
  5125	      message.next_attempt_at = new Date(now.getTime() + 30_000).toISOString();
  5126	      message.last_heartbeat_at = at;
  5127	      const label = `Waiting for ${dependencyGate.pending.length} declared dependenc${dependencyGate.pending.length === 1 ? "y" : "ies"} · no model call`;
  5128	      const currentActivity = Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : [];
  5129	      const last = currentActivity.at(-1);
  5130	      if (!(last?.phase === "dependency-gate" && last?.label === label)) {
  5131	        message.activity = [...currentActivity, {
  5132	          event_id: randomUUID(),
  5133	          at,
  5134	          phase: "dependency-gate",
  5135	          status: "running" as const,
  5136	          label
  5137	        }].slice(-80);
  5138	      }
  5139	      commsStore.saveMessage(message);
  5140	      return message;
  5141	    }
  5142	  }
  5143	
  5144	  const contextResumeState =
  5145	    autonomousPreflight?.verified_current_state && typeof autonomousPreflight.verified_current_state === "object"
  5146	      ? autonomousPreflight.verified_current_state
  5147	      : {};
  5148	  const boundedContextResumePending =
  5149	    autonomousPreflight
  5150	    && contextResumeState.context_pressure_resume_pending === true
  5151	    && contextResumeState.force_compact_context === true;
  5152	
  5153	  const retryState = autonomousPreflight ? retryCircuitProgressState(message) : null;
  5154	  const retryStop =
  5155	    autonomousPreflight && retryState && retryState.provider_transient_failures >= geminxAutonomousRetryLimit()
  5156	      ? {
  5157	          code: "GEMINX_PROVIDER_TRANSIENT_CIRCUIT_OPEN",
  5158	          suspension_class: "PROVIDER_TRANSIENT_CIRCUIT_OPEN",
  5159	          detail: `Provider transient circuit already open (${retryState.provider_transient_failures}/${geminxAutonomousRetryLimit()}).`
  5160	        }
  5161	      : autonomousPreflight
  5162	        && retryState
  5163	        && retryState.turns_without_progress >= geminxNoProgressTurnLimit()
  5164	        && retryState.strategy_change_count >= geminxStrategyChangeLimit()
  5165	        ? {
  5166	            code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_STRATEGY_EXHAUSTED",
  5167	            suspension_class: "STRATEGY_EXHAUSTED",
  5168	            detail: `No-progress remained after ${retryState.strategy_change_count} materially different recovery strategies.`
  5169	          }
  5170	        : autonomousPreflight && retryState && retryState.total_objective_turns >= geminxObjectiveTurnLimitForCommand(autonomousPreflight)
  5171	          ? {
  5172	              code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_MAX_OBJECTIVE_TURNS",
  5173	              suspension_class: "MAX_OBJECTIVE_TURNS",
  5174	              detail: `Emergency run-loop ceiling reached (${retryState.total_objective_turns}/${geminxObjectiveTurnLimitForCommand(autonomousPreflight)}). This is a last-resort runaway guard, not a normal objective budget.`
  5175	            }
  5176	          : null;
  5177	  if (retryStop) {
  5178	    const now = new Date().toISOString();
  5179	    message.status = "BLOCKED";
  5180	    message.completed_at = now;
  5181	    delete message.next_attempt_at;
  5182	    message.last_heartbeat_at = now;
  5183	    message.receipt_id = `receipt-${message.message_id}`;
  5184	    message.reply_payload = {
  5185	      status: "BLOCKED",
  5186	      worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  5187	      route: "geminx-autonomous-companion",
  5188	      code: retryStop.code,
  5189	      founder_action_required: false,
  5190	      blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  5191	      internal_safety_circuit: true,
  5192	      suspension_class: retryStop.suspension_class,
  5193	      output: `${retryStop.detail} No model/provider call was made. This is an internal maintenance suspension, never a protected Founder boundary.`
  5194	    };
  5195	    message.activity = [...(Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : []), {
  5196	      event_id: randomUUID(),
  5197	      at: now,
  5198	      phase: "retry-circuit",
  5199	      status: "blocked" as const,
  5200	      label: `Internal safety circuit opened · ${retryStop.suspension_class} · Founder action not required`
  5201	    }].slice(-80);
  5202	    commsStore.saveMessage(message);
  5203	    try {
  5204	      emitSeamFault({
  5205	        commsStore,
  5206	        correlation_id: message.correlation_id,
  5207	        parent_job_id: message.message_id,
  5208	        step_id: "RETRY_CIRCUIT_STOP",
  5209	        code: retryStop.code,
  5210	        message: retryStop.detail,
  5211	        intent: autonomousPreflight?.objective ?? null,
  5212	        serialized_payload: message.payload,
  5213	        attempt_count: message.attempt_count
  5214	      });
  5215	    } catch {}
  5216	    await pushReceiptToBrain(message).catch(() => undefined);
  5217	    console.warn(`[geminx-inbox-worker] internal_safety_circuit message_id=${message.message_id} code=${retryStop.code} model_call_suppressed=true`);
  5218	    return message;
  5219	  }
  5220	
  5221	  message.status = "RUNNING";
  5222	  message.attempt_count = Math.max(0, Number(message.attempt_count ?? 0)) + 1;
  5223	  message.acknowledged_at = new Date().toISOString();
  5224	  message.last_heartbeat_at = message.acknowledged_at;
  5225	  delete message.next_attempt_at;
  5226	
  5227	  // Context-pressure retry-ceiling bypass is a one-shot permit. Consume it before
  5228	  // execution so an unrelated later failure cannot inherit an unlimited retry path.
  5229	  if (boundedContextResumePending && message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)) {
  5230	    const payload = { ...(message.payload as Record<string, unknown>) };
  5231	    const verified =
  5232	      payload.verified_current_state && typeof payload.verified_current_state === "object" && !Array.isArray(payload.verified_current_state)
  5233	        ? { ...(payload.verified_current_state as Record<string, unknown>) }
  5234	        : {};
  5235	    delete verified.context_pressure_resume_pending;
  5236	    payload.verified_current_state = verified;
  5237	    message.payload = payload;
  5238	  }
  5239	  commsStore.saveMessage(message);
  5240	
  5241	  const scheduledMessage = scheduledFounderMessageCommand(message.payload);
  5242	  if (scheduledMessage) {
  5243	    const result = await executeScheduledFounderMessage(message, scheduledMessage, commsStore);
  5244	    await pushReceiptToBrain(result).catch((error) => {
  5245	      console.error(`[geminx-inbox-worker] scheduled_receipt_write_failed message_id=${result.message_id} error=${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  5246	    });
  5247	    console.log(`[geminx-inbox-worker] message_id=${result.message_id} status=${result.status} route=founder-scheduled-message`);
  5248	    return result;
  5249	  }
  5250	
  5251	  const connectorResult = newtonConnectorResultCommand(message.payload);
  5252	  if (connectorResult) {
  5253	    const result = applyNewtonConnectorResult(message, connectorResult, commsStore);
  5254	    await pushReceiptToBrain(result).catch((error) => {
  5255	      console.error(`[geminx-inbox-worker] receipt_write_failed message_id=${result.message_id} error=${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  5256	    });
  5257	    console.log(`[geminx-inbox-worker] message_id=${result.message_id} status=${result.status} route=newton-chatgpt-connector-result`);
  5258	    return result;
  5259	  }
  5260	
  5261	  const frontierCommand = frontierReasonCommand(message.payload);
  5262	  if (frontierCommand) {
  5263	    const result = await executeFrontierReason(message, frontierCommand);
  5264	    commsStore.saveMessage(result);
  5265	    await pushReceiptToBrain(result).catch((error) => {
  5266	      console.error(`[geminx-inbox-worker] receipt_write_failed message_id=${result.message_id} error=${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  5267	    });
  5268	    console.log(`[geminx-inbox-worker] message_id=${result.message_id} status=${result.status} route=frontier-reasoning`);
  5269	    return result;
  5270	  }
  5271	
  5272	  const pennyPodCommand = pennyPodBridgeCommand(message.payload);
  5273	  if (pennyPodCommand) {
  5274	    try {
  5275	      const { response, body } = await executePennyPodBridge(message, pennyPodCommand);
  5276	      const ok = response.ok && body.ok === true;
  5277	      message.status = ok ? "COMPLETED" : "BLOCKED";
  5278	      message.completed_at = new Date().toISOString();
  5279	      message.receipt_id = `receipt-${message.message_id}`;
  5280	      message.reply_payload = {
  5281	        status: message.status,
  5282	        worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  5283	        route: "penny-pod-social-bridge",
  5284	        provider_http_status: response.status,
  5285	        result: body,
  5286	      };
  5287	      commsStore.saveMessage(message);
  5288	      await pushReceiptToBrain(message).catch((error) => {
  5289	        console.error(`[geminx-inbox-worker] receipt_write_failed message_id=${message.message_id} error=${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  5290	      });
  5291	      console.log(`[geminx-inbox-worker] message_id=${message.message_id} status=${message.status} route=penny-pod-social-bridge`);
  5292	      return message;
  5293	    } catch (error) {
  5294	      message.status = "FAILED";
  5295	      message.completed_at = new Date().toISOString();
  5296	      message.receipt_id = `receipt-${message.message_id}`;
  5297	      message.reply_payload = {
  5298	        status: "FAILED",
  5299	        code: "PENNY_POD_BRIDGE_ERROR",
  5300	        worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  5301	        output: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
  5302	      };
  5303	      commsStore.saveMessage(message);
  5304	      await pushReceiptToBrain(message).catch(() => undefined);
  5305	      return message;
  5306	    }
  5307	  }
  5308	
  5309	
  5310	  const smartSiteCommand = smartSiteProspectBusCommand(message.payload);
  5311	  if (smartSiteCommand) {
  5312	    const result = await executeSmartSiteProspectBus(message, smartSiteCommand, smartSiteStore);
  5313	    commsStore.saveMessage(result);
  5314	    await pushReceiptToBrain(result).catch((error) => {
  5315	      console.error(`[geminx-inbox-worker] receipt_write_failed message_id=${result.message_id} error=${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  5316	    });
  5317	    console.log(`[geminx-inbox-worker] message_id=${result.message_id} status=${result.status} route=smart-site-prospect-bus`);
  5318	    return result;
  5319	  }
  5320	
  5321	  const autonomousCommand = autonomousTaskCommand(message.payload);
  5322	  if (autonomousCommand) {
  5323	    const result = await executeAutonomousTask(message, autonomousCommand, commsStore);
  5324	    commsStore.saveMessage(result);
  5325	    if (!["QUEUED", "RUNNING", "CANCELLED"].includes(result.status)) {
  5326	      const terminalCode = String(result.reply_payload?.code ?? "UNKNOWN").replace(/[\r\n]+/g, " ").slice(0, 120);
  5327	      const terminalDetailRaw = String(
  5328	        result.reply_payload?.output ??
  5329	        result.reply_payload?.message ??
  5330	        result.last_error ??
  5331	        ""
  5332	      ).replace(/[\r\n]+/g, " ").slice(0, 900);
  5333	      const terminalDetail = String(redactSensitive(terminalDetailRaw) ?? "").slice(0, 900);
  5334	      console.log(
  5335	        `[geminx-inbox-worker] terminal_evidence message_id=${result.message_id} status=${result.status} code=${terminalCode} detail=${JSON.stringify(terminalDetail)}`
  5336	      );
  5337	      await pushReceiptToBrain(result).catch((error) => {
  5338	        console.error(`[geminx-inbox-worker] receipt_write_failed message_id=${result.message_id} error=${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  5339	      });
  5340	    }
  5341	    console.log(`[geminx-inbox-worker] message_id=${result.message_id} status=${result.status} route=geminx-autonomous-companion attempt=${result.attempt_count ?? 0}`);
  5342	    return result;
  5343	  }
  5344	
  5345	  const taskPackage = taskPackageFromPayload(message.payload);
  5346	  if (!taskPackage) {
  5347	    message.status = "BLOCKED";
  5348	    message.completed_at = new Date().toISOString();
  5349	    message.receipt_id = `receipt-${message.message_id}`;
  5350	    message.reply_payload = {
  5351	      status: "BLOCKED",
  5352	      code: "GEMINX_TASK_PACKAGE_REQUIRED",
  5353	      worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  5354	      output: "Message reached GeminX but did not contain a bounded task_package, so no work was fabricated."
  5355	    };
  5356	    commsStore.saveMessage(message);
  5357	    await pushReceiptToBrain(message).catch((error) => {
  5358	      console.error(`[geminx-inbox-worker] receipt_write_failed message_id=${message.message_id} error=${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  5359	    });
  5360	    return message;
  5361	  }
  5362	
  5363	  try {
  5364	    const result = await taskEngine.executeTaskPackage(taskPackage);
  5365	    const status = result.receipt.status === "completed"
  5366	      ? "COMPLETED"
  5367	      : result.receipt.status === "blocked"
  5368	        ? "BLOCKED"
  5369	        : "FAILED";
  5370	
  5371	    message.status = status;
  5372	    message.completed_at = new Date().toISOString();
  5373	    message.receipt_id = result.receipt.id;
  5374	    message.reply_payload = {
  5375	      status,
  5376	      worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  5377	      run_id: result.run.id,
  5378	      receipt_id: result.receipt.id,
  5379	      summary: result.receipt.summary,
  5380	      changed_files: result.receipt.changed_files,
  5381	      protected_action_refused: result.receipt.protected_action_refused,
  5382	      blockers: result.receipt.blockers,
  5383	      commands: result.receipt.commands,
  5384	      head_sha: result.receipt.head_sha,
  5385	      commit_sha: result.receipt.commit_sha
  5386	    };
  5387	    commsStore.saveMessage(message);
  5388	
  5389	    await pushReceiptToBrain(message).catch((error) => {
  5390	      console.error(`[geminx-inbox-worker] receipt_write_failed message_id=${message.message_id} error=${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  5391	    });
  5392	
  5393	    console.log(`[geminx-inbox-worker] message_id=${message.message_id} status=${status} run_id=${result.run.id}`);
  5394	    return message;
  5395	  } catch (error) {
  5396	    message.status = "FAILED";
  5397	    message.completed_at = new Date().toISOString();
  5398	    message.receipt_id = `receipt-${message.message_id}`;
  5399	    message.reply_payload = {
  5400	      status: "FAILED",
  5401	      code: "GEMINX_TASK_EXECUTION_ERROR",
  5402	      worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  5403	      output: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)
  5404	    };
  5405	    commsStore.saveMessage(message);
  5406	    await pushReceiptToBrain(message).catch(() => undefined);
  5407	    console.error(`[geminx-inbox-worker] message_id=${message.message_id} status=FAILED error=${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  5408	    return message;
  5409	  }
  5410	}
  5411	
  5412	async function sweep(): Promise<void> {
  5413	  if (sweepRunning) return;
  5414	  sweepRunning = true;
  5415	  lastSweepStartedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  5416	  try {
  5417	    const commsStore = new CommsStore();
  5418	    if (Date.now() - lastDurableMemoryMirrorFlushAt >= DURABLE_MEMORY_MIRROR_RETRY_MS) {
  5419	      lastDurableMemoryMirrorFlushAt = Date.now();
  5420	      void durableMemoryStore.flushBrainMirror(20)
  5421	        .then((result) => {
  5422	          if (result.pending > 0 || result.attempted > 0 || result.status !== "synced") {
  5423	            console.log(
  5424	              `[geminx-durable-memory] mirror status=${result.status} attempted=${result.attempted} synced=${result.synced} pending=${result.pending} brain_ref=${result.brain_ref} last_http_status=${result.last_http_status ?? "none"} last_error_class=${result.last_error_class ?? "none"} last_error_detail=${result.last_error_detail ?? "none"} cooldown_until=${result.cooldown_until ?? "none"}`
  5425	            );
  5426	          }
  5427	        })
  5428	        .catch((error) => {
  5429	          console.warn(`[geminx-durable-memory] mirror retry failed: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  5430	        });
  5431	    }
  5432	    const availableSlots = Math.max(0, maxConcurrentJobs() - activeJobs.size);
  5433	    if (availableSlots <= 0) return;
  5434	
  5435	    const queued = commsStore.listMessages()
  5436	      .filter(message =>
  5437	        isPrimaryAgentRecipient(message.recipient) &&
  5438	        !isDedicatedCloudAgDelivery(message) &&
  5439	        message.status === "QUEUED" &&
  5440	        dueForAttempt(message) &&
  5441	        !activeJobs.has(message.message_id)
  5442	      )
  5443	      .sort(compareGeminxInboxQueue)
  5444	      .slice(0, availableSlots);
  5445	
  5446	    for (const message of queued) {
  5447	      activeJobs.add(message.message_id);
  5448	      const heartbeat = setInterval(() => heartbeatJob(commsStore, message.message_id), heartbeatMs());
  5449	      void processGeminxInboxMessage(message, commsStore, new GeminxTaskEngineService())
  5450	        .catch((error) => {
  5451	          const latest = commsStore.getMessage(message.message_id) ?? message;
  5452	          if (latest.status === "CANCELLED") {
  5453	            commsStore.saveMessage(latest);
  5454	          } else if (transientExecutionError(error)) {
  5455	            const attempt = Math.max(1, Number(latest.attempt_count ?? 1));
  5456	            const delayMs = retryDelayMs(attempt);
  5457	            latest.status = "QUEUED";
  5458	            latest.next_attempt_at = new Date(Date.now() + delayMs).toISOString();
  5459	            latest.last_error = error instanceof Error ? error.message.slice(0, 800) : String(error).slice(0, 800);
  5460	            latest.last_heartbeat_at = new Date().toISOString();
  5461	            commsStore.saveMessage(latest);
  5462	          } else {
  5463	            latest.status = "FAILED";
  5464	            latest.completed_at = new Date().toISOString();
  5465	            latest.last_error = error instanceof Error ? error.message.slice(0, 800) : String(error).slice(0, 800);
  5466	            latest.reply_payload = {
  5467	              status: "FAILED",
  5468	              code: "GEMINX_INBOX_UNHANDLED_EXECUTION_ERROR",
  5469	              output: latest.last_error
  5470	            };
  5471	            commsStore.saveMessage(latest);
  5472	          }
  5473	        })
  5474	        .finally(() => {
  5475	          clearInterval(heartbeat);
  5476	          activeJobs.delete(message.message_id);
  5477	        });
  5478	    }
  5479	  } finally {
  5480	    sweepRunning = false;
  5481	    lastSweepFinishedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  5482	  }
  5483	}
  5484	
  5485	export function geminxInboxWorkerStatus(): {
  5486	  started: boolean;
  5487	  sweep_running: boolean;
  5488	  active_jobs: number;
  5489	  max_concurrent_jobs: number;
  5490	  poll_interval_ms: number;
  5491	  heartbeat_interval_ms: number;
  5492	  last_sweep_started_at: string | null;
  5493	  last_sweep_finished_at: string | null;
  5494	} {
  5495	  return {
  5496	    started,
  5497	    sweep_running: sweepRunning,
  5498	    active_jobs: activeJobs.size,
  5499	    max_concurrent_jobs: maxConcurrentJobs(),
  5500	    poll_interval_ms: intervalMs(),
  5501	    heartbeat_interval_ms: heartbeatMs(),
  5502	    last_sweep_started_at: lastSweepStartedAt,
  5503	    last_sweep_finished_at: lastSweepFinishedAt,
  5504	  };
  5505	}
  5506	
  5507	export function startGeminxInboxWorker(): void {
  5508	  if (started) return;
  5509	  started = true;
  5510	  console.log(`[geminx-inbox-worker] started interval_ms=${intervalMs()} heartbeat_ms=${heartbeatMs()} concurrency=${maxConcurrentJobs()}`);
  5511	  const memoryDiagnostics = durableMemoryStore.diagnostics();
  5512	  console.log(
  5513	    `[geminx-durable-memory] root=${memoryDiagnostics.memory_root} task_streams=${memoryDiagnostics.task_stream_count} conversation_streams=${memoryDiagnostics.conversation_stream_count} pending_mirror=${memoryDiagnostics.mirror_outbox_count} brain_ref=${memoryDiagnostics.brain_ref} mirror_armed=${memoryDiagnostics.mirror_armed}`
  5514	  );
  5515	  void sweep();
  5516	  setInterval(() => void sweep(), intervalMs());
  5517	}

    36	function objectOf(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
    37	  return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value) ? value as Record<string, unknown> : {};
    38	}
    39	function nonEmpty(value: unknown): string {
    40	  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
    41	}
    42	function evidenceFields(call: DurableToolReceipt): Record<string, unknown>[] {
    43	  const top = objectOf(call.result);
    44	  const data = objectOf(top.data);
    45	  return [top, data, objectOf(top.receipt), objectOf(top.proof), objectOf(data.receipt), objectOf(data.proof)];
    46	}
    47	function field(fields: Record<string, unknown>[], keys: string[]): unknown {
    48	  for (const part of fields) for (const key of keys) if (part[key] !== undefined && part[key] !== null) return part[key];
    49	  return undefined;
    50	}
    51	function validSha(raw: unknown, min = 40): string {
    52	  const str = nonEmpty(raw);
    53	  return new RegExp("^[a-fA-F0-9]{" + min + ",64}$").test(str) ? str : "";
    54	}
    55	function explicitCriterionIds(call: DurableToolReceipt): string[] {
    56	  const fields = evidenceFields(call);
    57	  const ids = [objectOf(call.args).criterion_id, field(fields, ["criterion_id"]), field(fields, ["criterion_ids"])];
    58	  return ids.flatMap(value => Array.isArray(value) ? value : [value])
    59	    .filter((id): id is string => typeof id === "string" && id.length > 0);
    60	}

    62	function isMutatingToolName(toolName: string): boolean {
    63	  return /\b(?:write|edit|patch|create_or_update|delete|remove|deploy|send|publish|submit|open|close|cancel|reset)\b/i.test(toolName) &&
    64	    !/\b(?:workspace_create_isolated|read|status|list|get|search|fetch|verify|inspect|health|scan)\b/i.test(toolName);
    65	}

    67	function receiptForCriterion(
    68	  call: DurableToolReceipt,
    69	  criterion: DurableCriterion,
    70	  allCalls: readonly DurableToolReceipt[] = []
    71	): string | null {
    72	  if (call.status !== "succeeded") return null;
    73	  const boundIds = explicitCriterionIds(call);
    74	  // An explicit criterion id must match. Otherwise only a concrete named
    75	  // evidence category may be automatically matched, never a generic task claim.
    76	  if (boundIds.length && !boundIds.includes(criterion.id)) return null;
    77	  const fields = evidenceFields(call);
    78	  const top = objectOf(call.result);
    79	  if (top.ok === false || top.connected === false || field(fields, ["degraded"]) === true ||
    80	    field(fields, ["error"]) || field(fields, ["failure"])) return null;
    81	  const tool = nonEmpty(call.tool);
    82	  if (!tool) return null;
    83	  const callSuffix = nonEmpty(call.tool_call_id)
    84	    ? ":" + nonEmpty(call.tool_call_id)
    85	    : ":" + createHash("sha256").update(tool + ":" + JSON.stringify(call.args ?? {})).digest("hex").slice(0, 12);
    86	  const description = criterion.description.toLowerCase();
    87	  // A model-provided criterion_id is routing metadata, not proof. Only an
    88	  // actual successful tool readback with a matching independently typed receipt
    89	  // can advance the state. A write/POST alone never certifies its own effect.
    90	  const sha = validSha(field(fields, ["commit_sha", "commitHash", "revision", "sha"])) ||
    91	    validSha(objectOf(top.commit).sha);
    92	  const deploymentId = nonEmpty(field(fields, ["deployment_id", "deploymentId"]));
    93	  const deploymentState = nonEmpty(field(fields, ["deployment_status", "status"])).toUpperCase();
    94	  const artifactHash = validSha(field(fields, ["artifact_sha256", "sha256"]), 64);
    95	  const artifactPath = nonEmpty(field(fields, ["artifact_path", "path", "url"]));
    96	  const exitCode = field(fields, ["exit_code", "exitCode"]);
    97	  const command = nonEmpty(field(fields, ["command", "test_command", "suite"]));
    98	  const statusRaw = field(fields, ["http_status", "status_code", "statusCode"]);
    99	  const httpStatus = typeof statusRaw === "number" ? statusRaw : Number(statusRaw);
   100	  const url = nonEmpty(field(fields, ["url", "request_url", "endpoint"]));
   101	  // ARGON-4 / DS-NOVEL-02: HTTP method must come from verified result fields, never assumed or read from call.args.
   102	  const resultMethod = nonEmpty(field(fields, ["method"])).toUpperCase();
   103	  const isDedicatedGetTool = /^(?:http_get|http_probe|url_fetch|read_url_content)$/i.test(tool);
   104	  const readMethod = resultMethod || (isDedicatedGetTool ? "GET" : "");
   105	
   106	  const wantsReadOnlyConstraint = /\b(?:perform no mutation|no mutation|read[- ]only)\b/.test(description);
   107	  if (wantsReadOnlyConstraint) {
   108	    const anyMutationExecuted = allCalls.some(c => isMutatingToolName(nonEmpty(c.tool)));
   109	    if (anyMutationExecuted || isMutatingToolName(tool)) return null;
   110	    return "readonly_verified:" + tool + callSuffix;
   111	  }
   112	
   113	  const wantsDeploy = /\b(?:deploy|railway|production rollout|release to production)\b/.test(description);
   114	  const wantsTests = /\b(?:test|regression|typecheck|probe|vitest|compile|tsc)\b/.test(description);
   115	  const wantsArtifact = /\b(?:artifact|image|screenshot|video|file hash|sha256)\b/.test(description);
   116	  const wantsHttp = /\b(?:http|status code|live endpoint|website|api route|reachable)\b/.test(description);
   117	  const wantsGit = /\b(?:git|commit|source sha|repository revision)\b/.test(description);
   118	  const wantsInspection = /\b(?:inspect|truth|audit|verify|check)\b/.test(description);
   119	  const wantsRepair = /\b(?:fix|repair|remedy)\b/.test(description);
   120	  const wantsReceipts = /\b(?:receipts?|proven|completion|evidence)\b/.test(description);
   121	  const wantsContinue = /\b(?:continue|standing|self-maintenance)\b/.test(description);
   122	  const evidence: Record<string, string> = {};
   123	  if (/railway.*(?:status|deployment|describe|get)|deployment.*status/i.test(tool) &&
   124	      sha && deploymentId && deploymentState === "SUCCESS") {
   125	    evidence.deploy = "railway:" + deploymentId + ":" + sha;
   126	  }
   127	  if ((/test|shell|command|exec|compile/i.test(tool)) &&
   128	      Number(exitCode) === 0 && exitCode !== undefined &&
   129	      /\b(?:vitest|jest|pytest|npm\s+test|npx\s+tsc|typecheck|unittest)\b/i.test(command)) {
   130	    evidence.test = "test:" + tool + ":" + command.slice(0, 120) + ":exit0";
   131	  }
   132	  const isWorkspaceClean = field(fields, ["clean"]) === true || field(fields, ["status"]) === "VERIFIED_CLEAN";
   133	  const hasTestsPassed = field(fields, ["tests_passed"]) === true;
   134	  if (/workspace_run_verification/i.test(tool) && isWorkspaceClean && hasTestsPassed) {
   135	    const wsSha = nonEmpty(field(fields, ["receipt_sha256"])) || "passed";
   136	    evidence.test = "test:workspace_run_verification:" + wsSha.slice(0, 32);
   137	  }
   138	  // ARGON-4: Calling antigravity_harness_execute alone does NOT prove tests passed.
   139	  // Tests require explicit command/execution exitCode === 0 proof.
   140	  const ambushOutcome = nonEmpty(field(fields, ["outcome", "status"]));
   141	  const ambushPreviewUrl = nonEmpty(field(fields, ["preview_url", "concept_url"]));
   142	  const ambushProspectId = nonEmpty(field(fields, ["prospect_id", "slug"]));
   143	  if (/office_digital_ambush_run|office_digital_ambush_stage/i.test(tool) &&
   144	      (top.ok === true || ambushOutcome === "DOM_QA_PASSED_AWAITING_FOUNDER_APPROVAL" || Boolean(ambushPreviewUrl))) {
   145	    const ambushRef = (ambushProspectId || ambushPreviewUrl || "verified").slice(0, 120);
   146	    // Ambush staging only proves ambush activity; it does NOT claim independent visual test, artifact, or http proof.
   147	    evidence.ambush = "ambush:" + tool + ":" + ambushRef;
   148	  }
   149	  if (/office_digital_ambush_status/i.test(tool) && (field(fields, ["found"]) === true || Boolean(ambushPreviewUrl))) {
   150	    const ambushRef = (ambushProspectId || ambushPreviewUrl || "status").slice(0, 120);
   151	    evidence.ambush = evidence.ambush || ("ambush:status:" + ambushRef);
   152	  }
   153	  // A real browser screenshot must return a concrete artifact path or
   154	  // SHA-256 through the trusted tool result. A bare {ok:true} (or model prose)
   155	  // cannot certify six-width visual QA or an image acceptance criterion.
   156	  const isBrowserCapture = /^(?:browser_take_screenshot|chrome_screenshot)$/.test(tool);
   157	  const isGeneratedImage = tool === "google_image_generate";
   158	  if ((isBrowserCapture || isGeneratedImage) && (artifactHash || artifactPath)) {
   159	    const imgRef = (artifactHash || artifactPath || callSuffix).slice(0, 120);
   160	    evidence.artifact = evidence.artifact || ("artifact:" + tool + ":" + imgRef);
   161	  }
   162	  // An unregistered office_submit_lead cannot verify CRM persistence. Model
   163	  // speech and even successful POST/write results are not independent proof.
   164	  // Only a *separate* successful readback from the existing durable V7 CRM
   165	  // with the matching persisted ID, scope, and provenance may pass this gate.
   166	  if (/\b(?:crm|test[- ]?lead|lead capture|lead submission|submit (?:a )?lead)\b/i.test(description)) {
   167	    const id=nonEmpty(field(fields,["lead_id"]));
   168	    const proof=validSha(field(fields,["receipt_sha256"]),64);
   169	    if (tool !== "office_crm_verify_test_lead" ||
   170	        top.ok !== true || field(fields,["readback_verified"]) !== true ||
   171	        field(fields,["persisted"]) !== true ||
   172	        field(fields,["workspace_id"]) !== "entreprenuity" ||

   174	    return "crm:persisted:readback:"+id+":"+proof;
   175	  }
   176	  if (/read|inspect|status|verify|head|fetch/i.test(tool) && artifactHash && artifactPath) {
   177	    evidence.artifact = "artifact:" + artifactHash + ":" + artifactPath.slice(0, 120);
   178	  }
   179	  if (/read|status|fetch|verify|request|browse/i.test(tool) && httpStatus === 200 && url &&
   180	      readMethod === "GET") {
   181	    evidence.http = "http:get:200:" + url.slice(0, 180);
   182	  }
   183	  if (/github.*(?:fetch|read|status|commit|get|create_or_update|patch)|git.*(?:read|status|show)/i.test(tool) && sha) {
   184	    evidence.git = "git:" + sha;
   185	  }
   186	  if (wantsDeploy) return evidence.deploy ?? null;
   187	  if (wantsTests) return evidence.test || null;
   188	  if (wantsArtifact) return evidence.artifact || null;
   189	  if (wantsHttp) return evidence.http || null;
   190	  if (wantsGit) return evidence.git ?? null;
   191	  // ARGON-4: Delete synthetic keyword catch-alls (inspection:, repair:, receipt:, continue:).
   192	  // Real criteria require genuine typed evidence receipts.
   193	  if (wantsInspection) return evidence.artifact || evidence.http || evidence.git || evidence.test || null;
   194	  if (wantsRepair) return evidence.git || evidence.test || evidence.artifact || null;
   195	  if (wantsReceipts) return evidence.test || evidence.artifact || evidence.git || evidence.deploy || evidence.http || null;
   196	  if (wantsContinue) return evidence.git || evidence.test || evidence.artifact || null;
   197	  // General/process criteria cannot be completed by a vaguely successful
   198	  // tool. They need an explicit criterion-specific acceptance workflow.
   199	  return boundIds.length
   200	    ? evidence.deploy || evidence.test || evidence.artifact || evidence.http || evidence.git || null
   201	    : null;
   202	}
   203	
   204	export function promoteDurableCriteriaFromToolReceipts(
   205	  contract: DurableObjectiveContract,
   206	  toolCalls: readonly DurableToolReceipt[]
   207	): CriterionPromotionResult {
   208	  const newly_verified: string[] = [];
   209	  const criteria = contract.acceptance_criteria.map(criterion => {
   210	    if (criterion.status === "VERIFIED" && criterion.evidence_receipt) return criterion;
   211	    // A FAILED criterion cannot be converted to VERIFIED without explicitly
   212	    // restarting that criterion through the owning acceptance workflow.
   213	    if (criterion.status !== "PENDING") return criterion;
   214	    for (const call of toolCalls) {
   215	      const evidence = receiptForCriterion(call, criterion, toolCalls);
   216	      if (evidence) {
   217	        newly_verified.push(criterion.id);
   218	        return { ...criterion, status: "VERIFIED" as const, evidence_receipt: evidence };
   219	      }
   220	    }
   221	    return criterion;
   222	  });
   223	  const remaining = criteria.filter(c => c.status !== "VERIFIED" || !c.evidence_receipt).map(c => c.id);
   224	  return {
   225	    contract: {
   226	      ...contract,
   227	      acceptance_criteria: criteria,
   228	      current_stage: remaining.length ? "VERIFYING" : "CRITERIA_VERIFIED",
   229	      next_action_pointer: remaining.length ? "VERIFY_CRITERIA:" + remaining.join(",") : "RECONCILE_TERMINAL_PROOF",
   230	      updated_at: newly_verified.length ? new Date().toISOString() : contract.updated_at
   231	    },
   232	    newly_verified,
   233	    remaining
   234	  };
   235	}
   236	
   237	export function classifyDurableArchetype(rawIntent: string, readOnly: boolean): DurableArchetype {
   238	  if (readOnly) return "AUDIT_OR_RESEARCH";
   239	  if (/\b(?:deploy|deployment|railway|vercel|production rollout|ship(?:ping)? to production)\b/i.test(rawIntent))
   240	    return "PRODUCTION_DEPLOYMENT";
   241	  if (/\b(?:outreach|campaign|email sequence|prospect|marketing|social post|social blitz|digital ambush|content|newsletter)\b/i.test(rawIntent))
   242	    return "CONTENT_OR_OUTREACH";
   243	  return "INFRASTRUCTURE_REPAIR";
   244	}
   245	export function ensureDurableObjective(
   246	  existing: Partial<DurableObjectiveContract> | undefined,
   247	  rawIntent: string,
   248	  criteria: string[] = [],
   249	  readOnly = false,
   250	  explicitSpendCeilingCents?: number | null
   251	): DurableObjectiveContract {
   252	  const previous = Array.isArray(existing?.acceptance_criteria) ? existing.acceptance_criteria : [];
   253	  const acceptance_criteria: DurableCriterion[] = previous.length
   254	    ? previous
   255	    : (criteria.length ? criteria : [rawIntent]).map((description, i) => ({
   256	      id: "criterion_" + (i + 1), description, status: "PENDING" as const
   257	    }));
   258	  const maxRecovery = typeof existing?.max_recovery_attempts === "number" && existing.max_recovery_attempts > 0
   259	    ? Math.min(10, Math.floor(existing.max_recovery_attempts))
   260	    : 2;
   261	  return {
   262	    objective_id: existing?.objective_id || randomUUID(),
   263	    raw_founder_intent: existing?.raw_founder_intent || rawIntent,
   264	    archetype: existing?.archetype || classifyDurableArchetype(existing?.raw_founder_intent || rawIntent, readOnly),
   265	    acceptance_criteria,
   266	    current_stage: existing?.current_stage || "ACTIVE_EXECUTION",
   267	    next_action_pointer: existing?.next_action_pointer || "EXECUTE_NEXT_UNVERIFIED_CRITERION",
   268	    recovery_attempts: Math.min(maxRecovery, Math.max(0, Math.floor(existing?.recovery_attempts ?? 0))),
   269	    max_recovery_attempts: maxRecovery,
   270	    spend_ceiling_cents: typeof explicitSpendCeilingCents === "number" && Number.isSafeInteger(explicitSpendCeilingCents) && explicitSpendCeilingCents >= 0
   271	      ? explicitSpendCeilingCents
   272	      : (typeof existing?.spend_ceiling_cents === "number" ? existing.spend_ceiling_cents : null),
   273	    updated_at: existing?.updated_at || new Date().toISOString()
   274	  };
   275	}
   276	  export function resolveDurableRecovery(
   277	    contract: DurableObjectiveContract,
   278	    failedToolName?: string
   279	  ): { action: "ATTEMPT_FALLBACK_CAPABILITY" | "ESCALATE_TO_WARDEN_INBOX"; contract: DurableObjectiveContract } {
   280	    const maxAttempts = typeof contract.max_recovery_attempts === "number" && contract.max_recovery_attempts > 0
   281	      ? Math.min(10, Math.floor(contract.max_recovery_attempts))
   282	      : 2;
   283	    const attempts = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxAttempts, contract.recovery_attempts));
   284	    const canRecover = attempts < maxAttempts;
   285	    const now = new Date().toISOString();
   286	    return {
   287	      action: canRecover ? "ATTEMPT_FALLBACK_CAPABILITY" : "ESCALATE_TO_WARDEN_INBOX",
   288	      contract: {
   289	        ...contract, recovery_attempts: canRecover ? attempts + 1 : attempts, max_recovery_attempts: maxAttempts,
   290	        current_stage: canRecover ? "ATTEMPT_FALLBACK_CAPABILITY" : "WARDEN_CORRECTION_REQUIRED",
   291	        next_action_pointer: canRecover
   292	          ? failedToolName === "shell" ? "USE_GITHUB_OR_FS_DIRECT_TOOLS" : "RETRY_WITH_DIFFERENT_AUTHORISED_TOOL_STRATEGY"
   293	          : "ESCALATE_STRUCTURED_BLOCKER_WITH_EVIDENCE",
   294	        updated_at: now
   295	      }
   296	    };
   297	  }
   298	
   299	/** Idempotent per (job, failure fingerprint), with a suffix never truncated away. */
   300	export function wardenCorrectionMessageId(jobId: string, failureFingerprint: string): string {
   301	  const safe = jobId.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]/g, "_").slice(0, 112);
   302	  const digest = createHash("sha256").update(jobId + "\\0" + failureFingerprint).digest("hex").slice(0, 24);
   303	  return "warden-correction-" + safe + "-" + digest;
   304	}

     2	  | "HEALTHY"
     3	  | "AUTO_REPAIR_SAFE"

     5	  | "ROB_REQUIRED"

     9	export interface OpenClawDiagnosticSnapshot {
    10	  /**
    11	   * OpenClaw is optional to GeminX-native NEO. In native_neo mode the warden
    12	   * must not resurrect a stopped/missing OpenClaw gateway as though NEO were down.
    13	   */
    14	  control_plane_mode?: "legacy_gateway" | "native_neo";
    15	  gateway_service_installed: boolean;
    16	  gateway_running: boolean;
    17	  health_ready: boolean;
    18	  expected_worker_count: number;
    19	  active_worker_count: number;
    20	  duplicate_process_count?: number;
    21	  browser_required?: boolean;
    22	  browser_available?: boolean;
    23	  provider_auth_ok?: boolean;
    24	  provider_reachable?: boolean;
    25	  protected_policy_mismatch?: boolean;
    26	}
    27	
    28	export interface OpenClawDiagnosticDecision {
    29	  schema_version: "geminx.openclaw-diagnostic.v1";
    30	  incident_class: OpenClawIncidentClass;
    31	  safe_to_auto_repair: boolean;
    32	  reasons: string[];
    33	}
    34	
    35	function invalidProcessCount(label: string, value: number): string | null {
    36	  if (!Number.isInteger(value) || value < 0) {
    37	    return `${label} must be a non-negative integer before GeminX can trust the OpenClaw snapshot.`;
    38	  }
    39	  return null;
    40	}
    41	
    42	function contradictoryRuntimeEvidence(snapshot: OpenClawDiagnosticSnapshot): string[] {
    43	  const reasons: string[] = [];
    44	  if (snapshot.control_plane_mode === "native_neo") return reasons;
    45	
    46	  if (!snapshot.gateway_service_installed && snapshot.gateway_running) {
    47	    reasons.push("OpenClaw telemetry claims the canonical gateway is running while its service is not installed.");
    48	  }
    49	  if (!snapshot.gateway_running && snapshot.health_ready) {
    50	    reasons.push("OpenClaw telemetry claims readiness while the canonical gateway is not running.");
    51	  }
    52	
    53	  return reasons;
    54	}
    55	
    56	export function diagnoseOpenClawSnapshot(snapshot: OpenClawDiagnosticSnapshot): OpenClawDiagnosticDecision {
    57	  const reasons: string[] = [];
    58	
    59	  if (snapshot.protected_policy_mismatch) {
    60	    return {
    61	      schema_version: "geminx.openclaw-diagnostic.v1",
    62	      incident_class: "PROTECTED_BOUNDARY",
    63	      safe_to_auto_repair: false,
    64	      reasons: ["Protected OpenClaw policy/configuration differs from declared authority; automatic mutation is refused."]
    65	    };
    66	  }
    67	
    68	  const invalidCounts = [
    69	    invalidProcessCount("expected_worker_count", snapshot.expected_worker_count),
    70	    invalidProcessCount("active_worker_count", snapshot.active_worker_count),
    71	    snapshot.duplicate_process_count === undefined
    72	      ? null
    73	      : invalidProcessCount("duplicate_process_count", snapshot.duplicate_process_count)
    74	  ].filter((reason): reason is string => reason !== null);
    75	
    76	  if (invalidCounts.length > 0) {
    77	    return {
    78	      schema_version: "geminx.openclaw-diagnostic.v1",
    79	      incident_class: "CAPABILITY_GAP",
    80	      safe_to_auto_repair: false,
    81	      reasons: invalidCounts
    82	    };
    83	  }
    84	
    85	  const contradictoryEvidence = contradictoryRuntimeEvidence(snapshot);
    86	  if (contradictoryEvidence.length > 0) {
    87	    return {
    88	      schema_version: "geminx.openclaw-diagnostic.v1",
    89	      incident_class: "CAPABILITY_GAP",
    90	      safe_to_auto_repair: false,
    91	      reasons: contradictoryEvidence
    92	    };
    93	  }
    94	
    95	  const duplicates = snapshot.duplicate_process_count ?? 0;
    96	  const observedExcessProcesses = Math.max(0, snapshot.active_worker_count - snapshot.expected_worker_count);
    97	  if (duplicates !== observedExcessProcesses) {
    98	    return {
    99	      schema_version: "geminx.openclaw-diagnostic.v1",
   100	      incident_class: "CAPABILITY_GAP",
   101	      safe_to_auto_repair: false,
   102	      reasons: [
   103	        `OpenClaw process telemetry is inconsistent: observed ${observedExcessProcesses} excess worker process(es) but duplicate_process_count reports ${duplicates}.`
   104	      ]
   105	    };
   106	  }
   107	
   108	  if (snapshot.provider_auth_ok === false) {
   109	    return {
   110	      schema_version: "geminx.openclaw-diagnostic.v1",
   111	      incident_class: "ROB_REQUIRED",
   112	      safe_to_auto_repair: false,
   113	      reasons: ["Provider authentication is invalid or unavailable; credentials require owner action."]
   114	    };
   115	  }
   116	
   117	  if (snapshot.provider_reachable === false) {
   118	    return {
   119	      schema_version: "geminx.openclaw-diagnostic.v1",
   120	      incident_class: "BLOCKED_EXTERNAL",
   121	      safe_to_auto_repair: false,
   122	      reasons: ["Configured provider is unreachable; do not rewrite a healthy local runtime for an external outage."]
   123	    };
   124	  }
   125	
   126	  const nativeNeo = snapshot.control_plane_mode === "native_neo";
   127	
   128	  if (!nativeNeo && !snapshot.gateway_service_installed) {
   129	    reasons.push("Legacy OpenClaw gateway service is not installed.");
   130	  }
   131	  if (snapshot.browser_required && snapshot.browser_available === false) {
   132	    reasons.push("Required browser capability is unavailable.");
   133	  }
   134	  if (snapshot.active_worker_count < snapshot.expected_worker_count) {
   135	    reasons.push(`Expected ${snapshot.expected_worker_count} worker(s), found ${snapshot.active_worker_count}.`);
   136	  }
   137	
   138	  if (reasons.length > 0) {
   139	    return {
   140	      schema_version: "geminx.openclaw-diagnostic.v1",
   141	      incident_class: "CAPABILITY_GAP",
   142	      safe_to_auto_repair: false,
   143	      reasons
   144	    };
   145	  }
   146	
   147	  if ((!nativeNeo && (!snapshot.gateway_running || !snapshot.health_ready)) || duplicates > 0) {
   148	    if (!nativeNeo && !snapshot.gateway_running) reasons.push("Legacy OpenClaw gateway is not running.");
   149	    if (!nativeNeo && !snapshot.health_ready) reasons.push("Legacy OpenClaw gateway readiness/health check is failing.");
   150	    if (duplicates > 0) reasons.push(`Detected ${duplicates} duplicate managed process(es).`);
   151	
   152	    return {
   153	      schema_version: "geminx.openclaw-diagnostic.v1",
   154	      incident_class: "AUTO_REPAIR_SAFE",
   155	      safe_to_auto_repair: true,
   156	      reasons
   157	    };
   158	  }
   159	
   160	  return {
   161	    schema_version: "geminx.openclaw-diagnostic.v1",
   162	    incident_class: "HEALTHY",
   163	    safe_to_auto_repair: false,
   164	    reasons: [nativeNeo
   165	      ? "GeminX-native NEO is authoritative; OpenClaw gateway presence is optional."
   166	      : "OpenClaw legacy gateway runtime state is healthy."]
   167	  };
   168	}

    18	function ensureDirectory(targetPath: string): void {
    19	  mkdirSync(targetPath, { recursive: true });
    20	}
    21	
    22	function readJsonFile<T>(filePath: string): T | null {
    23	  try {
    24	    return JSON.parse(readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")) as T;
    25	  } catch (error) {
    26	    const code = (error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code;
    27	    if (code === "ENOENT") return null;
    28	
    29	    throw new Error(
    30	      `Runtime state is unreadable or corrupt at ${filePath}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`
    31	    );
    32	  }
    33	}
    34	
    35	function atomicWrite(filePath: string, content: string): void {
    36	  ensureDirectory(path.dirname(filePath));
    37	  const tempPath = `${filePath}.${process.pid}.${randomUUID()}.tmp`;
    38	
    39	  try {
    40	    writeFileSync(tempPath, content, { encoding: "utf8", flag: "wx" });
    41	    renameSync(tempPath, filePath);
    42	  } catch (error) {
    43	    rmSync(tempPath, { force: true });
    44	    throw error;
    45	  }
    46	}

    48	function assertSafeRuntimeId(label: string, value: string): string {
    49	  const hasWindowsUnsafeFilenameCharacter = /[<>:"|?*\u0000-\u001f]/u.test(value);
    50	  if (
    51	    value.length === 0 ||
    52	    value === "." ||
    53	    value === ".." ||
    54	    value.includes("/") ||
    55	    value.includes("\\") ||
    56	    hasWindowsUnsafeFilenameCharacter
    57	  ) {
    58	    throw new Error(`${label} contains an unsafe runtime path identifier.`);
    59	  }
    60	  return value;
    61	}

    63	function assertStoredIdentity(label: string, requestedId: string, storedId: unknown, filePath: string): void {
    64	  if (typeof storedId !== "string" || storedId !== requestedId) {
    65	    throw new Error(
    66	      `Runtime state identity mismatch at ${filePath}: expected ${label} ${requestedId}, found ${String(storedId)}`

    72	  const filePath = path.join(root, entry);
    73	  const record = readJsonFile<T>(filePath);

   100	  leaseId: string,

   120	export class GeminxRuntimeStore {
   121	  readonly runtimeRoot: string;
   122	  readonly runsRoot: string;
   123	  readonly receiptsRoot: string;
   124	  readonly logsRoot: string;
   125	  readonly locksRoot: string;
   126	  private readonly memoryStore: DurableMemoryStore;
   127	
   128	  constructor(runtimeRoot = process.env.GEMINX_RUNTIME_ROOT ?? path.join(process.cwd(), "runtime")) {
   129	    this.runtimeRoot = path.resolve(runtimeRoot);
   130	    this.runsRoot = path.join(this.runtimeRoot, "runs");
   131	    this.receiptsRoot = path.join(this.runtimeRoot, "receipts");
   132	    this.logsRoot = path.join(this.runtimeRoot, "logs");
   133	    this.locksRoot = path.join(this.runtimeRoot, "locks");
   134	    this.memoryStore = new DurableMemoryStore({ root: this.runtimeRoot });
   135	
   136	    ensureDirectory(this.runtimeRoot);
   137	    ensureDirectory(this.runsRoot);
   138	    ensureDirectory(this.receiptsRoot);
   139	    ensureDirectory(this.logsRoot);
   140	    ensureDirectory(this.locksRoot);
   141	  }
   142	
   143	  runPath(runId: string): string {
   144	    return path.join(this.runsRoot, `${assertSafeRuntimeId("runId", runId)}.json`);
   145	  }
   146	
   147	  receiptPath(receiptId: string): string {
   148	    return path.join(this.receiptsRoot, `${assertSafeRuntimeId("receiptId", receiptId)}.json`);
   149	  }
   150	
   151	  logPath(runId: string): string {
   152	    return path.join(this.logsRoot, `${assertSafeRuntimeId("runId", runId)}.log`);
   153	  }
   154	
   155	  attemptLogPath(runId: string, receiptId: string): string {
   156	    return path.join(
   157	      this.logsRoot,
   158	      `${assertSafeRuntimeId("runId", runId)}.${assertSafeRuntimeId("receiptId", receiptId)}.log`
   159	    );
   160	  }
   161	
   162	  private mutationLockPath(runId: string): string {
   163	    return path.join(this.locksRoot, `${assertSafeRuntimeId("runId", runId)}.lock`);
   164	  }
   165	
   166	  private currentProcessOwnsMutationLock(runId: string): boolean {
   167	    try {
   168	      const current = JSON.parse(readFileSync(this.mutationLockPath(runId), "utf8")) as { pid?: number };
   169	      return current.pid === process.pid;
   170	    } catch {
   171	      return false;
   172	    }
   173	  }
   174	
   175	  private tryAcquireMutationLock(lockPath: string, nowMs: number): boolean {
   176	    try {
   177	      writeFileSync(
   178	        lockPath,
   179	        JSON.stringify({ pid: process.pid, acquired_at: isoNow(nowMs) }),
   180	        { encoding: "utf8", flag: "wx" }
   181	      );
   182	      return true;
   183	    } catch (error) {
   184	      if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== "EEXIST") throw error;
   185	      return false;
   186	    }
   187	  }
   188	
   189	  private withRunMutationLock<T>(runId: string, operation: () => T): T {
   190	    const lockPath = this.mutationLockPath(runId);
   191	    const nowMs = Date.now();
   192	    let acquired = this.tryAcquireMutationLock(lockPath, nowMs);
   193	
   194	    if (!acquired) {
   195	      let acquiredAt = Number.NaN;
   196	      try {
   197	        const existing = JSON.parse(readFileSync(lockPath, "utf8")) as { acquired_at?: string };
   198	        acquiredAt = existing.acquired_at ? Date.parse(existing.acquired_at) : Number.NaN;
   199	      } catch {
   200	        acquiredAt = Number.NaN;
   201	      }
   202	      const stale = !Number.isFinite(acquiredAt) || nowMs - acquiredAt > MUTATION_LOCK_STALE_MS;
   203	      if (!stale) throw new Error("GEMINX_RECOVERY_MUTATION_LOCK_BUSY");
   204	
   205	      rmSync(lockPath, { force: true });
   206	      acquired = this.tryAcquireMutationLock(lockPath, nowMs);
   207	      if (!acquired) throw new Error("GEMINX_RECOVERY_MUTATION_LOCK_BUSY");
   208	    }
   209	
   210	    try {
   211	      return operation();
   212	    } finally {
   213	      rmSync(lockPath, { force: true });
   214	    }
   215	  }
   216	
   217	  private writeRun(record: GeminxRunRecord): string {
   218	    const filePath = this.runPath(record.id);
   219	    atomicWrite(filePath, JSON.stringify(record, null, 2));
   220	    this.memoryStore.append({
   221	      stream_type: "task",
   222	      stream_id: record.task_id,
   223	      task_id: record.task_id,
   224	      run_id: record.id,
   225	      event_type: "RUN_STATE",
   226	      timestamp: record.updated_at ?? record.created_at,
   227	      actor: record.caller,
   228	      status: record.status,
   229	      summary: record.goal,
   230	      record_ref: `runs/${path.basename(filePath)}`,
   231	      record_sha256: durableMemoryRecordHash(record),
   232	      protected_action_refused: record.protected_action_refused === true,
   233	      idempotency_key: `run:${record.id}:${durableMemoryRecordHash(record)}`
   234	    });
   235	    return filePath;
   236	  }
   237	
   238	  appendTaskDiaryEvent(input: {
   239	    task_id: string;
   240	    run_id: string;
   241	    event_type: string;
   242	    timestamp?: string;
   243	    actor?: string | null;
   244	    status?: string | null;
   245	    summary?: string | null;
   246	    record_ref?: string | null;
   247	    idempotency_key?: string | null;
   248	  }): void {
   249	    this.memoryStore.append({
   250	      stream_type: "task",
   251	      stream_id: input.task_id,
   252	      task_id: input.task_id,
   253	      run_id: input.run_id,
   254	      event_type: input.event_type,
   255	      timestamp: input.timestamp,
   256	      actor: input.actor ?? null,
   257	      status: input.status ?? null,
   258	      summary: input.summary ?? null,
   259	      record_ref: input.record_ref ?? null,
   260	      idempotency_key: input.idempotency_key ?? null
   261	    });
   262	  }
   263	
   264	  saveRun(record: GeminxRunRecord): string {
   265	    const candidateLease = record.recovery?.status === "leased" ? record.recovery.lease : null;
   266	    if (!candidateLease || this.currentProcessOwnsMutationLock(record.id)) {
   267	      return this.writeRun(record);
   268	    }
   269	
   270	    return this.withRunMutationLock(record.id, () => {
   271	      const persisted = this.getRun(record.id);
   272	      if (!persisted) throw new Error(`GEMINX_RECOVERY_RUN_NOT_FOUND:${record.id}`);
   273	      if (persisted.task_id !== record.task_id) throw new Error("GEMINX_RECOVERY_TASK_IDENTITY_MISMATCH");
   274	      assertLeaseOwner(recoveryState(persisted), candidateLease.lease_id, candidateLease.generation, Date.now());
   275	      return this.writeRun(record);
   276	    });
   277	  }
   278	
   279	  saveReceipt(record: GeminxReceiptRecord): string {
   280	    record.escalation = classifyGeminxEscalation({
   281	      status: record.status,
   282	      protectedActionRefused: record.protected_action_refused,
   283	      blockers: record.blockers,
   284	      commands: record.commands
   285	    });
   286	
   287	    const filePath = this.receiptPath(record.id);
   288	    atomicWrite(filePath, JSON.stringify(record, null, 2));
   289	    const durableStreamId = String(record.task_id ?? record.run_id ?? record.id).trim();
   290	    this.memoryStore.append({
   291	      stream_type: "task",
   292	      stream_id: durableStreamId,
   293	      task_id: record.task_id ?? record.run_id,
   294	      run_id: record.run_id,
   295	      event_type: "RECEIPT",
   296	      timestamp: record.created_at,
   297	      actor: record.caller,
   298	      status: record.status,
   299	      summary: record.summary || record.goal,
   300	      record_ref: `receipts/${path.basename(filePath)}`,
   301	      record_sha256: durableMemoryRecordHash(record),
   302	      protected_action_refused: record.protected_action_refused === true,
   303	      escalation_class: record.escalation?.kind ?? null,
   304	      idempotency_key: `receipt:${record.id}`
   305	    });
   306	    return filePath;
   307	  }
   308	
   309	  saveLog(runId: string, content: string): string {
   310	    const canonicalPath = this.logPath(runId);
   311	    const filePath = existsSync(canonicalPath)
   312	      ? this.attemptLogPath(runId, randomUUID())
   313	      : canonicalPath;
   314	    atomicWrite(filePath, content);
   315	    return filePath;
   316	  }
   317	
   318	  saveAttemptLog(runId: string, receiptId: string, content: string): string {
   319	    const filePath = this.attemptLogPath(runId, receiptId);
   320	    atomicWrite(filePath, content);
   321	    return filePath;
   322	  }
   323	
   324	  getRun(runId: string): GeminxRunRecord | null {
   325	    const filePath = this.runPath(runId);
   326	    const record = readJsonFile<GeminxRunRecord>(filePath);
   327	    if (record === null) return null;
   328	    assertStoredIdentity("run id", runId, record.id, filePath);
   329	    return record;
   330	  }
   331	
   332	  getReceipt(receiptId: string): GeminxReceiptRecord | null {
   333	    const filePath = this.receiptPath(receiptId);
   334	    const record = readJsonFile<GeminxReceiptRecord>(filePath);
   335	    if (record === null) return null;
   336	    assertStoredIdentity("receipt id", receiptId, record.id, filePath);
   337	    return record;
   338	  }
   339	
   340	  acquireRecoveryLease(
   341	    runId: string,
   342	    owner: string,
   343	    options: { ttl_ms?: number; max_retries?: number; now_ms?: number } = {}
   344	  ): GeminxRunRecord {
   345	    if (!owner.trim()) throw new Error("GEMINX_RECOVERY_LEASE_OWNER_REQUIRED");
   346	    const nowMs = options.now_ms ?? Date.now();
   347	    const ttlMs = Math.max(1_000, options.ttl_ms ?? DEFAULT_RECOVERY_LEASE_TTL_MS);
   348	    const configuredMaxRetries = Math.max(1, options.max_retries ?? DEFAULT_RECOVERY_MAX_RETRIES);
   349	
   350	    return this.withRunMutationLock(runId, () => {
   351	      const record = this.getRun(runId);
   352	      if (!record) throw new Error(`GEMINX_RECOVERY_RUN_NOT_FOUND:${runId}`);
   353	      const current = recoveryState(record, configuredMaxRetries);
   354	      if (current.status === "cancelled") throw new Error("GEMINX_RECOVERY_CANCELLED");
   355	      if (current.status === "completed") return record;
   356	
   357	      const activeLease = current.lease && Date.parse(current.lease.expires_at) > nowMs ? current.lease : null;
   358	      if (activeLease) throw new Error("GEMINX_RECOVERY_LEASE_ACTIVE");
   359	
   360	      const maxRetries = current.max_retries || configuredMaxRetries;
   361	      if (current.retry_count >= maxRetries) throw new Error("GEMINX_RECOVERY_RETRY_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED");
   362	
   363	      const generation = current.generation + 1;
   364	      const acquiredAt = isoNow(nowMs);
   365	      const lease: GeminxRecoveryLease = {
   366	        lease_id: randomUUID(),
   367	        owner,
   368	        generation,
   369	        acquired_at: acquiredAt,
   370	        heartbeat_at: acquiredAt,
   371	        expires_at: isoNow(nowMs + ttlMs)
   372	      };
   373	      const next: GeminxRunRecord = {
   374	        ...record,
   375	        updated_at: acquiredAt,
   376	        recovery: {
   377	          ...current,
   378	          status: "leased",
   379	          generation,
   380	          retry_count: current.retry_count + 1,
   381	          max_retries: maxRetries,
   382	          lease,
   383	          cancelled_at: null,
   384	          cancellation_reason: null
   385	        }
   386	      };
   387	      this.saveRun(next);
   388	      return next;
   389	    });
   390	  }
   391	
   392	  heartbeatRecoveryLease(
   393	    runId: string,
   394	    leaseId: string,
   395	    generation: number,
   396	    options: { ttl_ms?: number; now_ms?: number } = {}
   397	  ): GeminxRunRecord {
   398	    const nowMs = options.now_ms ?? Date.now();
   399	    const ttlMs = Math.max(1_000, options.ttl_ms ?? DEFAULT_RECOVERY_LEASE_TTL_MS);
   400	    return this.withRunMutationLock(runId, () => {
   401	      const record = this.getRun(runId);
   402	      if (!record) throw new Error(`GEMINX_RECOVERY_RUN_NOT_FOUND:${runId}`);
   403	      const current = recoveryState(record);
   404	      const lease = assertLeaseOwner(current, leaseId, generation, nowMs);
   405	      const heartbeatAt = isoNow(nowMs);
   406	      const next: GeminxRunRecord = {
   407	        ...record,
   408	        updated_at: heartbeatAt,
   409	        recovery: {
   410	          ...current,
   411	          lease: {
   412	            ...lease,
   413	            heartbeat_at: heartbeatAt,
   414	            expires_at: isoNow(nowMs + ttlMs)
   415	          }
   416	        }
   417	      };
   418	      this.saveRun(next);
   419	      return next;
   420	    });
   421	  }
   422	
   423	  saveRecoveryCheckpoint(
   424	    runId: string,
   425	    leaseId: string,
   426	    generation: number,
   427	    checkpoint: Omit<GeminxRecoveryCheckpoint, "at"> & { at?: string },
   428	    nowMs = Date.now()
   429	  ): GeminxRunRecord {
   430	    return this.withRunMutationLock(runId, () => {
   431	      const record = this.getRun(runId);
   432	      if (!record) throw new Error(`GEMINX_RECOVERY_RUN_NOT_FOUND:${runId}`);
   433	      const current = recoveryState(record);
   434	      assertLeaseOwner(current, leaseId, generation, nowMs);
   435	      if (current.checkpoint && checkpoint.sequence <= current.checkpoint.sequence) {
   436	        throw new Error("GEMINX_RECOVERY_CHECKPOINT_SEQUENCE_STALE");
   437	      }
   438	      const at = checkpoint.at ?? isoNow(nowMs);
   439	      const next: GeminxRunRecord = {
   440	        ...record,
   441	        updated_at: at,
   442	        recovery: {
   443	          ...current,
   444	          checkpoint: {
   445	            sequence: checkpoint.sequence,
   446	            label: checkpoint.label,
   447	            at,
   448	            ...(checkpoint.evidence ? { evidence: [...checkpoint.evidence] } : {})
   449	          }
   450	        }
   451	      };
   452	      this.saveRun(next);
   453	      return next;
   454	    });
   455	  }
   456	
   457	  releaseRecoveryLease(runId: string, leaseId: string, generation: number, nowMs = Date.now()): GeminxRunRecord {
   458	    return this.withRunMutationLock(runId, () => {
   459	      const record = this.getRun(runId);
   460	      if (!record) throw new Error(`GEMINX_RECOVERY_RUN_NOT_FOUND:${runId}`);
   461	      const current = recoveryState(record);
   462	      assertLeaseOwner(current, leaseId, generation, nowMs);
   463	      const updatedAt = isoNow(nowMs);
   464	      const next: GeminxRunRecord = {
   465	        ...record,
   466	        updated_at: updatedAt,
   467	        recovery: { ...current, status: "idle", lease: null }
   468	      };
   469	      this.saveRun(next);
   470	      return next;
   471	    });
   472	  }
   473	
   474	  completeRecovery(
   475	    runId: string,
   476	    leaseId: string,
   477	    generation: number,
   478	    completionReceiptId: string,
   479	    nowMs = Date.now()
   480	  ): GeminxRunRecord {
   481	    return this.withRunMutationLock(runId, () => {
   482	      const record = this.getRun(runId);
   483	      if (!record) throw new Error(`GEMINX_RECOVERY_RUN_NOT_FOUND:${runId}`);
   484	      const current = recoveryState(record);
   485	      if (current.status === "completed") {
   486	        if (current.completion_receipt_id === completionReceiptId) return record;
   487	        throw new Error("GEMINX_RECOVERY_ALREADY_COMPLETED");
   488	      }
   489	      assertLeaseOwner(current, leaseId, generation, nowMs);
   490	      const updatedAt = isoNow(nowMs);
   491	      const next: GeminxRunRecord = {
   492	        ...record,
   493	        updated_at: updatedAt,
   494	        recovery: {
   495	          ...current,
   496	          status: "completed",
   497	          lease: null,
   498	          completion_receipt_id: completionReceiptId
   499	        }
   500	      };
   501	      this.saveRun(next);
   502	      return next;
   503	    });
   504	  }
   505	
   506	  cancelRecovery(runId: string, reason: string, nowMs = Date.now()): GeminxRunRecord {
   507	    return this.withRunMutationLock(runId, () => {
   508	      const record = this.getRun(runId);
   509	      if (!record) throw new Error(`GEMINX_RECOVERY_RUN_NOT_FOUND:${runId}`);
   510	      const current = recoveryState(record);
   511	      if (current.status === "completed") throw new Error("GEMINX_RECOVERY_ALREADY_COMPLETED");
   512	      if (current.status === "cancelled") return record;
   513	      const cancelledAt = isoNow(nowMs);
   514	      const next: GeminxRunRecord = {
   515	        ...record,
   516	        updated_at: cancelledAt,
   517	        recovery: {
   518	          ...current,
   519	          status: "cancelled",
   520	          lease: null,
   521	          cancelled_at: cancelledAt,
   522	          cancellation_reason: reason.trim() || "cancelled"
   523	        }
   524	      };
   525	      this.saveRun(next);
   526	      return next;
   527	    });
   528	  }
   529	
   530	  listRuns(): GeminxRunRecord[] {
   531	    return readdirSync(this.runsRoot)
   532	      .filter((entry) => entry.endsWith(".json"))
   533	      .map((entry) => readIndexedRecord<GeminxRunRecord>(this.runsRoot, entry, "run id"))
   534	      .filter((entry): entry is GeminxRunRecord => entry !== null)
   535	      .sort((left, right) => right.created_at.localeCompare(left.created_at));
   536	  }
   537	
   538	  listReceipts(): GeminxReceiptRecord[] {
   539	    return readdirSync(this.receiptsRoot)
   540	      .filter((entry) => entry.endsWith(".json"))
   541	      .map((entry) => readIndexedRecord<GeminxReceiptRecord>(this.receiptsRoot, entry, "receipt id"))
   542	      .filter((entry): entry is GeminxReceiptRecord => entry !== null)
   543	      .sort((left, right) => right.created_at.localeCompare(left.created_at));
   544	  }
   545	}

    33	  correlation_id: string;

    44	  acknowledged_at?: string;
